    PROCEDURE UpdateAllWebshopSetup@1000000063(CountRecords@1000000000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      IsHandled@1000000001 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateAllWebshopSetup(CountRecords,IsHandled);

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WITH WebshopSetup DO BEGIN
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
            UpdateWebshopSetup;
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterUpdateAllWebshopSetup(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateWebshopSetup@1000000039();
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000000 : Text[1024];
      IsHandled@1000000001 : Boolean;
      ShippingOptionsSetup@1000000002 : Record 5447450;
    BEGIN
      // Webshop-Setup �bertragen

      WebshopSetup.GET;
      GeneralLedgerSetup.GET;
      ShippingOptionsSetup.GET;

      OnBeforeUpdateWebshopSetup(IsHandled,WebshopSetup,GeneralLedgerSetup,ShippingOptionsSetup);

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_setup');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[nas_email_text_module]',WebshopSetup."NAS Error E-Mail Text");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[nas_email_recipient]',WebshopSetup."NAS Error E-Mail Recipient");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[nas_email_recipient_2]',WebshopSetup."NAS Error E-Mail Recipient 2");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[nas_email_sender]',WebshopSetup."NAS Error E-Mail Sender Addr.");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[default_currency_code]',GeneralLedgerSetup."LCY Code");
        ShippingOptionsSetup.CALCFIELDS("Default Priority");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[default_shipping_class_priority]',ShippingOptionsSetup."Default Priority");
        WebshopManagement.AddDTVar(CurrRec,'data[last_datetime_nav_updated]',WebshopSetup."Time Last Full Update");

        IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
          WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
        ELSE
          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);
      END;

      OnAfterUpdateWebshopSetup(IsHandled,WebshopSetup,GeneralLedgerSetup,ShippingOptionsSetup);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllShop@1000000049(CountRecords@1000000000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      Shop@1000000003 : Record 5447301;
      IsHandled@1000000001 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateAllShop(CountRecords,IsHandled);

      // Alle Webshops �bertragen

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WITH Shop DO BEGIN
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
            DeleteAllShop(FALSE);
            IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              UpdateShop(Shop);
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            UNTIL NEXT = 0;
            DeleteAllShop(TRUE);
          END;
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterUpdateAllShop(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateShop@1000000048(ParameterShop@1000000002 : Record 5447301);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000000 : Text[1024];
      AttributeLink@1000000001 : Record 5447321;
      Shop@1000000003 : Record 5447301;
      IStream@1000000004 : InStream;
      EbayAuthTokenBigText@1000000005 : BigText;
      EbayAuthToken@1000000006 : Text;
      FirstLine@1000000007 : Boolean;
      IsHandled@1000000008 : Boolean;
      CouponGroups@1000000009 : Record 5447474;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateShop(IsHandled,ParameterShop);

      // Einzelnen Webshop �bertragen

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WITH Shop DO BEGIN
          GET(ParameterShop.Code);

          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_shop');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[description]',Description);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[shop_typ]',"Shop Type");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[default_language_code]',"Default Language Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[use_items_from_shop_code]',"Use Items from Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[use_categorys_from_shop_code]',"Use Categories from Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[variant_typ]',"Variant Type");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[use_customer_from_shop_code]',"Use Customers from Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[email_sender]',"E-Mail Sender Addr.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[email_order_mail_1_copy]',"Order Conf. E-Mail BCC Addr.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[computop_merchant_id]',"Payment Provider Account ID");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[computop_password]',"Payment Provider Password");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[computop_hmac_key]',"Payment Provider HMAC Key");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[show_invoice_discount]',Shop."Display Inv. Disc. in Checkout");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[small_quantity_charge]',"Small Quantity Charge Amnt.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[small_quantity_charge_limit]',"Small Qty. Charge Threshold");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[online_discount]',"Online Discount %");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[retail_price_typ]',"Retail Price Type");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[cust_price_group_retail_price]',"Cust. Price Group Retail Price");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[cust_disc_group_retail_price]',"Cust. Disc. Group Retail Price");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[base_price_typ]',"Base Price Type");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[cust_price_group_base_price]',"Cust. Price Group Base Price");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[cust_disc_group_base_price]',"Cust. Disc. Group Base Price");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[login_type]',"Login-Type");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[extended_search]',"Use Extended Search");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[item_availability]',"Item Availability Calculation");

          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


          //Auf 2 Teile aufteilen wegen L�nge
          CLEAR(CurrRec);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_shop');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[campain_no]',"Campain No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[prices_including_vat]',"Display Prices Incl. VAT");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[vat_bus_posting_group]',"VAT Bus. Posting Group");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[select_req_delivery_date]',"Select Req. Delivery Date");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[addition_req_delivery_date]',"Addition Req. Delivery Date");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[cross_price_typ]',"Cross Price Type");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[show_filters]',"Attribute Filter Active");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[show_comparison]',"Atrribute Comparison Active");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[show_filter_on_card]',"Attribute Info Active");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[attribute_brand]',"Attribute Brand");
          //Google Merchant---
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[g_ftp_url]',"Google FTP URL");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[g_ftp_user]',"Google FTP User");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[g_ftp_passwd]',"Google FTP Password");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[g_channel_title]',"Google Channel Title");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[g_channel_link]',"Google Channel Link");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[g_channel_desc]',"Google Channel Description");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[g_product_category]',"Google Product Category");
          //Google Merchant+++

          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[max_days_shipment_returnable]',"Return Max. Days Shipment");

          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[forerun_subsc_order_create]',"Forerun Subscr. Order Create");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[forerun_payment_capture]',"Forerun Payment Capture");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[order_options_display]',"Order Options Display");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[order_options_sorting]',"Order Options Sorting");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[inventory_display]',"Inventory Display");

          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

          //Auf 3 Teile aufteilen wegen L�nge
          CLEAR(CurrRec);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_shop');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[paydirekt_api_key]',"Paydirekt Api Key");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[vat_identifier_1]',"VAT Identifier 1");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[vat_identifier_2]',"VAT Identifier 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[vat_identifier_3]',"VAT Identifier 3");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[us_sales_tax_enabled]',"US Sales Tax enabled");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[shipping_group_code]',"Shipping Group Code");

          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


          //Marketplace Daten
          IF "Marketplace Type" = "Marketplace Type"::Amazon THEN BEGIN
            CLEAR(CurrRec);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_marketplace_config_amazon');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',Code);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Default Language Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[environment]','production');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[aws_access_key_id]',"AWS Access Key ID");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[aws_secret_access_key]',"AWS Secret Access Key");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[merchant_id]',"Amazon Merchant ID");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_id]',"Amazon Marketplace ID");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[mws_auth_token]',"Amazon Auth Token");
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);
          END;

          IF "Marketplace Type" = "Marketplace Type"::eBay THEN BEGIN
            CLEAR(CurrRec);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_marketplace_config_ebay');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',Code);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Default Language Code");
            IF "Ebay Environment" = "Ebay Environment"::production THEN
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[environment]','production')
            ELSE
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[environment]','sandbox');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[dev_name]',"Ebay Dev Name");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[app_name]',"Ebay App Name");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[cert_name]',"Ebay Cert Name");
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

            CALCFIELDS("Ebay Auth Token");
            "Ebay Auth Token".CREATEINSTREAM(IStream);
            EbayAuthTokenBigText.READ(IStream);
            EbayAuthTokenBigText.GETSUBTEXT(EbayAuthToken,1);

            FirstLine := TRUE;
            IF STRLEN(EbayAuthToken) > 600 THEN REPEAT
              CLEAR(CurrRec);
              AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_marketplace_config_ebay');
              AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
              AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',Code);
              AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Default Language Code");
              IF NOT FirstLine THEN
                AddVar(CurrRec,'function[add]',TRUE);
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[auth_token]',WebshopManagement.ExplodeByLenght(EbayAuthToken,600));
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);
              FirstLine := FALSE;
            UNTIL EbayAuthToken = '';
          END;

          CLEAR(CurrRec);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_shop');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[paypal_email]',"PayPal Address");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[shop_location_zip]',"Item Loc. Post Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_type]',"Marketplace Type");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[shop_url]',"Webshop URL");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[no_of_items_per_page]',"No. of Items per Page");
          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

          //Newsletter
          CLEAR(CurrRec);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_shop');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[newsletter_account]',"Newsletter Account");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[newsletter_login]',"Newsletter Login");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[newsletter_password]',"Newsletter Password");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[newsletter_group]',"Newsletter Group");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[newsletter_segment]',"Newsletter Segment");
            IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

          // Zugeh�rige Merkmale aktualisieren (Sprach�bergreifend)
          AttributeLink.SETRANGE(Type,AttributeLink.Type::Shop);
          AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code",Code);
          IF AttributeLink.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            UpdateAttributeLink(AttributeLink);
          UNTIL AttributeLink.NEXT = 0;

          CouponGroups.SETFILTER(Code,"Coupon Group Filter");
          IF CouponGroups.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            UpdateShopCouponGroup(CouponGroups,Shop);
          UNTIL CouponGroups.NEXT = 0;

           //Google Merchant Funktions aufruf
          IF NOT GUIALLOWED THEN BEGIN
            IF (WebshopSetup."Update Google Merchant Center") AND ("Google FTP URL" <> '') THEN
              UpdateGoogleMerchant(Code);
          END;
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterUpdateShop(IsHandled,ParameterShop);
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteAllShop@1000000042(ConfirmDelete@1000000001 : Boolean);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000000 : Text[1024];
      IsHandled@1000000002 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeDeleteAllShop(ConfirmDelete,IsHandled);

      // Alle Webshops l�schen
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_shop');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'confirm_delete',ConfirmDelete);
        WebshopManagement.SendRequest('delete',CurrRec);
        IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
          WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('delete',CurrRec,FALSE,TRUE)
        ELSE
          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('delete',CurrRec);
      END;

      OnAfterDeleteAllShop(ConfirmDelete,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllLanguage@1000000056(CountRecords@1000000000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      WebshopLanguage@1000000003 : Record 5447302;
      IsHandled@1000000001 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateAllLanguage(CountRecords,IsHandled);

      // Alle Webshop-Sprachen �bertragen

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WITH WebshopLanguage DO BEGIN
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
            DeleteAllLanguage(FALSE);
            IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              UpdateLanguage(WebshopLanguage);
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            UNTIL NEXT = 0;
            DeleteAllLanguage(TRUE);
          END;
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterUpdateAllLanguage(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

          CLEAR(CurrRec);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_language');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[check_shipping_address]',"Address Validation Active");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[address_doctor_customer_id]',"Address Validation Login");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[address_doctor_password]',"Address Validation Password");
          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

          // Zugeh�rige W�hrungen �bertragen
          IF "Currency Filter" <> '' THEN BEGIN
            Currency.SETFILTER(Code,"Currency Filter");
            DeleteCurrency("Shop Code",Code,FALSE);
            IF Currency.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              UpdateCurrency("Shop Code",Code,Currency);
            UNTIL Currency.NEXT = 0;
            DeleteCurrency("Shop Code",Code,TRUE);
          END;

        END;
      END;

      OnAfterUpdateLanguage(IsHandled,ParameterWebshopLanguage);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckItemPlaceholderImage@1000000071(WebshopLanguage@1000000000 : Record 5447302) Ok : Boolean;
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000004 : Text[1024];
      LogFile@1002 : File;
      LogResponse@1001 : Text;
      GetResponseText@1000 : Text;
      IsHandled@1000000002 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeCheckItemPlaceholderImage(IsHandled,WebshopLanguage);

      // Platzhalter Artikelbild pr�fen

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WITH WebshopLanguage DO BEGIN
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_language');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'field','item_placeholder_image');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
          GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('check_field_content',CurrRec);
          IF GetRec = '' THEN
            Ok := FALSE
          ELSE IF GetRec = '100' THEN
            Ok := TRUE
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
              ERROR(GetRec)
            ELSE BEGIN
              IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
                LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
                 IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                  ERROR(Error001);
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                    ERROR(Error002);
                END;
                LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
                LogFile.CLOSE;
                IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                  MESSAGE(GetRec);
                EXIT(TRUE);
              END ELSE
                ERROR(Error003);
            END;
          END;
          IF "Item Placeholder Image" <> Ok THEN BEGIN
            "Item Placeholder Image" := Ok;
            MODIFY;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterCheckItemPlaceholderImage(IsHandled,WebshopLanguage);
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteItemPlaceholderImage@1000000070(WebshopLanguage@1000000000 : Record 5447302);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      IsHandled@1000000002 : Boolean;

          AddVar(CurrRec,'code',Code);

          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('delete_item_placeholder_image',CurrRec,FALSE,TRUE)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('delete_item_placeholder_image',CurrRec);
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterDeleteItemPlaceholderImage(IsHandled,WebshopLanguage);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllCountry@1000000003(CountRecords@1000000000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      WebshopCountry@1000000003 : Record 5447310;
      IsHandled@1000000001 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateAllCountry(CountRecords,IsHandled);

      // Alle L�ndereinstellungen �bertragen

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WITH WebshopCountry DO BEGIN
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
            DeleteAllCountry(FALSE);
            DeleteAllCustomerTemplate(FALSE);
            IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              UpdateCountry(WebshopCountry);
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            UNTIL NEXT = 0;
            DeleteAllCountry(TRUE);
            DeleteAllCustomerTemplate(TRUE);
          END;
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterUpdateAllCountry(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckTextModule@1000000024(WebshopTextModule@1000000000 : Record 5447309) OK : Boolean;
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      LogFile@1002 : File;
      LogResponse@1001 : Text;
      GetResponseText@1000 : Text;
      IsHandled@1000000003 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeCheckTextModule(IsHandled,WebshopTextModule);

      // Textbaustein auf Inhalt �berpr�fen

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WITH WebshopTextModule DO BEGIN
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_text_module');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'field','content');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
          GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('check_field_content',CurrRec);
          IF GetRec = '' THEN
            OK := FALSE
          ELSE IF GetRec = '100' THEN
            OK := TRUE
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
              ERROR(GetRec)
            ELSE BEGIN
              IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
                LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
                 IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                  ERROR(Error001);
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                    ERROR(Error002);
                END;
                LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
                LogFile.CLOSE;
                IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                  MESSAGE(GetRec);
                EXIT(TRUE);
              END ELSE
                ERROR(Error003);
            END;
          END;

          IF OK <> Content THEN BEGIN
            Content := OK;
            MODIFY(TRUE);
          END;
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterCheckTextModule(IsHandled,WebshopTextModule);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckTextModuleAttachment@1000000136(WebshopTextModule@1000000000 : Record 5447309;AttachmentNo@1000000003 : Integer) OK : Boolean;
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      LogFile@1002 : File;
      LogResponse@1001 : Text;
      GetResponseText@1000 : Text;
      IsHandled@1000000004 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeCheckTextModuleAttachment(IsHandled,WebshopTextModule,AttachmentNo);

      // Textbaustein auf Inhalt der Anh�nge �berpr�fen

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WITH WebshopTextModule DO BEGIN
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_text_module');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'field','attachment_'+FORMAT(AttachmentNo));
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
          GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('check_field_content',CurrRec);
          IF GetRec = '' THEN
            OK := FALSE
          ELSE IF GetRec = '100' THEN
            OK := TRUE
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
              ERROR(GetRec)
            ELSE BEGIN
              IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
                LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
                 IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                  ERROR(Error001);
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                    ERROR(Error002);
                END;
                LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
                LogFile.CLOSE;
                IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                  MESSAGE(GetRec);
                EXIT(TRUE);
              END ELSE
                ERROR(Error003);
            END;
          END;

          IF (AttachmentNo=1) AND (OK <> "Attachment 1") THEN BEGIN
            "Attachment 1" := OK;
            MODIFY(TRUE);
          END ELSE IF (AttachmentNo=2) AND (OK <> "Attachment 2") THEN BEGIN
            "Attachment 2" := OK;
            MODIFY(TRUE);
          END;
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterCheckTextModuleAttachment(IsHandled,WebshopTextModule,AttachmentNo);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckPaymentOption@1000000210(WebshopPaymentOption@1000000000 : Record 5447312) OK : Boolean;
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      LogFile@1002 : File;
      LogResponse@1001 : Text;
      GetResponseText@1000 : Text;
      IsHandled@1000000003 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeCheckPaymentOption(IsHandled,WebshopPaymentOption);

      // PaymentOption Content auf Inhalt �berpr�fen

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WITH WebshopPaymentOption DO BEGIN
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_payment_option');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'field','content');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Language Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[line_no]',"Line No.");
          GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('check_field_content',CurrRec);
          IF GetRec = '' THEN
            OK := FALSE
          ELSE IF GetRec = '100' THEN
            OK := TRUE
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
              ERROR(GetRec)
            ELSE BEGIN
              IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
                LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
                 IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                  ERROR(Error001);
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                    ERROR(Error002);
                END;
                LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
                LogFile.CLOSE;
                IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                  MESSAGE(GetRec);
                EXIT(TRUE);
              END ELSE
                ERROR(Error003);
            END;
          END;

          IF OK <> "Has Description" THEN BEGIN
            "Has Description" := OK;
            MODIFY(TRUE);
          END;
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterCheckPaymentOption(IsHandled,WebshopPaymentOption);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetAndSaveLastSolrUpdateDT@1000000148();
    VAR
      GetRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      ResultText@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      ResultDateTime@1000000003 : DateTime;
      WebshopSetup@1000000000 : Record 5447300;
      IsHandled@1000000004 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeGetAndSaveLastSolrUpdateDT(IsHandled);

      //Letzte Datum-Uhrzeit Solr-Update abholen und speichern

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        ResultText := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_solr_update_datetime',GetRec);
        IF ResultText = '0' THEN
          ResultDateTime := 0DT
        ELSE
          EVALUATE(ResultDateTime,ResultText);
        WebshopSetup.GET;
        WebshopSetup."Last Update Item-Search" := ResultDateTime;
        WebshopSetup.MODIFY(FALSE);
      END;

      OnAfterGetAndSaveLastSolrUpdateDT(IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllShippingOptions@1000000230(CountRecords@1000000002 : Boolean);
    VAR
      ShippingOption@1000000000 : Record 5447453;
      IsHandled@1000000001 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      // Alle Versandarten �bertragen
      OnBeforeUpdateAllShippingOptions(CountRecords,IsHandled);

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WITH ShippingOption DO BEGIN
          SETRANGE("Is Active",TRUE);
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
            DeleteAllShippingOptions(FALSE);
            IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              UpdateShippingOption(ShippingOption);
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            UNTIL NEXT = 0;
            DeleteAllShippingOptions(TRUE);
          END;
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterUpdateAllShippingOptions(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateShippingOption@1000000219(ParameterShippingOption@1000000000 : Record 5447453);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000003 : Text[1024];
      IsHandled@1000000007 : Boolean;
      ShippingOption@1000000001 : Record 5447453;
      ShippingOptionsSetup@1000000002 : Record 5447450;
      ShippingZones@1000000004 : Record 5447454;
      ShippingOptionTranslation@1000000006 : Record 5447456;
      WebshopShippingOptionLink@1000000005 : Record 5447311;
    BEGIN
      // Einzelene Versandart �bertragen
      OnBeforeUpdateShippingOption(IsHandled,ParameterShippingOption);

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        ShippingOptionsSetup.GET;

        WITH ShippingOption DO BEGIN
          GET(ParameterShippingOption."Shipping Group Code",ParameterShippingOption."Line No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_shipping_option');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);

          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shipping_group_code]',"Shipping Group Code");

          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[line_no]',"Line No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[shipping_agent_code]',"Shipping Agent Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[shipping_agent_service_code]',"Shipping Agent Service Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[weight_from]',"Weight from");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[weight_to]',"Weight to");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[amount_from]',"Amount from");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[amount_to]',"Amount to");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[shipping_cost]',"Shipping Cost");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[exemption]',Exemption);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[description]',Description);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sorting]',Sorting);

          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[shipping_class_code]',"Shipping Class Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[valid_from]',"Valid from");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[valid_to]',"Valid to");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[shipping_zone_code]',"Shipping Zone Code");

          WebshopShippingOptionLink.SETRANGE("Shipping Group Code","Shipping Group Code");
          WebshopShippingOptionLink.SETRANGE("Line No.","Line No.");
          IF WebshopShippingOptionLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            UpdateWebshopShippingLink(WebshopShippingOptionLink);
          UNTIL WebshopShippingOptionLink.NEXT = 0;

          ShippingZones.SETRANGE(Code,"Shipping Zone Code");
          IF ShippingZones.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            UpdateShippingZone(ShippingZones);
          UNTIL ShippingZones.NEXT = 0;

          ShippingOptionTranslation.SETRANGE("Shipping Croup Code","Shipping Group Code");
          ShippingOptionTranslation.SETRANGE("Shipping Option Line No.","Line No.");
          IF ShippingOptionTranslation.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            UpdateShippingOptionTranslation(ShippingOptionTranslation);
          UNTIL ShippingOptionTranslation.NEXT = 0;

          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterUpdateShippingOption(IsHandled,ParameterShippingOption);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateWebshopShippingLink@1000000250(ParameterShippingOptionLink@1000000000 : Record 5447311);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000003 : Text[1024];
      WebshopShippingOptionLink@1000000004 : Record 5447311;
      IsHandled@1000000002 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      //OnBeforeUpdateWebshopShippingLink(IsHandled,ParameterShippingOption);

      // Einzelene Versandart �bertragen

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WITH WebshopShippingOptionLink DO BEGIN

          GET(ParameterShippingOptionLink."Shop Code",ParameterShippingOptionLink."Shipping Group Code",ParameterShippingOptionLink."Line No.");

          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shipping_option_link');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shipping_group_code]',"Shipping Group Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[line_no]',"Line No.");

            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

        END;
      END;

    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckShippingOption@1000000209(ShippingOption@1000000000 : Record 5447453) OK : Boolean;
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      LogFile@1002 : File;
      LogResponse@1001 : Text;
      GetResponseText@1000 : Text;
      IsHandled@1000000003 : Boolean;
      WebshopShippingOptionLink@1000000004 : Record 5447311;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeCheckShippingOption(IsHandled,ShippingOption);

      // ShippingOption Content auf Inhalt �berpr�fen

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        WITH ShippingOption DO BEGIN
          WebshopShippingOptionLink.SETRANGE("Shipping Group Code","Shipping Group Code");
          WebshopShippingOptionLink.SETRANGE("Line No.","Line No.");
          IF WebshopShippingOptionLink.FINDFIRST THEN;

          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_shipping_option');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'field','content');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shipping_group_code]',"Shipping Group Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[line_no]',"Line No.");
          GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('check_field_content',CurrRec);
          IF GetRec = '' THEN
            OK := FALSE
          ELSE IF GetRec = '100' THEN
            OK := TRUE
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
              ERROR(GetRec)
            ELSE BEGIN
              IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
                LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
                 IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                  ERROR(Error001);
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                    ERROR(Error002);
                END;
                LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
                LogFile.CLOSE;
                IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                  MESSAGE(GetRec);
                EXIT(TRUE);
              END ELSE
                ERROR(Error003);
            END;
          END;

          IF OK <> "Has Description" THEN BEGIN
            "Has Description" := OK;
            MODIFY(TRUE);
          END;
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterCheckShippingOption(IsHandled,ShippingOption);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllWebshopItem@1000000009(CountRecords@1000000002 : Boolean);
    VAR
      WebshopItem@1000000000 : Record 5447304;
      IsHandled@1000000001 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateAllWebshopItem(CountRecords,IsHandled);

      // Alle Webshop-Artikel �bertragen

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WITH WebshopItem DO BEGIN
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
            IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              IF ("Date Last Updated" < "Date Last Modified") OR ("Date Last Updated" = 0DT) THEN
                UpdateWebshopItem(WebshopItem);
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            UNTIL NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterUpdateAllWebshopItem(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateWebshopItemManually@1000000065(WebshopItem@1000000000 : Record 5447304);
    VAR
      Language@1000000002 : Record 8;
      UnitOfMeasure@1000000003 : Record 204;
      UnitOfMeasureTranslation@1000000010 : Record 5402;
      WebshopVarianType@1000000004 : Record 5447302;
      WebshopItemRecord@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      WebshopItemFile@1000000005 : Record 5447307;
      WebshopItemDescription@1000000006 : Record 5447306;
      WebshopItemLink@1000000007 : Record 5447308;
      Item@1000000008 : Record 27;
      SalesPrice@1000000009 : Record 7002;
      WebshopItemCategory@1000000011 : Record 5447305;
      WebshopCategory@1000000012 : Record 5447303;
      ParamKeys@1000000014 : ARRAY [20] OF Text;
      ParamValues@1000000013 : ARRAY [20] OF Text;
      WebshopItemCustomize@1000000015 : Record 5447348;
      IsHandled@1000000016 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateWebshopItemManually(WebshopItem,IsHandled);

      // Einzelnen Webshop-Artikel mit zugeh�rigen Kategorien �bertragen

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WITH WebshopItem DO BEGIN
          UpdateWebshopItem(WebshopItem);
          WebshopItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item Shop Code","Shop Code");
          WebshopItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item Language Code","Language Code");
          WebshopItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
          IF WebshopItemCategory.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            IF WebshopCategory.GET("Shop Code","Language Code",WebshopItemCategory."Category Line No.") THEN BEGIN
              UpdateCategory(WebshopCategory);
              UpdateWebshopItemCategory(WebshopItemCategory);
            END;
          UNTIL WebshopItemCategory.NEXT = 0;
          WebshopItemCustomize.SETRANGE("Item Shop Code","Shop Code");
          WebshopItemCustomize.SETRANGE("Item Language Code","Language Code");
          WebshopItemCustomize.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
          IF WebshopItemCustomize.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            UpdateItemCustomizations(WebshopItemCustomize);
          UNTIL WebshopItemCustomize.NEXT = 0;
          WebshopMarketplaceManagement.UpdateMarketplaceItem(WebshopItem);
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterUpdateWebshopItemManually(WebshopItem,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateWebshopItem@1000000008(ParameterWebshopItem@1000000000 : Record 5447304);
    VAR
      UnitOfMeasure@1000000003 : Record 204;
      UnitOfMeasureTranslation@1000000010 : Record 5402;
      WebshopItemFile@1000000005 : Record 5447307;
      WebshopItemDescription@1000000006 : Record 5447306;
      WebshopItemLink@1000000007 : Record 5447308;
      ItemCrossReference@1000000002 : Record 5717;
      ItemVariant@1000000004 : Record 5401;
      ItemVariantTranslation@1000000015 : Record 30;
      Item@1000000008 : Record 27;
      SalesPrice@1000000009 : Record 7002;
      Item2@1000000011 : Record 27;
      ItemUnitOfMeasure@1000000012 : Record 5404;
      Shop@1000000013 : Record 5447301;
      Language@1000000014 : Record 5447302;
      TempWebshopItem@1000000016 : Record 5447304;
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      AttributeLink@1000000017 : Record 5447321;
      AttributeTranslation@1000000018 : Record 5447323;
      WebshopItem@1000000019 : Record 5447304;
      Vendor@1000000020 : Record 23;
      Multiplier@1000000021 : Decimal;
      ItemUnitOfMeasure2@1000000022 : Record 5404;
      ItemCondition@1000000023 : Integer;
      WebshopShop@1000000024 : Record 5447301;
      WebshopItemComment@1000000026 : Record 5447317;
      WebshopItemCustomizations@1000000025 : Record 5447348;
      IsHandled@1000000027 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateWebshopItem(IsHandled,ParameterWebshopItem);

      // Einzelnen Webshop-Artikel �bertragen

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WITH WebshopItem DO BEGIN
          GET(ParameterWebshopItem."Shop Code",ParameterWebshopItem."Language Code",ParameterWebshopItem."Item No.");
          IF Item.GET("Item No.") AND Shop.GET("Shop Code") AND Language.GET("Shop Code","Language Code") THEN BEGIN

            // Datens�tze Webshop-Artikel �bertragen
            CALCFIELDS("Parent Item No.");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_item');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Language Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_no]',"Item No.");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[description]',Description);
            IF UnitOfMeasure.GET("Unit of Measure Code") THEN BEGIN
              IF UnitOfMeasureTranslation.GET("Unit of Measure Code",Language.Code) THEN
                AddVar(CurrRec,'data[base_unit_of_measure]',UnitOfMeasureTranslation.Description)
              ELSE
                AddVar(CurrRec,'data[base_unit_of_measure]',UnitOfMeasure.Description);
            END;
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[unit_of_measure_code]',"Unit of Measure Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[variant_type]',"Variant Description");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[active]',"Is Active");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[validity_from]',"Starting Date");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[validity_to]',"Ending Date");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[summary]',Summary);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[main_picture_line_no]',"Main Picture Line No.");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[main_category_line_no]',"Main Category Line No.");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[insufficient_inventory_limit]',"Insufficient Inventory Limit");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[creation_date]',"Creation Date");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[allow_gift_package]',"Allows Gift Packaging");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[is_gift_package]',"Is Gift Package");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[minimum_order_quantity]',"Minimum Order Quantity");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[quantity_packing_unit]',"Quantity Packing Unit");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[order_per_packing_unit]',"Order per Packing Unit only");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[search_query]',"Search Query");

            IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
              WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
            ELSE
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


            // Datensatz wg. L�nge in zwei Teilen senden
            CLEAR(CurrRec);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_item');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Language Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_no]',"Item No.");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[meta_keywords]',"Meta Keywords");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[meta_description]',"Meta Description");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[site_title]',"Site Title");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[item_slug]',"Item Slug");

            IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
              WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
            ELSE
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


            // Datensatz wg. L�nge in drei Teilen senden
            CLEAR(CurrRec);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_item');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Language Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_no]',"Item No.");

            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[parent_item_no]',"Parent Item No.");

            // Datens�tze aus NAV-Artikel �bertragen
            Item.CALCFIELDS("Qty. on Purch. Order");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[quantity_on_purchase_order]',Item."Qty. on Purch. Order");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[discount_group]',Item."Item Disc. Group");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[allow_invoice_discount]',Item."Allow Invoice Disc.");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[vendor_no]',Item."Vendor No.");
            IF Vendor.GET(Item."Vendor No.") THEN
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[vendor_name]',Vendor.Name);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[price_includes_vat]',Item."Price Includes VAT");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[vat_prod_posting_group]',Item."VAT Prod. Posting Group");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[nav_base_unit_code]',Item."Base Unit of Measure");
            IF ItemUnitOfMeasure.GET("Item No.","Unit of Measure Code") THEN BEGIN
              IF ItemUnitOfMeasure.Weight <> 0 THEN
                AddVar(CurrRec,'data[weight]',ItemUnitOfMeasure.Weight)
              ELSE
                AddVar(CurrRec,'data[weight]',Item."Gross Weight");
              IF ItemUnitOfMeasure.Width <> 0 THEN
                AddVar(CurrRec,'data[width]',ItemUnitOfMeasure.Width);
              IF ItemUnitOfMeasure.Height <> 0 THEN
                AddVar(CurrRec,'data[height]',ItemUnitOfMeasure.Height);
              IF ItemUnitOfMeasure.Length <> 0 THEN
                AddVar(CurrRec,'data[length]',ItemUnitOfMeasure.Length);
              IF ItemUnitOfMeasure.Cubage <> 0 THEN
                AddVar(CurrRec,'data[volume]',ItemUnitOfMeasure.Cubage);
              IF ItemUnitOfMeasure2.GET("Item No.",Item."Base Unit of Measure") THEN
                AddVar(CurrRec,'data[multiplier]',
                        (ItemUnitOfMeasure."Qty. per Unit of Measure"/ItemUnitOfMeasure2."Qty. per Unit of Measure"))
              ELSE
                AddVar(CurrRec,'data[multiplier]','1');
            END;
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[net_weight]',Item."Net Weight");

            IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
              WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
            ELSE
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

            // Funktionen aus Webshop-Artikel �bertragen
            CLEAR(CurrRec);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_item');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Language Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_no]',"Item No.");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[retail_price]',WebshopRetailPrice);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[base_price]',WebshopBasePrice);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[inventory]',WebshopInventory(''));
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[order_ranking]',WebshopOrderRanking);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[customization_price]',WebshopCustomizePrice);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[customizable]',Customizability);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[shipping_class_code]',Item."Shipping Class Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[always_available]',"Item Availability Calculation");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[g_product_category]',"Google Product Category");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[is_greeting_card]',"Is Greeting Card");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[is_greeting_card]',"Is Greeting Card");

            IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
              WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
            ELSE
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

            // Marketplace
            WebshopShop.GET("Shop Code");
            CLEAR(CurrRec);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_item');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Language Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_no]',"Item No.");
            CASE "Ebay Item Condition" OF
              "Ebay Item Condition"::"1000": ItemCondition := 1000;
              "Ebay Item Condition"::"1500": ItemCondition := 1500;
              "Ebay Item Condition"::"1750": ItemCondition := 1750;
              "Ebay Item Condition"::"2000": ItemCondition := 2000;
              "Ebay Item Condition"::"2500": ItemCondition := 2500;
              "Ebay Item Condition"::"3000": ItemCondition := 3000;
              "Ebay Item Condition"::"4000": ItemCondition := 4000;
              "Ebay Item Condition"::"5000": ItemCondition := 5000;
              "Ebay Item Condition"::"6000": ItemCondition := 6000;
              "Ebay Item Condition"::"7000": ItemCondition := 7000;
            END;

            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[item_condition]',ItemCondition);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[item_location_zip]',WebshopShop."Item Loc. Post Code");
            IF Shop."Marketplace Type" = Shop."Marketplace Type"::eBay THEN BEGIN
              IF "Marketplace Ebay Item Template" <> '' THEN
                AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_ebay_item_template]',"Marketplace Ebay Item Template")
              ELSE
                AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_ebay_item_template]',Language."Marketplace Ebay Item Template")
            END;

            IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
              WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
            ELSE
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

            UpdateWebshopItemMarketplace(WebshopItem,TRUE,TRUE,TRUE,TRUE);

            // Aktualisierungsdatum setzten
            "Last Updated Inventory" := WebshopInventory('');
            "Date Last Updated" := CURRENTDATETIME;
            MODIFY(FALSE);

            // Zugeh�rige Bilder, Videos und Dateien aktualisieren
            WebshopItemFile.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
            WebshopItemFile.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
            WebshopItemFile.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
            IF WebshopItemFile.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              UpdateWebshopItemFile(WebshopItemFile);
            UNTIL WebshopItemFile.NEXT = 0;

            // Zugeh�rige Beschreibung aktualisieren
            WebshopItemDescription.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
            WebshopItemDescription.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
            WebshopItemDescription.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
            WebshopItemDescription.SETRANGE(Type,WebshopItemDescription.Type::Description);
            IF WebshopItemDescription.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              UpdateWebshopItemDescription(WebshopItemDescription);
            UNTIL WebshopItemDescription.NEXT = 0;

            // Zugeh�rige Kommentare aktualisieren
            WebshopItemComment.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
            WebshopItemComment.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
            WebshopItemComment.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
            IF WebshopItemComment.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              UpdateWebshopItemComment(WebshopItemComment);
            UNTIL WebshopItemComment.NEXT = 0;

            // Nur aktualisieren wenn in den letzten 30 Sekunden keine gleiche Artikelnr. im gleichen Shop �bertragen wurde
            TempWebshopItem.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
            TempWebshopItem.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
            TempWebshopItem.SETFILTER("Date Last Updated",'>%1',CREATEDATETIME(TODAY,DT2TIME("Date Last Updated")-30000));
            IF TempWebshopItem.COUNT <= 1 THEN BEGIN

              // Zugeh�rige Verkn�pfungen aktualisieren (Sprach�bergreifend)
              WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE(Type);
              WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
              WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
              IF WebshopItemLink.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
                UpdateWebshopItemLink(WebshopItemLink);
              UNTIL WebshopItemLink.NEXT = 0;

              // Zugeh�rige Merkmale aktualisieren (Sprach�bergreifend)
              AttributeLink.SETRANGE(Type,AttributeLink.Type::Item);
              AttributeLink.SETRANGE("No.","Item No.");
              IF AttributeLink.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
                UpdateAttributeLink(AttributeLink);
                //Zugeh�rige �bersetzungen �bertragen
                AttributeTranslation.SETRANGE(Type,AttributeTranslation.Type::"Attribute Link");
                AttributeTranslation.SETRANGE("Attribute Link No.",AttributeLink."No.");
                IF AttributeTranslation.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
                  UpdateAttributeTranslation(AttributeTranslation);
                UNTIL AttributeTranslation.NEXT = 0;
              UNTIL AttributeLink.NEXT = 0;

            END;

            // Nur aktualisieren wenn in den letzten 30 Sekunden keine gleiche Artikelnr. �bertragen wurde
            TempWebshopItem.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
            TempWebshopItem.SETRANGE("Shop Code");
            TempWebshopItem.SETFILTER("Date Last Updated",'>%1',CREATEDATETIME(TODAY,DT2TIME("Date Last Updated")-30000));
            IF TempWebshopItem.COUNT <= 1 THEN BEGIN

              // Zugeh�rige Referenzen aktualisieren (Shop- und Sprach�bergreifend)
              ItemCrossReference.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
              ItemCrossReference.SETFILTER("Cross-Reference Type",'<>%1',ItemCrossReference."Cross-Reference Type"::Vendor);
              DeleteItemCrossReference("Item No.",FALSE);
              IF ItemCrossReference.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
                UpdateItemCrossReference(ItemCrossReference);
              UNTIL ItemCrossReference.NEXT = 0;
              DeleteItemCrossReference("Item No.",TRUE);

              // Zugeh�rige Artikelvarianten aktualisieren (Shop- und Sprach�bergreifend)
              WebshopSetup.GET;
              IF WebshopSetup."Update Item Variants" THEN BEGIN
                ItemVariant.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
                ItemVariant.SETRANGE("Is Webshop Active",TRUE);
                DeleteItemVariant("Item No.",FALSE);
                IF ItemVariant.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
                  UpdateItemVariant(ItemVariant);
                UNTIL ItemVariant.NEXT = 0;
                DeleteItemVariant("Item No.",TRUE);
              END;

              // Zugeh�rige Varianten�bersetzungen aktualisieren (Shop- und Sprach�bergreifend)
              ItemVariantTranslation.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
              ItemVariantTranslation.SETFILTER("Variant Code",'<>%1','');
              DeleteItemVariantTranslation("Item No.",FALSE);
              IF ItemVariantTranslation.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
                UpdateItemVariantTranslation(ItemVariantTranslation);
              UNTIL ItemVariantTranslation.NEXT = 0;
              DeleteItemVariantTranslation("Item No.",TRUE);

              // Zugeh�rige Preise aktualisieren (Shop- und Sprach�bergreifend)
              UpdateSalesPrice("Item No.");

            END;

            //Artikelindividualisierung
            WebshopItemCustomizations.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
            WebshopItemCustomizations.SETRANGE("Item Shop Code","Shop Code");
            WebshopItemCustomizations.SETRANGE("Item Language Code","Language Code");
            DeleteItemCustomization("Shop Code","Language Code","Item No.",FALSE);
            IF WebshopItemCustomizations.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              UpdateItemCustomizations(WebshopItemCustomizations);
            UNTIL WebshopItemCustomizations.NEXT = 0;
            DeleteItemCustomization("Shop Code","Language Code","Item No.",TRUE);

            COMMIT;

          END;
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterUpdateWebshopItem(IsHandled,ParameterWebshopItem);
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteWebshopItem@1000000020(WebshopItem@1000000000 : Record 5447304);
    VAR
      Language@1000000002 : Record 8;
      UnitOfMeasure@1000000003 : Record 204;
      WebshopVarianType@1000000004 : Record 5447302;
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      WebshopItemFile@1000000005 : Record 5447307;
      WebshopItemDescription@1000000006 : Record 5447306;
      WebshopItemLink@1000000007 : Record 5447308;
      TempWebshopItem@1000000008 : Record 5447304;
      WebshopItemCategory@1000000009 : Record 5447305;
      IsHandled@1000000010 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeDeleteWebshopItem(IsHandled,WebshopItem);

      // Einzelnen Webshop-Artikel l�schen

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WITH WebshopItem DO BEGIN
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_item');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[language_code]',"Language Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[item_no]',"Item No.");
          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('instant_delete',CurrRec,FALSE,TRUE)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('instant_delete',CurrRec);


          // Zugeh�rige Kategorien l�schen
          WebshopItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item Shop Code","Shop Code");
          WebshopItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item Language Code","Language Code");
          WebshopItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
          WebshopItemCategory.SetInWebshopItemDelete(TRUE);
          IF WebshopItemCategory.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            WebshopItemCategory.DELETE(TRUE);
          UNTIL WebshopItemCategory.NEXT = 0;

          // Zugeh�rige Bilder, Videos und Dateien l�schen
          WebshopItemFile.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
          WebshopItemFile.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
          WebshopItemFile.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
          IF WebshopItemFile.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            WebshopItemFile.DELETE(TRUE);
          UNTIL WebshopItemFile.NEXT = 0;

          // Zugeh�rige Beschreibung l�schen
          WebshopItemDescription.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
          WebshopItemDescription.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
          WebshopItemDescription.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
          IF WebshopItemDescription.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            WebshopItemDescription.DELETE(TRUE);
          UNTIL WebshopItemDescription.NEXT = 0;

          // Nur l�schen wenn kein anderer Artikel zu Artikelnr. und Shopcode vorhanden ist
          TempWebshopItem.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
          TempWebshopItem.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
          IF TempWebshopItem.COUNT <= 1 THEN BEGIN

            // Zugeh�rige Verkn�pfungen l�schen (wenn kein anderer Webshopartikel zu Artikelnummer vorhanden)
            WebshopItemLink.SetDeleteItem(TRUE);
            WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
            WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
            IF WebshopItemLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
              WebshopItemLink.DELETE(TRUE);
            UNTIL WebshopItemLink.NEXT = 0;

          END;

          // Nur l�schen wenn kein anderer Artikel zu Artikelnr. vorhanden ist
          TempWebshopItem.SETRANGE("Shop Code");
          TempWebshopItem.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
          IF TempWebshopItem.COUNT <= 1 THEN BEGIN

            // Zugeh�rige Referenzen l�schen
            DeleteItemCrossReference("Item No.",FALSE);
            DeleteItemCrossReference("Item No.",TRUE);

            // Zugeh�rige Varianten l�schen
            DeleteItemVariant("Item No.",FALSE);
            DeleteItemVariant("Item No.",TRUE);

            // Zugeh�rige Varianten�bersetzungen l�schen
            DeleteItemVariantTranslation("Item No.",FALSE);
            DeleteItemVariantTranslation("Item No.",TRUE);

            // Zugeh�rige Preise l�schen (wenn kein anderer Webshopartikel zu Artikelnummer vorhanden)
            DeleteSalesPricePerItem(FALSE,"Item No.");
            DeleteSalesPricePerItem(TRUE,"Item No.");

          END;

        END;
      END;

      OnAfterDeleteWebshopItem(IsHandled,WebshopItem);
    END;

    PROCEDURE RenameWebshopItem@1000000102(ItemNo@1000000000 : Code[20];NewItemNo@1000000010 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      IsHandled@1000000002 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeRenameWebshopItem(IsHandled,ItemNo,NewItemNo);

      // Webshop-Artikel umbenennen

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        AddVar(CurrRec,'item_no',ItemNo);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'new_item_no',NewItemNo);

          WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('rename_item',CurrRec,FALSE,TRUE)
        ELSE
          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('rename_item',CurrRec);
      END;

      OnAfterRenameWebshopItem(IsHandled,ItemNo,NewItemNo);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateWebshopItemMarketplace@1000000228(ParaWebshopItem@1000000000 : Record 5447304;ParaUpdate@1000000005 : Boolean;ParaUpdateImages@1000000006 : Boolean;ParaUpdatePrice@1000000007 : Boolean;ParaUpdateInventory@1000000008 : Boolean);
    VAR
      WebshopShop@1000000001 : Record 5447301;
      WebshopLanguage@1000000002 : Record 5447302;
      WebshopItem@1000000003 : Record 5447304;
      CurrRec@1000000004 : Text[1024];
      IsHandled@1000000009 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateWebshopItemMarketplace(IsHandled,ParaWebshopItem,ParaUpdate,ParaUpdateImages,ParaUpdatePrice,ParaUpdateInventory);

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WebshopSetup.GET;

        WebshopItem.GET(ParaWebshopItem."Shop Code",ParaWebshopItem."Language Code",ParaWebshopItem."Item No.");
        WebshopShop.SETRANGE(WebshopShop."Use Items from Shop Code",WebshopItem."Shop Code");
        WebshopShop.SETRANGE(WebshopShop."Shop Type",WebshopShop."Shop Type"::marketplace);
        IF WebshopShop.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
          WebshopLanguage.SETRANGE(WebshopLanguage."Shop Code",WebshopShop.Code);
          IF WebshopLanguage.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            CLEAR(CurrRec);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_marketplace_item_update');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',WebshopShop.Code);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',WebshopLanguage.Code);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_shop_code]',WebshopItem."Shop Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_language_code]',WebshopItem."Language Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_no]',WebshopItem."Item No.");

            WebshopManagement.AddDTVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_last_nav_update]',CURRENTDATETIME);
            IF ParaUpdate THEN
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_update]',TRUE);
            IF ParaUpdateImages THEN
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_update_images]',TRUE);
            IF ParaUpdatePrice THEN
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_update_price]',TRUE);
            IF ParaUpdateInventory THEN
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_update_inventory]',TRUE);

            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[active]',WebshopItem."Active on Marketplace");

            IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
              WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
            ELSE
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

          UNTIL WebshopLanguage.NEXT = 0;
        UNTIL WebshopShop.NEXT = 0;

        CLEAR(WebshopShop);
        WebshopShop.RESET;
        IF WebshopShop.GET(WebshopItem."Shop Code") THEN BEGIN
          IF WebshopShop."Shop Type" = WebshopShop."Shop Type"::marketplace THEN BEGIN
            WebshopLanguage.SETRANGE(WebshopLanguage."Shop Code",WebshopShop.Code);
            IF WebshopLanguage.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              CLEAR(CurrRec);
              AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_marketplace_item_update');
              AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
              AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',WebshopShop.Code);
              AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',WebshopLanguage.Code);
              AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_shop_code]',WebshopItem."Shop Code");
              AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_language_code]',WebshopItem."Language Code");
              AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_no]',WebshopItem."Item No.");

              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_update]',TRUE);
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_update_images]',TRUE);
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_update_price]',TRUE);
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_update_inventory]',TRUE);
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[active]',WebshopItem."Active on Marketplace");

              IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
                WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
              ELSE
                WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

            UNTIL WebshopLanguage.NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterUpdateWebshopItemMarketplace(IsHandled,ParaWebshopItem,ParaUpdate,ParaUpdateImages,ParaUpdatePrice,ParaUpdateInventory);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllWebshopItemInventory@1000000045(CountRecords@1000000002 : Boolean);
    VAR
      WebshopItem@1000000000 : Record 5447304;
      Text001@1000000001 : TextConst 'DEU="Lagerbestand ";ENU=Inventory';
      IsHandled@1000000003 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateAllWebshopItemInventory(CountRecords,IsHandled);

      // Lagerbestand f�r alle Webshop-Artikel �bertragen

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WITH WebshopItem DO BEGIN
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(Text001 + TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(Text001 + TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
            IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              IF WebshopInventory('') <> "Last Updated Inventory" THEN
                UpdateWebshopItemInventory(WebshopItem);
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            UNTIL NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterUpdateAllWebshopItemInventory(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckWebshopItemDescription@1000000017(WebshopItemDescription@1000000000 : Record 5447306) OK : Boolean;
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      LogFile@1002 : File;
      LogResponse@1001 : Text;
      GetResponseText@1000 : Text;
      IsHandled@1000000003 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeCheckWebshopItemDescription(IsHandled,WebshopItemDescription);

      // Inhalt von Webshop Artikelbeschreibung pr�fen

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WITH WebshopItemDescription DO BEGIN
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_item_description');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'field','content');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Language Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_no]',"Item No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[line_no]',"Line No.");
          GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('check_field_content',CurrRec);
          IF GetRec = '' THEN
            OK := FALSE
          ELSE IF GetRec = '100' THEN
            OK := TRUE
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
              ERROR(GetRec)
            ELSE BEGIN
              IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
                LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
                 IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                  ERROR(Error001);
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                    ERROR(Error002);
                END;
                LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
                LogFile.CLOSE;
                IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                  MESSAGE(GetRec);
                EXIT(TRUE);
              END ELSE
                ERROR(Error003);
            END;
          END;

          IF OK <> Content THEN BEGIN
            Content := OK;
            MODIFY(TRUE);
          END;
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterCheckWebshopItemDescription(IsHandled,WebshopItemDescription);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateWebshopItemFile@1000000015(ParameterWebshopItemFile@1000000000 : Record 5447307);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      WebshopItemFile@1000000002 : Record 5447307;
      IsHandled@1000000003 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateWebshopItemFile(IsHandled,ParameterWebshopItemFile);

      // Einzelne Webshop Artikeldatei �bertragen

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WITH WebshopItemFile DO BEGIN
          GET(ParameterWebshopItemFile."Shop Code",ParameterWebshopItemFile."Language Code",
              ParameterWebshopItemFile."Item No.",ParameterWebshopItemFile.Type,ParameterWebshopItemFile."Variant Code",ParameterWebshopItemFile."Line No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_item_file');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Language Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_no]',"Item No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[type]',Type);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[line_no]',"Line No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[description]',Description);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[customization]',"Customization Image");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[all_language_codes]',"All Language Codes");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[youtube_video_id]',"Youtube Video ID");
          IF (NOT File) AND ("Temp Filename" <> '') THEN BEGIN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[filename]',"Temp Filename");
          END;
          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

        END;
      END;

      OnAfterUpdateWebshopItemFile(IsHandled,ParameterWebshopItemFile);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckWebshopItemFile@1000000018(WebshopItemFile@1000000000 : Record 5447307) Ok : Boolean;
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      LogFile@1002 : File;
      LogResponse@1001 : Text;
      GetResponseText@1000 : Text;
      IsHandled@1000000003 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeCheckWebshopItemFile(IsHandled,WebshopItemFile);

      // Einzelne Webshop Artikeldatei pr�fen
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WebshopSetup.GET;
        WITH WebshopItemFile DO BEGIN
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_item_file');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'field','filename');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Language Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_no]',"Item No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[type]',Type);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[line_no]',"Line No.");
          GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('check_field_content',CurrRec);
          IF GetRec = '' THEN
            Ok := FALSE
          ELSE IF GetRec = '100' THEN
            Ok := TRUE
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
              ERROR(GetRec)
            ELSE BEGIN
              IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
                LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                IF NOT EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
                 IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                  ERROR(Error001);
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                    ERROR(Error002);
                END;
                LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
                LogFile.CLOSE;
                IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                  MESSAGE(GetRec);
                EXIT(TRUE);
              END ELSE
                ERROR(Error003);
            END;
          END;

          IF Ok <> File THEN BEGIN
            File := Ok;
            MODIFY(TRUE);
          END;
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterCheckWebshopItemFile(IsHandled,WebshopItemFile);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllItemVariantInventory@1000000079(CountRecords@1000000002 : Boolean);
    VAR
      WebshopItem@1000000000 : Record 5447304;
      Text001@1000000001 : TextConst 'DEU="Lagerbestand ";ENU=Inventory';
      ItemVariant@1000000003 : Record 5401;
      IsHandled@1000000004 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateAllItemVariantInventory(CountRecords,IsHandled);

      // Lagerbestand f�r alle Webshop-Artikelvarianten �bertragen

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WITH ItemVariant DO BEGIN
          ItemVariant.SETRANGE("Is Webshop Active",TRUE);
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(Text001 + TABLECAPTION,ItemVariant.COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(Text001 + TABLECAPTION,ItemVariant.COUNT);
            IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
              IF WebshopItem.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                IF WebshopItem.WebshopInventory(Code) <> "Last Updated Webshop Inventory" THEN
                  UpdateItemVariant(ItemVariant);
                END;
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            UNTIL NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterUpdateAllItemVariantInventory(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateItemVariant@1000000098(ParameterItemVariant@1000000000 : Record 5401);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      WebshopItem@1000000002 : Record 5447304;
      VariantInventory@1000000003 : Decimal;
      ItemVariant@1000000004 : Record 5401;
      IsHandled@1000000005 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateItemVariant(IsHandled,ParameterItemVariant);

      // Einzelne Artikelvariante �bertragen

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WITH ItemVariant DO BEGIN
          GET(ParameterItemVariant."Item No.",ParameterItemVariant.Code);
          // Lagerbestand ausrechnen
          WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
          IF WebshopItem.FINDFIRST THEN
            VariantInventory := WebshopItem.WebshopInventory(Code);

          // Daten �bertragen
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_item_variant');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_no]',"Item No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[description]',Description);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[description_2]',"Description 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[inventory]',VariantInventory);
          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


          // �bertragenen Lagerbestand speichern
          "Last Updated Webshop Inventory" := VariantInventory;
          MODIFY(FALSE);
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterUpdateItemVariant(IsHandled,ParameterItemVariant);
    END;
