    PROCEDURE GetAllItemComments@1000000117(CountRecords@1000000000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      Text001@1000000001 : TextConst 'DEU=Bestellungen abrufen;ENU=Fetch orders';
      NoOfOrder@1000000002 : Integer;
      CurrRec@1000000003 : Text[1024];
      LogFile@1002 : File;
      LogResponse@1001 : Text;
      GetResponseText@1000 : Text;
      IsHandled@1000000004 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeGetAllItemComments(CountRecords,IsHandled);

      // Alle neuen Kommentare abrufen

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WebshopSetup.GET;
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_item_comments');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[update_insert]',TRUE);
        CLEAR(GetResponseText);
        GetResponseText := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_no_of_records',CurrRec);
        IF EVALUATE(NoOfOrder,WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_no_of_records',CurrRec)) THEN BEGIN
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(Text001,NoOfOrder);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(Text001,NoOfOrder);
            GetItemComment;
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
            ERROR(GetResponseText)
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
              LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
              LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
              IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
               IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                ERROR(Error001);
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                  ERROR(Error002);
              END;
              LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
              LogFile.WRITE(GetResponseText);
              LogFile.CLOSE;
              IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                MESSAGE(GetResponseText);
            END ELSE
              ERROR(Error003);
          END;
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterGetAllItemComments(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetItemComment@1000000116();
    VAR
      Shop@1000000012 : Record 5447301;
      Language@1000000011 : Record 5447302;
      Text001@1000000076 : TextConst 'DEU=Lastschriftkonto: %1;ENU=Direct debit account: %1';
      Text002@1000000080 : TextConst 'DEU=Gutscheinrabatt Code %1;ENU=Coupon Discount Code %1';
      WebshopUpdateManagement@1118900000 : Codeunit 5447301;
      ExtendedTextLine@1000000082 : Record 280;
      Delimiter@1000000000 : Text[5];
      WebshopItemComment@1000000001 : Record 5447317;
      NextWebshopItemComment@1000000002 : Boolean;
      CurrRec@1000000003 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000004 : Text[1024];
      ItemCommentId@1000000005 : Integer;
      ShopCode@1000000006 : Code[10];
      LanguageCode@1000000007 : Code[10];
      ItemNo@1000000008 : Code[10];
      Rating@1000000009 : Integer;
      City@1000000010 : Text[100];
      Header@1000000013 : Text[100];
      UserEmail@1000000014 : Text[100];
      CreationDate@1000000015 : DateTime;
      Item@1000000016 : Record 27;
      SetRec@1000000017 : Text[1024];
      LineNo@1118900001 : Integer;
      WebshopItemComment2@1118900002 : Record 5447317;
      UserName@1118900003 : Text[100];
      LogFile@1001 : File;
      LogResponse@1000 : Text;
      Released@1000000025 : Boolean;
      CommentValue@1000000024 : Text;
      DateValue@1000000023 : Text;
      intDay@1000000022 : Integer;
      intMonth@1000000021 : Integer;
      IntYear@1000000020 : Integer;
      ValueDate@1000000019 : Date;
      ValueTime@1000000018 : Time;
      IsHandled@1000000026 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeGetItemComment(IsHandled);

      // Neuen Kommentar abrufen

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        Delimiter := '-;-';

        WITH WebshopItemComment DO REPEAT
          NextWebshopItemComment := FALSE;

          CLEAR(CurrRec);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_item_comments');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'delimiter',Delimiter);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'fields','id,shop_code,language_code,item_no,rating,city,header,email,' +
                                  'name,creation_date,released,comment');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[update_insert]',TRUE);
          GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_content',CurrRec);
          IF STRPOS(GetRec,Delimiter) <> 0 THEN BEGIN
            EVALUATE(ItemCommentId,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(ShopCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(LanguageCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(ItemNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(Rating,(WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter)));
            EVALUATE(City,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(Header,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(UserEmail,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(UserName,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(DateValue,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(intDay,COPYSTR(DateValue,1,2));
            EVALUATE(intMonth,COPYSTR(DateValue,4,2));
            EVALUATE(IntYear,COPYSTR(DateValue,7,4));

            ValueDate := DMY2DATE(intDay,intMonth,IntYear);
            EVALUATE(ValueTime,COPYSTR(DateValue,12,8));

            CreationDate := CREATEDATETIME(ValueDate,ValueTime);

            EVALUATE(Released,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(CommentValue,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));

            IF (ShopCode <> '') AND (LanguageCode <> '') AND (ItemNo <>'') THEN BEGIN
              IF Item.GET(ItemNo) THEN BEGIN

                // Zeilennr. generieren
                WebshopItemComment2.SETRANGE("Shop Code",ShopCode);
                WebshopItemComment2.SETRANGE("Language Code",LanguageCode);
                WebshopItemComment2.SETRANGE("Item No.",Item."No.");
                IF WebshopItemComment2.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
                  LineNo := WebshopItemComment2."Line No." + 10000;
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  LineNo := 10000;
                END;

                INIT();
                VALIDATE("Shop Code",ShopCode);
                VALIDATE("Language Code",LanguageCode);
                VALIDATE("Item No.",Item."No.");
                VALIDATE("Review Title",Header);
                VALIDATE("Line No.", LineNo);
                VALIDATE("Review Date", CreationDate);
                VALIDATE("Review Rating", Rating * 2000); //For ExtendedDataType Ratio
                VALIDATE("Review User Name", UserName);
                VALIDATE("Review User City", City);
                VALIDATE("Review Text", CommentValue);
                VALIDATE("Is Review Released", Released);
                VALIDATE("Review User E-Mail", UserEmail);
                VALIDATE( "Date Last Updated", CURRENTDATETIME);
                VALIDATE( "Date Last Modified", CURRENTDATETIME);
                INSERT(TRUE);

                // Kommentar als �bertragen markieren
                CLEAR(SetRec);
                AddVar(SetRec,'table','shop_item_comments');
                AddVar(SetRec,'primary[id]',ItemCommentId);
                AddVar(SetRec,'data[update_insert]',FALSE);
                AddVar(SetRec,'data[line_no]',LineNo);
                IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
                  WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',SetRec,FALSE,TRUE)
                ELSE
                  WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',SetRec);

                NextWebshopItemComment := TRUE;
                WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
                COMMIT;
              END;
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF GetRec<>'0' THEN BEGIN
              IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
                ERROR(GetRec)
              ELSE BEGIN
                IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
                  LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                  LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                  IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
                   IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                    ERROR(Error001);
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                      ERROR(Error002);
                  END;
                  LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                  LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
                  LogFile.CLOSE;
                  IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                    MESSAGE(GetRec);
                END ELSE
                  ERROR(Error003);
              END;
            END;
          END;
        UNTIL NOT NextWebshopItemComment;
      END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllAttribute@1000000105(CountRecords@1000000000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      Attribute@1000000001 : Record 5447320;
      IsHandled@1000000002 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateAllAttribute(CountRecords,IsHandled);

      // Alle Merkmale �bertragen

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WITH Attribute DO BEGIN
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
            DeleteAllAttribute(FALSE);
            IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              UpdateAttribute(Attribute);
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            UNTIL NEXT = 0;
            DeleteAllAttribute(TRUE);
          END;
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterUpdateAllAttribute(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAttribute@1000000106(ParameterAttribute@1000000000 : Record 5447320);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      AttributeTranslation@1000000002 : Record 5447323;
      Attribute@1000000003 : Record 5447320;
      WebshopShop@1000000004 : Record 5447301;
      IsHandled@1000000005 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateAttribute(IsHandled,ParameterAttribute);

      // Einzelnes Merkmal �bertragen

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WITH Attribute DO BEGIN
          GET(ParameterAttribute.Code);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_attribute');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[attribute_type]',"Attribute Type");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[description]',Description);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[display_type]',"Display Type");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[multiple_choices]',"Multiple Choice");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[data_type]',"Data Type");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[navision_value]',"Existing Data");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[filter_all]',"Use for Filtering");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[filter_category]',"Use for Item-Comparison");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[show_on_card]',"Use for Item-Info");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[use_for_comparison]',"Use for Item-Comparison");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[auto_link]',"Auto-Link existing Data");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[map_to_attribute]',"Map-to Attribute Code");
          IF "Marketplace Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
            IF WebshopShop.GET("Marketplace Code") THEN
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_type]',WebshopShop."Marketplace Type");
          END ELSE
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_type]',0);

          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[show_in_header]',"Show in Header");

          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


          //Zugeh�rige �bersetzungen �bertragen
          AttributeTranslation.SETRANGE(Type,AttributeTranslation.Type::Attribute);
          AttributeTranslation.SETRANGE("Attribute Code",Attribute.Code);
          IF AttributeTranslation.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            UpdateAttributeTranslation(AttributeTranslation);
          UNTIL AttributeTranslation.NEXT = 0;
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterUpdateAttribute(IsHandled,ParameterAttribute);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckFilterImage@1000000114(AttributeOption@1000000000 : Record 5447322) Ok : Boolean;
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      LogFile@1001 : File;
      LogResponse@1000 : Text;
      IsHandled@1000000003 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeCheckFilterImage(IsHandled,AttributeOption);

      // Einzelne Webshop Artikeldatei pr�fen

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN
        WITH AttributeOption DO BEGIN
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_attribute_option');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'field','icon');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[attribute_code]',"Attribute Code");
          GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('check_field_content',CurrRec);
          IF GetRec = '' THEN
            Ok := FALSE
          ELSE IF GetRec = '100' THEN
            Ok := TRUE
          ELSE
            IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
              ERROR(GetRec)
            ELSE BEGIN
              IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
                LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
                 IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                  ERROR(Error001);
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                    ERROR(Error002);
                END;
                LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
                LogFile.CLOSE;
                IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                  MESSAGE(GetRec);
              END ELSE
                ERROR(Error003);
            END;
          IF Ok <> Icon THEN BEGIN
            Icon := Ok;
            MODIFY(TRUE);
          END;
        END;
      END;

      OnAfterCheckFilterImage(IsHandled,AttributeOption);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateCategory@1000000022(ParameterWebshopCategory@1000000000 : Record 5447303);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      TempWebshopCategory@1000000005 : Record 5447303;
      NextCategory@1000000006 : Boolean;
      ParentLineNo@1000000003 : Integer;
      RootLineNo@1000000007 : Integer;
      Sorting@1000000004 : Integer;
      AttributeLink@1000000002 : Record 5447321;
      AttributeTranslation@1000000008 : Record 5447323;
      WebshopCategory@1000000009 : Record 5447303;
      IsHandled@1000000010 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateCategory(IsHandled,ParameterWebshopCategory);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Einzelne Webshop Kategorie �bertragen

        WITH WebshopCategory DO BEGIN
          GET(ParameterWebshopCategory."Shop Code",ParameterWebshopCategory."Language Code",ParameterWebshopCategory."Line No.");
          IF Name <> '' THEN BEGIN

            TempWebshopCategory.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
            TempWebshopCategory.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");

            // Feld Sorting f�r Web ermitteln
            Sorting := 1;
            TempWebshopCategory.SETFILTER(Level,'<=%1',Level);
            TempWebshopCategory.SETFILTER("Line No.",'<%1',"Line No.");
            IF TempWebshopCategory.FINDLAST THEN REPEAT
              IF TempWebshopCategory.Level <> WebshopCategory.Level THEN
                NextCategory := TRUE;
              IF NOT NextCategory THEN
                Sorting := Sorting + 1;
            UNTIL TempWebshopCategory.NEXT(-1) = 0;

            // Feld "parent_line_no" f�r Web ermitteln
            ParentLineNo := 0;
            TempWebshopCategory.SETFILTER(Level,'<%1',Level);
            TempWebshopCategory.SETFILTER("Line No.",'<%1',"Line No.");
            IF TempWebshopCategory.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
              ParentLineNo := TempWebshopCategory."Line No.";
            END;

            // Feld "root_line_no" f�r Web ermitteln
            RootLineNo := 0;
            IF Level > 1 THEN BEGIN
              TempWebshopCategory.SETRANGE(Level,1);
              TempWebshopCategory.SETFILTER("Line No.",'<%1',"Line No.");
              IF TempWebshopCategory.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
                RootLineNo := TempWebshopCategory."Line No.";
              END;
            END;

            // Kagegorie Teil 1 �bertragen
            AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_category');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Language Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[line_no]',"Line No.");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sorting]',Sorting);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[root_line_no]',RootLineNo);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[parent_line_no]',ParentLineNo);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[name]',Name);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[level]',Level);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[code]',"Search Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[show_random_items]',0);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[no_of_random_items]',0);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[show_random_items]',0);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[no_of_random_items]',0);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[show_campain_items]',"Show Campain Items");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[no_of_campain_items]',"No of Campain Items");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[user_sorting]',"User Sorting");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sort_items]',"Sort Items");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[show_all_items]',"Show All Items");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[show_sub_categorys]',"Show Sub Categorys");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[active]',Active);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[validity_from]',"Validity from");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[validity_to]',"Validity to");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[promotion_active]',"Promtion Active");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[promotion_validity_from]',"Promtion Validity from");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[promotion_validity_to]',"Promtion Validity to");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[promotion_label]',"Promotion Label");
            IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
              WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
            ELSE
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

            CLEAR(CurrRec);

             // Kagegorie Teil 2 �bertragen
            AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_category');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Language Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[line_no]',"Line No.");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[meta_keywords]',"Meta Keywords");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[meta_description]',"Meta Description");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[site_titel]',"Site Title");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[search_query]',"Search Query");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[url]',GetCategoryURL);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[g_product_category]',"Google Product Category");
            IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
              WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
            ELSE
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


            // Zugeh�rige Merkmale aktualisieren
            AttributeLink.SETRANGE(Type,AttributeLink.Type::Category);
            AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Category Line No.","Line No.");
            AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
            AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
            IF AttributeLink.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              UpdateAttributeLink(AttributeLink);
              //Zugeh�rige �bersetzungen �bertragen
              AttributeTranslation.SETRANGE(Type,AttributeTranslation.Type::"Attribute Link");
              AttributeTranslation.SETRANGE("Attribute Link No.",AttributeLink."No.");
              IF AttributeTranslation.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
                UpdateAttributeTranslation(AttributeTranslation);
              UNTIL AttributeTranslation.NEXT = 0;
            UNTIL AttributeLink.NEXT = 0;

            //Als �bertragen kennzeichnen
            "Last Date Updated" := CURRENTDATETIME;
            MODIFY(FALSE);

          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateCategory(IsHandled,ParameterWebshopCategory);
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteCategory@1000000076(WebshopCategory@1000000001 : Record 5447303);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000000 : Text[1024];
      IsHandled@1000000002 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeDeleteCategory(IsHandled,WebshopCategory);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Einzelne Webshop Kategorie l�schen

        WITH WebshopCategory DO BEGIN

          // Kategoriebild l�schen wenn vorhanden
          IF "Category Picture" THEN
            DeleteCategoryFiles(WebshopCategory,0);

          // Kategoriesymbol l�schen wenn vorhanden
          IF "Category Icon" THEN
            DeleteCategoryFiles(WebshopCategory,1);

          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_category');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[language_code]',"Language Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[line_no]',"Line No.");
          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('instant_delete',CurrRec,FALSE,FALSE)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('instant_delete',CurrRec);
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterDeleteCategory(IsHandled,WebshopCategory);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckCategoryFiles@1000000054(WebshopCategory@1000000000 : Record 5447303;Type@1000000002 : 'Picture,Icon,Description,Promotion Description,Description 2,Excerpt') Ok : Boolean;
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000004 : Text[1024];
      Field@1000000003 : Text[30];
      LogFile@1001 : File;
      LogResponse@1000 : Text;
      IsHandled@1000000005 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeCheckCategoryFiles(IsHandled,WebshopCategory,Type);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Dateien an Webshop-Kategorie pr�fen

        WITH WebshopCategory DO BEGIN
          CASE Type OF
            Type::Picture: Field := 'category_picture';
            Type::Icon: Field := 'category_icon';
            Type::Description: Field := 'category_description';
            Type::"Promotion Description": Field := 'promotion_description';
            Type::"Description 2": Field := 'category_description_2';
            Type::Excerpt: Field := 'category_description_excerpt';
          END;
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_category');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'field',Field);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Language Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[line_no]',"Line No.");
          GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('check_field_content',CurrRec);
          IF GetRec = '' THEN
            Ok := FALSE
          ELSE IF GetRec = '100' THEN
            Ok := TRUE
          ELSE
            IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
              ERROR(GetRec)
            ELSE BEGIN
              IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
                LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
                 IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                  ERROR(Error001);
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                    ERROR(Error002);
                END;
                LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
                LogFile.CLOSE;
                IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                  MESSAGE(GetRec);
              END ELSE
                ERROR(Error003);
            END;
          GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");
          CASE Type OF
            Type::Picture: "Category Picture" := Ok;
            Type::Icon: "Category Icon" := Ok;
            Type::Description: "Category Description" := Ok;
            Type::"Promotion Description": "Promotion Description" := Ok;
            Type::"Description 2": "Category Description 2" := Ok;
            Type::Excerpt: "Category Excerpt" := Ok;
          END;
          MODIFY;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterCheckCategoryFiles(IsHandled,WebshopCategory,Type);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateWebshopItemCategory@1000000010(ParameterWebshopItemCategory@1000000000 : Record 5447305);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      WebshopItem@1000000003 : Record 5447304;
      WebshopCategory@1000000004 : Record 5447303;
      WebshopItemCategory@1000000002 : Record 5447305;
      IsHandled@1000000005 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateWebshopItemCategory(IsHandled,ParameterWebshopItemCategory);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Einzelne Webshopartikel-Kategorie �bertragen

        WITH WebshopItemCategory DO BEGIN

          GET(ParameterWebshopItemCategory."Item Shop Code",ParameterWebshopItemCategory."Item Language Code",
              ParameterWebshopItemCategory."Item No.",ParameterWebshopItemCategory."Category Shop Code",
              ParameterWebshopItemCategory."Category Language Code",ParameterWebshopItemCategory."Category Line No.");

          IF WebshopItem.GET("Item Shop Code","Item Language Code","Item No.") AND
             WebshopCategory.GET("Category Shop Code","Category Language Code","Category Line No.") AND
             (WebshopItem."Date Last Updated" <> 0DT) THEN BEGIN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_item_has_category');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Item Shop Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Item Language Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_no]',"Item No.");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[category_shop_code]',"Category Shop Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[category_language_code]',"Category Language Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[category_line_no]',"Category Line No.");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sorting]',Sorting);
            IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
              WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
            ELSE
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

            //Als �bertragen kennzeichnen
            "Date Last Updated" := CURRENTDATETIME;
            MODIFY(FALSE);

          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateWebshopItemCategory(IsHandled,ParameterWebshopItemCategory);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateCategoryManually@1000000163(ParameterWebshopCategory@1000000000 : Record 5447303);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      TempWebshopCategory@1000000005 : Record 5447303;
      NextCategory@1000000006 : Boolean;
      ParentLineNo@1000000003 : Integer;
      RootLineNo@1000000007 : Integer;
      Sorting@1000000004 : Integer;
      AttributeLink@1000000002 : Record 5447321;
      AttributeTranslation@1000000008 : Record 5447323;
      WebshopCategory@1000000009 : Record 5447303;
      WebshopItemCategory@1000000010 : Record 5447305;
      IsHandled@1000000011 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateCategoryManually(ParameterWebshopCategory,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Einzelne Webshop Kategorie �bertragen

        WITH WebshopCategory DO BEGIN
          GET(ParameterWebshopCategory."Shop Code",ParameterWebshopCategory."Language Code",ParameterWebshopCategory."Line No.");
          IF Name <> '' THEN BEGIN

            TempWebshopCategory.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
            TempWebshopCategory.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");

            // Feld Sorting f�r Web ermitteln
            Sorting := 1;
            TempWebshopCategory.SETFILTER(Level,'<=%1',Level);
            TempWebshopCategory.SETFILTER("Line No.",'<%1',"Line No.");
            IF TempWebshopCategory.FINDLAST THEN REPEAT
              IF TempWebshopCategory.Level <> WebshopCategory.Level THEN
                NextCategory := TRUE;
              IF NOT NextCategory THEN
                Sorting := Sorting + 1;
            UNTIL TempWebshopCategory.NEXT(-1) = 0;

            // Feld "parent_line_no" f�r Web ermitteln
            ParentLineNo := 0;
            TempWebshopCategory.SETFILTER(Level,'<%1',Level);
            TempWebshopCategory.SETFILTER("Line No.",'<%1',"Line No.");
            IF TempWebshopCategory.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
              ParentLineNo := TempWebshopCategory."Line No.";
            END;

            // Feld "root_line_no" f�r Web ermitteln
            RootLineNo := 0;
            IF Level > 1 THEN BEGIN
              TempWebshopCategory.SETRANGE(Level,1);
              TempWebshopCategory.SETFILTER("Line No.",'<%1',"Line No.");
              IF TempWebshopCategory.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
                RootLineNo := TempWebshopCategory."Line No.";
              END;
            END;

            // Kagegorie Teil 1 �bertragen
            AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_category');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllCustomer@1000000033(CountRecords@1000000002 : Boolean);
    VAR
      Customer@1000000000 : Record 18;
      IsHandled@1000000001 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateAllCustomer(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Alle Debitoren �bertragen

        WITH Customer DO BEGIN
          WebshopSetup.GET;
          IF NOT WebshopSetup."Update All Customers" THEN
            SETFILTER("Date Webshop Login Sent",'<>%1',0DT);
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
            IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              IF ("Date Last Updated (Webshop)" < "Date Last Modified (Webshop)") OR ("Date Last Updated (Webshop)" = 0DT) THEN
                UpdateCustomer(Customer);
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            UNTIL NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateAllCustomer(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateCustomer@1000000031(ParameterCustomer@1000000000 : Record 18);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000003 : Text[1024];
      CustomerActive@1000000004 : Boolean;
      BillToCustomer@1000000006 : Record 18;
      CustInvoiceDisc@1000000007 : Record 19;
      PaymentTerms@1000000008 : Record 3;
      Customer@1000000001 : Record 18;
      ShipToAddress@1000000002 : Record 222;
      tmpShipToAddress@1000000005 : TEMPORARY Record 222;
      IsHandled@1000000009 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateCustomer(IsHandled,ParameterCustomer);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Debitor �bertragen

        WITH Customer DO BEGIN
          GET(ParameterCustomer."No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_customer');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[customer_no]',"No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[shop_code]',"Webshop Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[language_code]',"Webshop Language Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[name]',Name);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[name_2]',"Name 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[address]',Address);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[address_2]',"Address 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[post_code]',"Post Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[city]',City);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[country]',"Country/Region Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[phone_no]',"Phone No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[fax_no]',"Fax No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[email]',"E-Mail");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[homepage]',"Home Page");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[salesperson_code]',"Salesperson Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[currency_code]',"Currency Code");

          IF "Bill-to Customer No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
            BillToCustomer.GET("Bill-to Customer No.");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[customer_price_group]',BillToCustomer."Customer Price Group");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[customer_disc_group]',BillToCustomer."Customer Disc. Group");
            IF PaymentTerms.GET(BillToCustomer."Payment Terms Code") THEN
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[payment_terms]',PaymentTerms.Description);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[customer_price_group]',"Customer Price Group");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[customer_disc_group]',"Customer Disc. Group");
            IF PaymentTerms.GET("Payment Terms Code") THEN
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[payment_terms]',PaymentTerms.Description);
          END;
          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


          CLEAR(CurrRec);

          //FK 17.01.2013 - Aufgesplittet wegen m�glicher �berschreitung von 1024 Zeichen
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_customer');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[customer_no]',"No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_customer_no]',BillToCustomer."No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_name]',BillToCustomer.Name);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_name_2]',BillToCustomer."Name 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_address]',BillToCustomer.Address);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_address_2]',BillToCustomer."Address 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_post_code]',BillToCustomer."Post Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_city]',BillToCustomer.City);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_country]',BillToCustomer."Country/Region Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[invoice_disc_code]',"Invoice Disc. Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[active]',("Has Webshop Login" AND NOT (Blocked = Blocked::All)));

          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


          "Date Last Updated (Webshop)" := CURRENTDATETIME;

          //�bertragen der Liefer-An Adressen inkl. Std. Debitor (Rechnungs)adresse
          tmpShipToAddress.DELETEALL(FALSE);
          CLEAR(tmpShipToAddress);
          ShipToAddress.SETRANGE("Customer No.","No.");
          IF ShipToAddress.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            tmpShipToAddress.INIT;
            tmpShipToAddress.TRANSFERFIELDS(ShipToAddress);
            tmpShipToAddress.INSERT(FALSE);
          UNTIL ShipToAddress.NEXT = 0;

          IF "Bill-to Customer No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
            BillToCustomer.GET("Bill-to Customer No.");
            tmpShipToAddress.INIT;
            tmpShipToAddress."Customer No." := "No.";
            tmpShipToAddress.Code := 'DEFAULT';
            tmpShipToAddress.Name := BillToCustomer.Name;
            tmpShipToAddress."Name 2" := BillToCustomer."Name 2";
            tmpShipToAddress.Address := BillToCustomer.Address;
            tmpShipToAddress."Address 2" := BillToCustomer."Address 2";
            tmpShipToAddress.City := BillToCustomer.City;
            tmpShipToAddress."Post Code" := BillToCustomer."Post Code";
            tmpShipToAddress."Country/Region Code" := BillToCustomer."Country/Region Code";
            tmpShipToAddress.INSERT(FALSE);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            tmpShipToAddress.INIT;
            tmpShipToAddress."Customer No." := "No.";
            tmpShipToAddress.Code := 'DEFAULT';
            tmpShipToAddress.Name := Name;
            tmpShipToAddress."Name 2" := "Name 2";
            tmpShipToAddress.Address := Address;
            tmpShipToAddress."Address 2" := "Address 2";
            tmpShipToAddress.City := City;
            tmpShipToAddress."Post Code" := "Post Code";
            tmpShipToAddress."Country/Region Code" := "Country/Region Code";
            tmpShipToAddress.INSERT(FALSE);
          END;

          tmpShipToAddress.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE);
          REPEAT
            UpdateShipToAddress(tmpShipToAddress);
          UNTIL tmpShipToAddress.NEXT = 0;

          //Webshop Debitor Verkn�pfung
          UpdateWebshopCustomerLink(Customer);


          MODIFY(FALSE);
          COMMIT;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateCustomer(IsHandled,ParameterCustomer);
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteCustomer@1000000030(Customer@1000000000 : Record 18);

    END;

    PROCEDURE SendCustomerLogin@1000000029(Customer@1000000002 : Record 18);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      ShipToAddress@1000000000 : Record 222;
      Language@1000000003 : Record 5447302;
      BillToCustomer@1000000004 : Record 18;
      tmpShipToAddress@1000000005 : TEMPORARY Record 222;
      IsHandled@1000000006 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeSendCustomerLogin(IsHandled,Customer);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Zugangsdaten an Debitor versenden

        WITH Customer DO BEGIN

          // Aktiv setzten
          "Has Webshop Login" := TRUE;
          MODIFY(FALSE);
          Language.GET("Webshop Shop Code","Webshop Language Code");
          Language.TESTFIELD("E-Mail Login");

          // Debitor aktualisieren
          UpdateCustomer(Customer);
          GET("No.");
          // Einmaliges �bertragen der Liefer-An Adressen inkl. Std. Debitor (Rechnungs)adresse
          tmpShipToAddress.DELETEALL(FALSE);
          CLEAR(tmpShipToAddress);
          ShipToAddress.SETRANGE("Customer No.","No.");
          IF ShipToAddress.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            tmpShipToAddress.INIT;
            tmpShipToAddress.TRANSFERFIELDS(ShipToAddress);
            tmpShipToAddress.INSERT(FALSE);
          UNTIL ShipToAddress.NEXT = 0;

          IF Customer."Bill-to Customer No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
            BillToCustomer.GET("Bill-to Customer No.");
            tmpShipToAddress.INIT;
            tmpShipToAddress."Customer No." := Customer."No.";
            tmpShipToAddress.Code := 'DEFAULT';
            tmpShipToAddress.Name := BillToCustomer.Name;
            tmpShipToAddress."Name 2" := BillToCustomer."Name 2";
            tmpShipToAddress.Address := BillToCustomer.Address;
            tmpShipToAddress."Address 2" := BillToCustomer."Address 2";
            tmpShipToAddress.City := BillToCustomer.City;
            tmpShipToAddress."Post Code" := BillToCustomer."Post Code";
            tmpShipToAddress.INSERT(FALSE);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            tmpShipToAddress.INIT;
            tmpShipToAddress."Customer No." := Customer."No.";
            tmpShipToAddress.Code := 'DEFAULT';
            tmpShipToAddress.Name := Customer.Name;
            tmpShipToAddress."Name 2" := Customer."Name 2";
            tmpShipToAddress.Address := Customer.Address;
            tmpShipToAddress."Address 2" := Customer."Address 2";
            tmpShipToAddress.City := Customer.City;
            tmpShipToAddress."Post Code" := Customer."Post Code";
            tmpShipToAddress.INSERT(FALSE);
          END;

          tmpShipToAddress.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE);
          REPEAT
            UpdateShipToAddress(tmpShipToAddress);
          UNTIL tmpShipToAddress.NEXT = 0;



          // E-Mail mit Kennwort �bertragen
          AddVar(CurrRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'customer_no',"No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'email',"Webshop Login E-Mail Addr.");
          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('send_customer_login',CurrRec,FALSE,TRUE)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('send_customer_login',CurrRec);


          // Aktualisierungsdatum setzten
          "Date Webshop Login Sent" := CURRENTDATETIME;
          MODIFY(FALSE);
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterSendCustomerLogin(IsHandled,Customer);
    END;

    PROCEDURE OpenCustomerLogin@1000000032(Customer@1000000000 : Record 18) SessionID : Text[200];
    VAR
      Shop@1000000002 : Record 5447301;
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      Text001@1000000003 : TextConst 'DEU=F�r Debitor %1 ist kein Webzugang angelegt;ENU=Customer  %1 has no access to webshop';
      IsHandled@1000000004 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeOpenCustomerLogin(IsHandled,Customer);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Hauptuser eines Kunden anmelden

        Shop.GET(Customer."Webshop Shop Code");
        Shop.TESTFIELD("Webshop URL");
        IF Customer."Date Webshop Login Sent" = 0DT THEN
          ERROR(Text001,Customer."No.");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'customer_no',Customer."No.");
        SessionID := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('open_customer_login',CurrRec);
        HYPERLINK(Shop."Webshop URL" + '/?sid=' + SessionID);

      END;
      OnAfterOpenCustomerLogin(IsHandled,Customer);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetAllNewCustomer@1118900000(CountRecords@1118900000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      NoOfRegistration@1118900001 : Integer;
      CurrRec@1118900002 : Text[1024];
      Text001@1118900003 : TextConst 'DEU=Registrierungen;ENU=Registrations';
      LogFile@1001 : File;
      LogResponse@1000 : Text;
      GetResponseText@1002 : Text;
      IsHandled@1000000000 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeGetAllNewCustomer(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Alle neuen Kundenregistrierungen abrufen

        //AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_customer');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'customer_no','TEMP_%');
        CLEAR(GetResponseText);
        GetResponseText := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_no_of_new_customer',CurrRec);
        IF EVALUATE(NoOfRegistration,GetResponseText) THEN BEGIN
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(Text001,NoOfRegistration);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(Text001,NoOfRegistration);
            GetNewCustomer;
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
            ERROR(GetResponseText)
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
              LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
              LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
              IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
               IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                ERROR(Error001);
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                  ERROR(Error002);
              END;
              LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
              LogFile.WRITE(GetResponseText);
              LogFile.CLOSE;
              IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                MESSAGE(GetResponseText);
            END ELSE
              ERROR(Error003);
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterGetAllNewCustomer(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetNewCustomer@1118900003();
    VAR
      CurrRec@1118900000 : Text[1024];
      Customer@1118900001 : Record 18;
      NextCustomer@1118900002 : Boolean;
      Delimiter@1118900003 : Text[30];
      GetRec@1118900004 : Text[1024];
      LocId@1118900005 : Integer;
      LocShopCode@1118900006 : Code[10];
      LocLanguageCode@1118900007 : Code[10];
      LocCustomerNo@1118900008 : Code[10];
      LocName@1118900009 : Text[30];
      LocAddress@1118900010 : Text[50];
      LocPostCode@1118900011 : Text[30];
      LocCity@1118900012 : Text[30];
      LocCountry@1118900013 : Code[10];
      LocPhoneNo@1118900014 : Text[50];
      LocEmail@1118900015 : Text[80];
      LocCurrencyCode@1118900016 : Code[10];
      SetRec@1118900017 : Text[1024];
      LogFile@1000 : File;
      IsHandled@1000000000 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeGetNewCustomer(IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        //Neue Registrierung abholen und NAV-Kundennummer �bertragen

        Delimiter := '-;-';

        WITH Customer DO REPEAT
          NextCustomer := FALSE;

          CLEAR(CurrRec);
          //AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_customer');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'delimiter',Delimiter);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'customer_no','TEMP_%');
          GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_new_customer',CurrRec);
          IF STRPOS(GetRec,Delimiter) <> 0 THEN BEGIN
            //Felder in Variablen speichern und Debitor anlegen
            EVALUATE(LocId,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(LocShopCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(LocLanguageCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(LocCustomerNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(LocName,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(LocAddress,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(LocPostCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(LocCity,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(LocCountry,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(LocPhoneNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(LocEmail,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(LocCurrencyCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            Customer.INIT;
            Customer.Name := LocName;
            Customer.Address := LocAddress;
            Customer.City := LocCity;
            Customer."Phone No." := LocPhoneNo;
            Customer."Currency Code" := LocCurrencyCode;
            Customer."E-Mail" := LocEmail;
            Customer."Post Code" := LocPostCode;
            Customer."Country/Region Code" := LocCountry;
            Customer.INSERT(TRUE);
            COMMIT;

            // Neue Kundennummer �bertragen in shop_customer und shop_user
            CLEAR(SetRec);
            AddVar(SetRec,'table','shop_customer');
            AddVar(SetRec,'primary[id]',LocId);
            AddVar(SetRec,'data[customer_no]',Customer."No.");
            AddVar(SetRec,'data[bill_to_customer_no]',Customer."No.");
            IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
              WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',SetRec,FALSE,TRUE)
            ELSE
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',SetRec);

            CLEAR(SetRec);
            AddVar(SetRec,'table','shop_user');
            AddVar(SetRec,'primary[customer_no]',LocCustomerNo);
            AddVar(SetRec,'data[customer_no]',Customer."No.");
            IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
              WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',SetRec,FALSE,TRUE)
            ELSE
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',SetRec);

            NextCustomer := TRUE;
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            COMMIT;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF GetRec<>'0' THEN BEGIN
              IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
                ERROR(GetRec)
              ELSE BEGIN
                IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
                  LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                  LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                  IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
                   IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                    ERROR(Error001);
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                      ERROR(Error002);
                  END;
                  LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                  LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
                  LogFile.CLOSE;
                  IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                    MESSAGE(GetRec);
                END ELSE
                  ERROR(Error003);
              END;
            END;
          END;
        UNTIL NOT NextCustomer;

      END;
      OnAfterGetNewCustomer(IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllSalesperson@1000000036(CountRecords@1000000000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      Salesperson@1000000003 : Record 13;
      IsHandled@1000000001 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateAllSalesperson(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Alle Verk�ufer �bertragen

        WITH Salesperson DO BEGIN
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
            DeleteAllSalesperson(FALSE);
            IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              UpdateSalesperson(Salesperson);
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            UNTIL NEXT = 0;
            DeleteAllSalesperson(TRUE);
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateAllSalesperson(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateSalesPrice@1000000046(ItemNo@1000000001 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      SalesPrice@1000000002 : Record 7002;
      SalesLineDiscount@1000000003 : Record 7004;
      Item@1000000004 : Record 27;
      ItemDiscountGroup@1000000005 : Record 341;
      CurrRec@1000000000 : Text[1024];
      IsHandled@1000000006 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateSalesPrice(IsHandled,ItemNo);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Preise/Rabatte pro Artikel �bertragen

        Item.GET(ItemNo);
        DeleteSalesPricePerItem(FALSE,ItemNo);

        // Verkaufspreise �bertragen
        WITH SalesPrice DO BEGIN
          SETRANGE("Item No.",ItemNo);
          IF FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            CLEAR(CurrRec);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_sales_price');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[type]',0);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_no]',ItemNo);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[sales_type]',"Sales Type");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[sales_code]',"Sales Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[starting_date]',"Starting Date");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[currency_code]',"Currency Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[variant_code]',"Variant Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[unit_of_measure_code]',"Unit of Measure Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[minimum_quantity]',"Minimum Quantity");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ending_date]',"Ending Date");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[unit_price]',"Unit Price");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[allow_line_disc]',"Allow Line Disc.");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[allow_invoice_disc]',"Allow Invoice Disc.");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[price_includes_vat]',"Price Includes VAT");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[vat_bus_posting_group]',"VAT Bus. Posting Gr. (Price)");
            IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
              WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
            ELSE
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

          UNTIL NEXT = 0;
        END;

        // Zeilenrabatte �bertragen
        WITH SalesLineDiscount DO BEGIN
          SETRANGE(Type,SalesLineDiscount.Type::Item);
          SETRANGE(Code,ItemNo);
          IF FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            CLEAR(CurrRec);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_sales_price');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[type]',1);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_no]',ItemNo);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[sales_type]',"Sales Type");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[sales_code]',"Sales Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[starting_date]',"Starting Date");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[currency_code]',"Currency Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[variant_code]',"Variant Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[unit_of_measure_code]',"Unit of Measure Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[minimum_quantity]',"Minimum Quantity");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ending_date]',"Ending Date");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[line_discount]',"Line Discount %");
            IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
              WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
            ELSE
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

          UNTIL NEXT = 0;
        END;

        DeleteSalesPricePerItem(TRUE,ItemNo);

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateSalesPrice(IsHandled,ItemNo);
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteSalesPricePerItem@1000000044(ConfirmDelete@1000000002 : Boolean;ItemNo@1000000000 : Code[20]);

    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllSalesLineDiscount@1000000064(CountRecords@1000000001 : Boolean);
    VAR
      SalesLineDiscount@1000000000 : Record 7004;
      IsHandled@1000000002 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateAllSalesLineDiscount(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Alle Artikelrabattgruppenzeilen �bertragen

        WITH SalesLineDiscount DO BEGIN
          SETRANGE(Type,SalesLineDiscount.Type::"Item Disc. Group");
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
            IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              IF ("Date Last Updated (Webshop)" < "Date Last Modified (Webshop)") OR ("Date Last Updated (Webshop)" = 0DT) THEN
                UpdateSalesLineDiscount(SalesLineDiscount);
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            UNTIL NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateAllSalesLineDiscount(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllInvoiceDiscount@1000000069(CountRecords@1000000001 : Boolean);

      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Alle Rechnungsrabatte �bertragen

        WITH CustInvoiceDisc DO BEGIN
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);

              UpdateInvoiceDiscount(CustInvoiceDisc);
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            UNTIL NEXT = 0;
            DeleteAllInvoiceDiscount(FALSE);
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateAllInvoiceDiscount(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllSalesInvoiceHeader@1140007(CountRecords@1000000002 : Boolean) Ok : Boolean;
    VAR
      SalesInvoiceHeader@1000000000 : Record 112;
      IsHandled@1000000001 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateAllSalesInvoiceHeader(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Alle Geb. Verkaufsrechnungen �bertragen

        WITH SalesInvoiceHeader DO BEGIN
          SETCURRENTKEY("Posting Date","Date Last Updated (Webshop)");
          SETRANGE("Posting Date",CALCDATE('<-CY-1Y>'),WORKDATE);
          SETRANGE("Date Last Updated (Webshop)",0DT);
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
            IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              UpdateSalesInvoiceHeader(SalesInvoiceHeader);
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            UNTIL NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateAllSalesInvoiceHeader(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateSalesInvoiceHeader@1140012(ParameterSalesInvoiceHeader@1140000 : Record 112);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1140001 : Text[1024];
      SalesInvoiceLine@1140002 : Record 113;
      ShipmentMethod@1000000005 : Record 10;
      PaymentTerms@1000000004 : Record 3;
      ShipmentMethodTranslation@1000000003 : Record 463;
      PaymentTermTranslation@1000000002 : Record 462;
      ShipmentMethodDescription@1000000001 : Text[50];
      PaymentTermsDescription@1000000000 : Text[50];
      SalesInvoiceHeader@1000000006 : Record 112;
      IsHandled@1000000007 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateSalesInvoiceHeader(IsHandled,ParameterSalesInvoiceHeader);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Einzelne Verkaufsrechnung �bertragen

        WebshopSetup.GET;
        WITH SalesInvoiceHeader DO BEGIN
          GET(ParameterSalesInvoiceHeader."No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_sales_invoice_header');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[no]',"No.");

          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[posting_date]',"Posting Date");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[order_no]',"Order No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[webshop_order_no]',"Webshop Order No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[your_reference]',"Your Reference");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_customer_no]',"Sell-to Customer No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_customer_no]',"Bill-to Customer No.");

          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_name]',"Sell-to Customer Name");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_name_2]',"Sell-to Customer Name 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_address]',"Sell-to Address");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_address_2]',"Sell-to Address 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_post_code]',"Sell-to Post Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_city]',"Sell-to City");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_country]',"Sell-to Country/Region Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_contact]',"Sell-to Contact");

          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_name]',"Bill-to Name");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_name_2]',"Bill-to Name 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_address]',"Bill-to Address");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_address_2]',"Bill-to Address 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_post_code]',"Bill-to Post Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_city]',"Bill-to City");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_country]',"Bill-to Country/Region Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_contact]',"Bill-to Contact");

          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


          CLEAR(CurrRec);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_sales_invoice_header');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[no]',"No.");

          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_name]',"Ship-to Name");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_name_2]',"Ship-to Name 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_address]',"Ship-to Address");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_address_2]',"Ship-to Address 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_post_code]',"Ship-to Post Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_city]',"Ship-to City");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_country]',"Ship-to Country/Region Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_contact]',"Ship-to Contact");

          CALCFIELDS(Amount,"Amount Including VAT");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[amount]',Amount);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[amount_including_vat]',"Amount Including VAT");

          // Lieferbedingung als Text
          IF ShipmentMethod.GET("Shipment Method Code") THEN BEGIN
            IF ShipmentMethodTranslation.GET("Shipment Method Code","Language Code") THEN
              ShipmentMethodDescription := ShipmentMethodTranslation.Description
            ELSE
              ShipmentMethodDescription := ShipmentMethod.Description;
          END;
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[shipment_method]',ShipmentMethodDescription);

          // Zahlungsbedingung als Text
          IF PaymentTerms.GET("Payment Terms Code") THEN BEGIN
            IF PaymentTermTranslation.GET("Payment Terms Code","Language Code") THEN
              PaymentTermsDescription := PaymentTermTranslation.Description
            ELSE
              PaymentTermsDescription := PaymentTerms.Description;
          END;
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[payment_terms]',PaymentTermsDescription);


          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


          // Verkaufsrechnungzeilen zu Rechnung �bertragen
          SalesInvoiceLine.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
          IF SalesInvoiceLine.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            UpdateSalesInvoiceLine(SalesInvoiceLine);
          UNTIL SalesInvoiceLine.NEXT = 0;

          "Date Last Updated (Webshop)" := CURRENTDATETIME;
          MODIFY(FALSE);
          COMMIT;

        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateSalesInvoiceHeader(IsHandled,ParameterSalesInvoiceHeader);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetAllInvoiceRequest@1140019(CountRecords@1140002 : Boolean);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1140000 : Text[1024];
      NoOfRec@1140001 : Integer;
      Text001@1140003 : TextConst 'DEU=Rechnungsanforderungen;ENU=Invoice requests';
      GetResponseText@1000 : Text;
      LogFile@1001 : File;
      IsHandled@1000000000 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeGetAllInvoiceRequest(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Rechnungsanforderungen verarbeiten

        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_sales_invoice_header');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[send_request]',TRUE);
        GetResponseText := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_no_of_records',CurrRec);
        IF EVALUATE(NoOfRec,GetResponseText) THEN BEGIN
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(Text001,NoOfRec);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(Text001,NoOfRec);
            GetInvoiceRequest;
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
            ERROR(GetResponseText)
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
              LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
              LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
              IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
               IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                ERROR(Error001);
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                  ERROR(Error002);
              END;
              LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
              LogFile.WRITE(GetResponseText);
              LogFile.CLOSE;
              IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                MESSAGE(GetResponseText);
            END ELSE
              ERROR(Error003);
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterGetAllInvoiceRequest(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetInvoiceRequest@1140020();
    VAR
      SalesInvoiceHeader@1000000000 : Record 112;
      CurrRec@1140019 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1140018 : Text[1024];
      SetRec@1140017 : Text[1024];
      InvoiceNo@1140015 : Code[20];
      RequestEMail@1140014 : Text[80];
      NextInvoiceRequest@1140013 : Boolean;
      Pos@1140012 : Integer;
      Delimiter@1140011 : Text[30];
      ShopCode@1000000001 : Code[10];
      LanguageCode@1000000002 : Code[10];
      LogFile@1000 : File;
      IsHandled@1000000003 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeGetInvoiceRequest(IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Rechnungsanforderungen verarbeiten

        Delimiter := '-;-';
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_sales_invoice_header');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'fields','no,request_shop_code,request_language_code,request_mail');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'delimiter',Delimiter);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[send_request]',TRUE);
        WITH SalesInvoiceHeader DO REPEAT
          NextInvoiceRequest := FALSE;
          GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_content',CurrRec);
          IF STRPOS(GetRec,Delimiter) <> 0 THEN BEGIN
            InvoiceNo := WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter);
            ShopCode := WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter);
            LanguageCode := WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter);
            RequestEMail := WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter);
            IF (InvoiceNo <> '') AND (RequestEMail <> '') AND (ShopCode <> '') AND (LanguageCode <> '') THEN BEGIN
              IF GET(InvoiceNo) THEN
                SendInvoiceRequestMail(SalesInvoiceHeader,ShopCode,LanguageCode,RequestEMail);
              CLEAR(SetRec);
              AddVar(SetRec,'table','shop_sales_invoice_header');
              AddVar(SetRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
              AddVar(SetRec,'primary[no]',"No.");
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[request_mail]','');
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[request_shop_code]','');
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[request_language_code]','');
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[send_request]',FALSE);
              IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
                WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',SetRec,FALSE,TRUE)
              ELSE
                WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',SetRec);

              NextInvoiceRequest := TRUE;
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF GetRec<>'0' THEN BEGIN
              IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
                ERROR(GetRec)
              ELSE BEGIN
                IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
                  LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                  LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                  IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
                   IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                    ERROR(Error001);
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                      ERROR(Error002);
                  END;
                  LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                  LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
                  LogFile.CLOSE;
                  IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                    MESSAGE(GetRec);
                END ELSE
                  ERROR(Error003);
              END;
            END;

          END;
        UNTIL NOT NextInvoiceRequest;

      END;
      OnAfterGetInvoiceRequest(IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SendInvoiceRequestMail@1140024(InvoiceHeader@1140000 : Record 112;ShopCode@1000000000 : Code[10];LanguageCode@1000000001 : Code[10];RequestMail@1000000002 : Text[80]);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1140002 : Text[1024];
      FileName@1140003 : Text[100];
      Language@1140004 : Record 5447302;
      IsHandled@1000000003 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeSendInvoiceRequestMail(IsHandled,InvoiceHeader,ShopCode,LanguageCode,RequestMail);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Mail f�r Rechnungsanforderungen versenden

        IF Language.GET(ShopCode,LanguageCode) THEN BEGIN
          IF Language."E-Mail Invoice Copy" <>  '' THEN BEGIN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'type','invoice_copy');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'shop_code',ShopCode);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'language_code',LanguageCode);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'invoice_no',InvoiceHeader."No.");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'customer_no',InvoiceHeader."Sell-to Customer No.");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'request_mail',RequestMail);
            IF Language."Report PDF Invoice" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
              FileName := WebshopManagement.CreateDocumentPDF(1,ShopCode,LanguageCode,InvoiceHeader."No.");
              AddVar(CurrRec,'attachment',FileName);
            END;
            IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
              WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('send_customer_email',CurrRec,FALSE,TRUE)
            ELSE
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('send_customer_email',CurrRec);
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterSendInvoiceRequestMail(IsHandled,InvoiceHeader,ShopCode,LanguageCode,RequestMail);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllSalesShipmentHeader@1140009(CountRecords@1000000002 : Boolean) Ok : Boolean;
    VAR
      SalesShipmentHeader@1000000000 : Record 110;
      IsHandled@1000000001 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateAllSalesShipmentHeader(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Alle Verkaufslieferungen �bertragen

        WITH SalesShipmentHeader DO BEGIN
          SETCURRENTKEY("Posting Date","Date Last Updated (Webshop)");
          SETRANGE("Posting Date",CALCDATE('<-CY-1Y>'),WORKDATE);
          SETRANGE("Date Last Updated (Webshop)",0DT);
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
            IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              UpdateSalesShipmentHeader(SalesShipmentHeader);
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            UNTIL NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateAllSalesShipmentHeader(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateSalesShipmentHeader@1140014(ParameterSalesShipmentHeader@1140000 : Record 110);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1140001 : Text[1024];
      SalesShipmentLine@1140002 : Record 111;
      ShipmentMethod@1000000002 : Record 10;
      ShipmentMethodTranslation@1000000001 : Record 463;
      ShipmentMethodDescription@1000000000 : Text[50];
      SalesShipmentHeader@1000000003 : Record 110;
      WebshopShop@1000000004 : Record 5447301;
      IsHandled@1000000005 : Boolean;
      ShippingAgent@1000000006 : Record 291;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateSalesShipmentHeader(IsHandled,ParameterSalesShipmentHeader);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Einzelne Verkaufslieferung �bertragen

        WITH SalesShipmentHeader DO BEGIN
          GET(ParameterSalesShipmentHeader."No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_sales_shipment_header');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[no]',"No.");

          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[posting_date]',"Posting Date");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[order_no]',"Order No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[webshop_order_no]',"Webshop Order No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[your_reference]',"Your Reference");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_customer_no]',"Sell-to Customer No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_customer_no]',"Bill-to Customer No.");

          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_name]',"Sell-to Customer Name");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_name_2]',"Sell-to Customer Name 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_address]',"Sell-to Address");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_address_2]',"Sell-to Address 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_post_code]',"Sell-to Post Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_city]',"Sell-to City");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_country]',"Sell-to Country/Region Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_contact]',"Sell-to Contact");

          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


          CLEAR(CurrRec);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_sales_shipment_header');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[no]',"No.");

          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_name]',"Bill-to Name");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_name_2]',"Bill-to Name 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_address]',"Bill-to Address");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_address_2]',"Bill-to Address 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_post_code]',"Bill-to Post Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_city]',"Bill-to City");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_country]',"Bill-to Country/Region Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_contact]',"Bill-to Contact");

          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_name]',"Ship-to Name");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_name_2]',"Ship-to Name 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_address]',"Ship-to Address");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_address_2]',"Ship-to Address 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_post_code]',"Ship-to Post Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_city]',"Ship-to City");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_country]',"Ship-to Country/Region Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_contact]',"Ship-to Contact");

          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[tracking_no]',"Package Tracking No.");
          IF ShippingAgent.GET("Shipping Agent Code") THEN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[tracking_link]',STRSUBSTNO(ShippingAgent."Internet Address","Package Tracking No."));

          // Lieferbedingung als Text
          IF ShipmentMethod.GET("Shipment Method Code") THEN BEGIN
            IF ShipmentMethodTranslation.GET("Shipment Method Code","Language Code") THEN
              ShipmentMethodDescription := ShipmentMethodTranslation.Description
            ELSE
              ShipmentMethodDescription := ShipmentMethod.Description;
          END;
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[shipment_method]',ShipmentMethodDescription);

          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


          // Verkaufslieferungszeilen zu Lieferung �bertragen
          SalesShipmentLine.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
          IF SalesShipmentLine.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            UpdateSalesShipmentLine(SalesShipmentLine);
          UNTIL SalesShipmentLine.NEXT = 0;

          //Marketplace
          IF "Webshop Order No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
            IF WebshopShop.GET("Webshop Shop Code") THEN BEGIN
              IF WebshopShop."Shop Type" = WebshopShop."Shop Type"::marketplace THEN BEGIN
                AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_sales_shipment_header');
                AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
                AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[no]',"No.");
                AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_update]',TRUE);

                IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
                  WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
                ELSE
                  WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);
              END;
            END;
          END;

          "Date Last Updated (Webshop)" := CURRENTDATETIME;
          MODIFY(FALSE);
          COMMIT;

        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateSalesShipmentHeader(IsHandled,ParameterSalesShipmentHeader);
    END;
