    PROCEDURE GetAllShipmentRequest@1140034(CountRecords@1140002 : Boolean);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1140000 : Text[1024];
      NoOfRec@1140001 : Integer;
      Text001@1140003 : TextConst 'DEU=Lieferscheinanforderungen;ENU=Shipping notice requests';
      GetResponseText@1000 : Text;
      LogFile@1001 : File;
      IsHandled@1000000000 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeGetAllShipmentRequest(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Alle neuen Lieferscheinanforderungen abholen

        WebshopSetup.GET;
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_sales_shipment_header');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[send_request]',TRUE);
        GetResponseText := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_no_of_records',CurrRec);
        IF EVALUATE(NoOfRec,GetResponseText) THEN BEGIN
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(Text001,NoOfRec);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(Text001,NoOfRec);
            GetShipmentRequest;
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
            ERROR(GetResponseText)
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
              LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
              LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
              IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
               IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                ERROR(Error001);
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                  ERROR(Error002);
              END;
              LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
              LogFile.WRITE(GetResponseText);
              LogFile.CLOSE;
              IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                MESSAGE(GetResponseText);
            END ELSE
              ERROR(Error003);
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterGetAllShipmentRequest(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetShipmentRequest@1140033() NoOfRequests : Integer;
    VAR
      CurrRec@1140019 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1140018 : Text[1024];
      SetRec@1140017 : Text[1024];
      ShipmentNo@1140015 : Code[20];
      RequestEmail@1140014 : Text[80];
      ShopCode@1000000000 : Code[10];
      LanguageCode@1000000001 : Code[10];
      NextShipmentRequest@1140013 : Boolean;
      Pos@1140012 : Integer;
      Delimiter@1140011 : Text[30];
      SalesShipmentHeader@1140010 : Record 110;
      LogFile@1000 : File;
      IsHandled@1000000002 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeGetShipmentRequest(IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Einzelne Lieferscheinanforderung verarbeiten

        Delimiter := '-;-';
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_sales_shipment_header');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'fields','no,request_shop_code,request_language_code,request_mail');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'delimiter',Delimiter);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[send_request]',TRUE);
        WITH SalesShipmentHeader DO REPEAT
          NextShipmentRequest := FALSE;
          GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_content',CurrRec);
          IF STRPOS(GetRec,Delimiter) <> 0 THEN BEGIN
            ShipmentNo := WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter);
            ShopCode := WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter);
            LanguageCode := WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter);
            RequestEmail := WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter);
            IF (ShipmentNo <> '') AND (RequestEmail <> '') AND (ShopCode <> '') AND (LanguageCode <> '') THEN BEGIN
              IF GET(ShipmentNo) THEN
                SendShipmentRequestMail(SalesShipmentHeader,ShopCode,LanguageCode,RequestEmail);
              CLEAR(SetRec);
              AddVar(SetRec,'table','shop_sales_shipment_header');
              AddVar(SetRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
              AddVar(SetRec,'primary[no]',"No.");
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[request_mail]','');
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[request_shop_code]','');
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[request_language_code]','');
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[send_request]',FALSE);
              IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
                WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',SetRec,FALSE,TRUE)
              ELSE
                WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',SetRec);

              NextShipmentRequest := TRUE;
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF GetRec<>'0' THEN BEGIN
              IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
                ERROR(GetRec)
              ELSE BEGIN
                IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
                  LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                  LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                  IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
                   IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                    ERROR(Error001);
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                      ERROR(Error002);
                  END;
                  LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                  LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
                  LogFile.CLOSE;
                  IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                    MESSAGE(GetRec);
                END ELSE
                  ERROR(Error003);
              END;
            END;

          END;
        UNTIL NOT NextShipmentRequest;

      END;
      OnAfterGetShipmentRequest(IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SendShipmentRequestMail@1140032(ShipmentHeader@1140000 : Record 110;ShopCode@1000000000 : Code[10];LanguageCode@1000000001 : Code[10];RequestMail@1140001 : Text[80]);

            ELSE

          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterSendShipmentRequestMail(IsHandled,ShipmentHeader,ShopCode,LanguageCode,RequestMail);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllSalesOrder@1000000137(CountRecords@1000000000 : Boolean) OK : Boolean;
    VAR
      SalesHeader@1000000003 : Record 36;
      Customer@1000000001 : Record 18;
      TempDate@1000000002 : Date;
      IsHandled@1000000004 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateAllSalesOrder(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Alle Verkaufsauftr�ge �bertragen

        WITH SalesHeader DO BEGIN
          SETRANGE("Document Type","Document Type"::Order);
          SETRANGE(SalesHeader.Status,SalesHeader.Status::Released,Status::"Pending Prepayment");
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
            IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              IF Customer.GET("Sell-to Customer No.") AND Customer."Has Webshop Login" THEN BEGIN
                IF (("Date Last Updated (Webshop)" < "Date Last Modified (Webshop)") OR ("Date Last Modified (Webshop)" = 0DT) OR ("Date Last Updated (Webshop)" = 0DT)) THEN
                  UpdateSalesOrder(SalesHeader);
              END;
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            UNTIL NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateAllSalesOrder(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

      SalesLine@1118900000 : Record 37;

          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_contact]',"Bill-to Contact");

          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


          CLEAR(CurrRec);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_nav_sales_header');

          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_country]',"Ship-to Country/Region Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_contact]',"Ship-to Contact");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[return_receipt_no]',"Return Receipt No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[last_return_receipt_no]',"Last Return Receipt No.");

          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[amount_including_vat]',"Amount Including VAT");

            IF ShipmentMethodTranslation.GET("Shipment Method Code","Language Code") THEN
              ShipmentMethodDescription := ShipmentMethodTranslation.Description

              ShipmentMethodDescription := ShipmentMethod.Description;

            IF PaymentTermTranslation.GET("Payment Terms Code","Language Code") THEN
              PaymentTermsDescription := PaymentTermTranslation.Description
            ELSE
              PaymentTermsDescription := PaymentTerms.Description;
          END;
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[payment_terms]',PaymentTermsDescription);

          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


          "Date Last Updated (Webshop)" := CURRENTDATETIME;
          MODIFY;

          // Zugeh�rige Auftragszeilen �bertragen
          SalesLine.SETRANGE("Document Type","Document Type");
          SalesLine.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
          DeleteSalesOrderLinePerOrder(FALSE,"Document Type","No.");
          IF SalesLine.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            UpdateSalesOrderLine(SalesLine);
          UNTIL SalesLine.NEXT = 0;
          DeleteSalesOrderLinePerOrder(TRUE,"Document Type","No.");

          COMMIT;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateSalesOrder(IsHandled,ParameterSalesHeader);
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteSalesOrder@1000000132(SalesHeader@1000000001 : Record 36);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000000 : Text[1024];

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllSalesCrMemoHeader@1000000152(CountRecords@1000000002 : Boolean) Ok : Boolean;
    VAR
      SalesCrMemoHeader@1000000000 : Record 114;
      IsHandled@1000000001 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateAllSalesCrMemoHeader(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Alle Geb. Verkaufsgutschriften �bertragen

        WITH SalesCrMemoHeader DO BEGIN
          SETCURRENTKEY("Posting Date","Date Last Updated (Webshop)");
          SETRANGE("Posting Date",CALCDATE('<-CY-1Y>'),WORKDATE);
          SETRANGE("Date Last Updated (Webshop)",0DT);
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
            IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              UpdateSalesCrMemoHeader(SalesCrMemoHeader);
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            UNTIL NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateAllSalesCrMemoHeader(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateSalesCrMemoHeader@1000000151(ParameterSalesCrMemoHeader@1140000 : Record 114);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1140001 : Text[1024];
      SalesCrMemoLine@1140002 : Record 115;
      ShipmentMethod@1000000005 : Record 10;
      PaymentTerms@1000000004 : Record 3;
      ShipmentMethodTranslation@1000000003 : Record 463;
      PaymentTermTranslation@1000000002 : Record 462;
      ShipmentMethodDescription@1000000001 : Text[50];
      PaymentTermsDescription@1000000000 : Text[50];
      SalesCrMemoHeader@1000000006 : Record 114;
      IsHandled@1000000007 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateSalesCrMemoHeader(IsHandled,ParameterSalesCrMemoHeader);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Einzelne Verkaufsrechnung �bertragen

        WebshopSetup.GET;
        WITH SalesCrMemoHeader DO BEGIN
          GET(ParameterSalesCrMemoHeader."No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_cr_memo_header');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[no]',"No.");

          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[posting_date]',"Posting Date");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[webshop_order_no]',"Webshop Order No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[your_reference]',"Your Reference");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_customer_no]',"Sell-to Customer No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_customer_no]',"Bill-to Customer No.");

          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_name]',"Sell-to Customer Name");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_name_2]',"Sell-to Customer Name 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_address]',"Sell-to Address");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_address_2]',"Sell-to Address 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_post_code]',"Sell-to Post Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_city]',"Sell-to City");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_country]',"Sell-to Country/Region Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sell_to_contact]',"Sell-to Contact");

          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_name]',"Bill-to Name");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_name_2]',"Bill-to Name 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_address]',"Bill-to Address");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_address_2]',"Bill-to Address 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_post_code]',"Bill-to Post Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_city]',"Bill-to City");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_country]',"Bill-to Country/Region Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_contact]',"Bill-to Contact");

          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


          CLEAR(CurrRec);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_cr_memo_header');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[no]',"No.");

          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_name]',"Ship-to Name");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_name_2]',"Ship-to Name 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_address]',"Ship-to Address");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_address_2]',"Ship-to Address 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_post_code]',"Ship-to Post Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_city]',"Ship-to City");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_country]',"Ship-to Country/Region Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_contact]',"Ship-to Contact");

          CALCFIELDS(Amount,"Amount Including VAT");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[amount]',Amount);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[amount_including_vat]',"Amount Including VAT");

          // Lieferbedingung als Text
          IF ShipmentMethod.GET("Shipment Method Code") THEN BEGIN
            IF ShipmentMethodTranslation.GET("Shipment Method Code","Language Code") THEN
              ShipmentMethodDescription := ShipmentMethodTranslation.Description
            ELSE
              ShipmentMethodDescription := ShipmentMethod.Description;
          END;
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[shipment_method]',ShipmentMethodDescription);

          // Zahlungsbedingung als Text
          IF PaymentTerms.GET("Payment Terms Code") THEN BEGIN
            IF PaymentTermTranslation.GET("Payment Terms Code","Language Code") THEN
              PaymentTermsDescription := PaymentTermTranslation.Description
            ELSE
              PaymentTermsDescription := PaymentTerms.Description;
          END;
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[payment_terms]',PaymentTermsDescription);


          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


          // Verkaufsgutschriftzeilen zu Gutschrift �bertragen
          SalesCrMemoLine.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
          IF SalesCrMemoLine.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            UpdateSalesCrMemoLine(SalesCrMemoLine);
          UNTIL SalesCrMemoLine.NEXT = 0;

          "Date Last Updated (Webshop)" := CURRENTDATETIME;
          MODIFY(FALSE);
          COMMIT;

        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateSalesCrMemoHeader(IsHandled,ParameterSalesCrMemoHeader);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetAllCrMemoRequest@1000000214(CountRecords@1140002 : Boolean);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1140000 : Text[1024];
      NoOfRec@1140001 : Integer;
      Text001@1140003 : TextConst 'DEU=Lieferscheinanforderungen;ENU=Shipping notice requests';
      GetResponseText@1000 : Text;
      LogFile@1001 : File;
      IsHandled@1000000000 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeGetAllCrMemoRequest(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Alle neuen Gutschriftanforderungen abholen

        WebshopSetup.GET;
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_cr_memo_header');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[send_request]',TRUE);
        GetResponseText := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_no_of_records',CurrRec);
        IF EVALUATE(NoOfRec,GetResponseText) THEN BEGIN
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(Text001,NoOfRec);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(Text001,NoOfRec);
            GetCrMemoRequest;
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
            ERROR(GetResponseText)
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
              LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
              LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
              IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
               IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                ERROR(Error001);
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                  ERROR(Error002);
              END;
              LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
              LogFile.WRITE(GetResponseText);
              LogFile.CLOSE;
              IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                MESSAGE(GetResponseText);
            END ELSE
              ERROR(Error003);
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterGetAllCrMemoRequest(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetCrMemoRequest@1000000213() NoOfRequests : Integer;
    VAR
      CurrRec@1140019 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1140018 : Text[1024];
      SetRec@1140017 : Text[1024];
      CrMemoNo@1140015 : Code[20];
      RequestEmail@1140014 : Text[80];
      ShopCode@1000000000 : Code[10];
      LanguageCode@1000000001 : Code[10];
      NextShipmentRequest@1140013 : Boolean;
      Pos@1140012 : Integer;
      Delimiter@1140011 : Text[30];
      SalesCrMemoHeader@1140010 : Record 114;
      LogFile@1000 : File;
      IsHandled@1000000002 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeGetCrMemoRequest(IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Einzelne Gutschriftanforderung verarbeiten

        Delimiter := '-;-';
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_cr_memo_header');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'fields','no,request_shop_code,request_language_code,request_mail');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'delimiter',Delimiter);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[send_request]',TRUE);
        WITH SalesCrMemoHeader DO REPEAT
          NextShipmentRequest := FALSE;
          GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_content',CurrRec);
          IF STRPOS(GetRec,Delimiter) <> 0 THEN BEGIN
            CrMemoNo := WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter);
            ShopCode := WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter);
            LanguageCode := WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter);
            RequestEmail := WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter);
            IF (CrMemoNo <> '') AND (RequestEmail <> '') AND (ShopCode <> '') AND (LanguageCode <> '') THEN BEGIN
              IF GET(CrMemoNo) THEN
                SendCrMemoRequestMail(SalesCrMemoHeader,ShopCode,LanguageCode,RequestEmail);
              CLEAR(SetRec);
              AddVar(SetRec,'table','shop_cr_memo_header');
              AddVar(SetRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
              AddVar(SetRec,'primary[no]',"No.");
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[request_mail]','');
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[request_shop_code]','');
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[request_language_code]','');
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[send_request]',FALSE);
              IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
                WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',SetRec,FALSE,TRUE)
              ELSE
                WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',SetRec);

              NextShipmentRequest := TRUE;
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF GetRec<>'0' THEN BEGIN
              IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
                ERROR(GetRec)
              ELSE BEGIN
                IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
                  LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                  LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                  IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
                   IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                    ERROR(Error001);
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                      ERROR(Error002);
                  END;
                  LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                  LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
                  LogFile.CLOSE;
                  IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                    MESSAGE(GetRec);
                END ELSE
                  ERROR(Error003);
              END;
            END;

          END;
        UNTIL NOT NextShipmentRequest;

      END;
      OnAfterGetCrMemoRequest(IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SendCrMemoRequestMail@1000000212(CrMemoHeader@1140000 : Record 114;ShopCode@1000000000 : Code[10];LanguageCode@1000000001 : Code[10];RequestMail@1140001 : Text[80]);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1140002 : Text[1024];
      FileName@1140003 : Text[100];
      Language@1140004 : Record 5447302;
      IsHandled@1000000002 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeSendCrMemoRequestMail(IsHandled,CrMemoHeader,ShopCode,LanguageCode,RequestMail);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Gutschriftkopie versenden

        IF Language.GET(ShopCode,LanguageCode) THEN BEGIN
          IF Language."E-Mail Cr Memo Copy" <>  '' THEN BEGIN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'type','cr_memo_copy');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'shop_code',ShopCode);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'language_code',LanguageCode);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'cr_memo_no',CrMemoHeader."No.");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'customer_no',CrMemoHeader."Sell-to Customer No.");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'request_mail',RequestMail);
            IF Language."Report PDF Cr.Memo" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
              FileName := WebshopManagement.CreateDocumentPDF(3,ShopCode,LanguageCode,CrMemoHeader."No.");
              AddVar(CurrRec,'attachment',FileName);
            END;
            IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
              WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('send_customer_email',CurrRec,FALSE,TRUE)
            ELSE
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('send_customer_email',CurrRec);
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterSendCrMemoRequestMail(IsHandled,CrMemoHeader,ShopCode,LanguageCode,RequestMail);
    END;

    END;

                IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
