    PROCEDURE SendTextModuleMail@1000000189(ShopLanguage@1000000001 : Record 5447302;TextModule@1000000003 : Record 5447309;SenderMail@1000000006 : Text[255];RecipientMail@1000000005 : Text[255];PostFieldsString@1000000002 : Text[1024];AttachmentPath@1000000004 : Text[255]);
    VAR
      Error001@1000000007 : TextConst;
      Shop@1000000000 : Record 5447301;
      WebshopSetup@1000000008 : Record 5447300;
      WebshopUpdateBuffer@1000000009 : Record 5447314;
      IsHandled@1000000010 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeSendTextModuleMail(IsHandled,ShopLanguage,TextModule,SenderMail,RecipientMail,PostFieldsString,AttachmentPath);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        WebshopSetup.GET;
        Shop.GET(ShopLanguage."Shop Code");
        AddVar(PostFieldsString,'company',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(PostFieldsString,'shop_code',Shop.Code);
        AddVar(PostFieldsString,'sender_email',SenderMail);
        AddVar(PostFieldsString,'recipient_email',RecipientMail);
        AddVar(PostFieldsString,'text_module_code',TextModule.Code);
        IF AttachmentPath <> '' THEN
          AddVar(PostFieldsString,'attachment',AttachmentPath);
        IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
          WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('text_module_mail_handler',PostFieldsString,FALSE,TRUE)
        ELSE
          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('text_module_mail_handler',PostFieldsString);

      END;
      OnAfterSendTextModuleMail(IsHandled,ShopLanguage,TextModule,SenderMail,RecipientMail,PostFieldsString,AttachmentPath);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SendAllOrderConfirmMail@1000000118(CountRecords@1140000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      SalesHeader@1000000000 : Record 36;
      IsHandled@1000000001 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeSendAllOrderConfirmMail(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Alle Auftragsbest�tigungen versendenn

        WITH SalesHeader DO BEGIN
          SETCURRENTKEY("Webshop Order No.","Send Webshop Order Confirm.","Date Web. Order Confirm. Sent");
          SETRANGE("Document Type",SalesHeader."Document Type"::Order);
          SETRANGE("Send Webshop Order Confirm.",TRUE);
          SETRANGE("Date Web. Order Confirm. Sent",0DT);
          SETFILTER("Document Date",'%1..%2',TODAY-7,TODAY);
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
            IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              IF ((("Document Type" IN ["Document Type"::Quote,"Document Type"::Order]) AND ("Webshop Order No." <> ''))
                OR (("Document Type" = "Document Type"::"Return Order") AND ("Webshop Return Order No." <> ''))) THEN BEGIN
                SendOrderConfirmMail(SalesHeader,"Webshop Shop Code","Webshop Language Code",'');
                GET("Document Type","No.");
                "Send Webshop Order Confirm." := FALSE;
                "Date Web. Order Confirm. Sent" := CURRENTDATETIME;
                MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            UNTIL NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterSendAllOrderConfirmMail(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SendOrderConfirmMail@1000000115(ParameterSalesHeader@1140000 : Record 36;ShopCode@1000000000 : Code[10];LanguageCode@1000000001 : Code[10];RequestMail@1140001 : Text[80]);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1140002 : Text[1024];
      FileName@1140003 : Text[100];
      Language@1140004 : Record 5447302;
      SalesHeader@1000000002 : Record 36;
      IsHandled@1000000003 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeSendOrderConfirmMail(IsHandled,ParameterSalesHeader,ShopCode,LanguageCode,RequestMail);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Auftragsbest�tigung versenden

        SalesHeader.GET(ParameterSalesHeader."Document Type",ParameterSalesHeader."No.");
        IF Language.GET(ShopCode,LanguageCode) THEN BEGIN
          IF Language."E-Mail Order 2" <>  '' THEN BEGIN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'type','order_mail_2');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'shop_code',ShopCode);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'language_code',LanguageCode);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'order_no',SalesHeader."Webshop Order No.");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'request_mail',RequestMail);
            IF Language."Report PDF Order Confirmation" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
              FileName := WebshopManagement.CreateDocumentPDF(0,ShopCode,LanguageCode,SalesHeader."No.");
              AddVar(CurrRec,'attachment',FileName);
            END;
            IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
              WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('send_customer_email',CurrRec,FALSE,TRUE)
            ELSE
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('send_customer_email',CurrRec);

          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterSendOrderConfirmMail(IsHandled,ParameterSalesHeader,ShopCode,LanguageCode,RequestMail);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SendAllOrderShippingMail@1140027(CountRecords@1140000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      ShipmentHeader@1140001 : Record 110;
      SubscriptionHeader@1000000000 : Record 5447340;
      IsHandled@1000000001 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeSendAllOrderShippingMail(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Alle Lieferbest�tigungen versenden

        WITH ShipmentHeader DO BEGIN
          SETCURRENTKEY("Webshop Order No.","Send Webshop Shipping Notice","Date Web. Ship. Notice Sent");
          SETFILTER("Webshop Order No.",'<>%1','');
          SETRANGE("Send Webshop Shipping Notice",TRUE);
          SETRANGE("Date Web. Ship. Notice Sent",0DT);
          SETFILTER("Package Tracking No.",'<>%1','');
          SETFILTER("Document Date",'%1..%2',TODAY-7,TODAY);
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
            IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              //Nur normale Lieferbest�tigung versenden wenn keine Abo-Bestellung
              //oder erstmalige Abo-Bestellung (aus nicht aus autom. Anlage)
              IF ("Webshop Subscription Code" = '') OR (SubscriptionHeader.GET("Webshop Subscription Code") AND
                  NOT((SubscriptionHeader.Type = SubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionOrderOption) OR ("Is Webshop Subscr. Ship. Order" = TRUE)))
                  THEN BEGIN
                  IF (CURRENTDATETIME > ShipmentHeader."Web. Ship. Mail Send Time") THEN BEGIN
                    SendOrderShipingMail(ShipmentHeader,"Webshop Shop Code","Webshop Language Code",'');
                    GET("No.");
                    "Send Webshop Shipping Notice" := FALSE;
                    "Date Web. Ship. Notice Sent" := CURRENTDATETIME;
                    MODIFY(FALSE);
                  END;
              END;
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            UNTIL NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterSendAllOrderShippingMail(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SendOrderShipingMail@1000000103(ParameterShipmentHeader@1140000 : Record 110;ShopCode@1000000000 : Code[10];LanguageCode@1000000001 : Code[10];RequestMail@1140001 : Text[80]);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1140002 : Text[1024];
      FileName@1140003 : Text[100];
      Language@1140004 : Record 5447302;
      ShipmentHeader@1000000002 : Record 110;
      ShippingAgent@1000000003 : Record 291;
      IsHandled@1000000004 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeSendOrderShipingMail(IsHandled,ParameterShipmentHeader,ShopCode,LanguageCode,RequestMail);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Lieferscheinkopie versenden

        ShipmentHeader.GET(ParameterShipmentHeader."No.");
        IF Language.GET(ShopCode,LanguageCode) THEN BEGIN
          IF Language."E-Mail Order 3" <>  '' THEN BEGIN
            IF (NOT Language."Shipment Notification Tracking") OR ((Language."Shipment Notification Tracking") AND (ShipmentHeader."Package Tracking No." <> '')) THEN BEGIN
              AddVar(CurrRec,'type','order_mail_3');
              AddVar(CurrRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
              AddVar(CurrRec,'shop_code',ShopCode);
              AddVar(CurrRec,'language_code',LanguageCode);
              AddVar(CurrRec,'order_no',ShipmentHeader."Webshop Order No.");
              AddVar(CurrRec,'tracking_no',ShipmentHeader."Package Tracking No.");
              IF ShippingAgent.GET(ShipmentHeader."Shipping Agent Code") THEN
                AddVar(CurrRec,'tracking_link',STRSUBSTNO(ShippingAgent."Internet Address",ShipmentHeader."Package Tracking No."));

              AddVar(CurrRec,'request_mail',RequestMail);
              IF Language."Report PDF Shipment" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
                FileName := WebshopManagement.CreateDocumentPDF(2,ShopCode,LanguageCode,ShipmentHeader."No.");
                AddVar(CurrRec,'attachment',FileName);
              END;
              IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
                WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('send_customer_email',CurrRec,FALSE,TRUE)
              ELSE
                WebshopManagement.SendRequest('send_customer_email',CurrRec);
            END;
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterSendOrderShipingMail(IsHandled,ParameterShipmentHeader,ShopCode,LanguageCode,RequestMail);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SendAllOrderInvoiceMail@1000000159(CountRecords@1140000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      InvoiceHeader@1000000000 : Record 112;
      SubscriptionHeader@1000000001 : Record 5447340;
      IsHandled@1000000002 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeSendAllOrderInvoiceMail(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Alle Rechnungen versendenn

        WITH InvoiceHeader DO BEGIN
          SETCURRENTKEY("Webshop Order No.","Send Webshop Invoice Mail","Date Webshop Invoice Mail Sent");
          SETFILTER("Webshop Order No.",'<>%1','');
          SETFILTER("Posting Date",'>%1',0D);
          SETRANGE("Send Webshop Invoice Mail",TRUE);
          SETRANGE("Date Webshop Invoice Mail Sent",0DT);
          SETFILTER("Document Date",'%1..%2',TODAY-7,TODAY);
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
            IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              IF ("Webshop Subscription Code" = '') OR (SubscriptionHeader.GET("Webshop Subscription Code") AND
                  NOT((SubscriptionHeader.Type = SubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionOrderOption) OR ("Is Webshop Subscr. Ship. Order" = TRUE)))
                  THEN BEGIN
                IF (CURRENTDATETIME > InvoiceHeader."Web. Inv. Mail Send Time") THEN BEGIN
                  SendOrderInvoiceMail(InvoiceHeader,"Webshop Shop Code","Webshop Language Code",'');
                  GET("No.");
                  "Send Webshop Invoice Mail" := FALSE;
                  "Date Webshop Invoice Mail Sent" := CURRENTDATETIME;
                  MODIFY(FALSE);
                END;
              END;
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            UNTIL NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterSendAllOrderInvoiceMail(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SendOrderInvoiceMail@1000000158(InvoiceHeader@1140000 : Record 112;ShopCode@1000000000 : Code[10];LanguageCode@1000000001 : Code[10];RequestMail@1000000002 : Text[80]);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1140002 : Text[1024];
      FileName@1140003 : Text[100];
      Language@1140004 : Record 5447302;
      PaymentOption@1000000003 : Record 5447312;
      TextModuleCode@1000000004 : Code[80];
      SendToPaymentProvider@1000000005 : Boolean;
      WebshopTextModule@1000000006 : Record 5447309;
      PaymentProviderEmailAddr@1000000007 : Text;
      IsHandled@1000000008 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeSendOrderInvoiceMail(IsHandled,InvoiceHeader,ShopCode,LanguageCode,RequestMail);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Mail f�r Rechnungsanforderungen versenden

        IF PaymentOption.GET(ShopCode,LanguageCode,InvoiceHeader."Webshop Payment Opt. Line No.") THEN BEGIN
          IF (PaymentOption."Email Invoice Copy To Provider" <> '') AND (WebshopTextModule.GET(PaymentOption."Email Invoice Copy To Provider")) THEN BEGIN
            TextModuleCode := PaymentOption."Email Invoice Copy To Provider";
            PaymentProviderEmailAddr := PaymentOption."Provider Email Addr. Invoice";
          END ELSE IF Language.GET(ShopCode,LanguageCode) AND (Language."E-Mail Order 4" <> '') AND (WebshopTextModule.GET(Language."E-Mail Order 4")) THEN
            TextModuleCode := Language."E-Mail Order 4";
          IF PaymentOption."Provider Email Addr. Invoice" <> '' THEN
            SendToPaymentProvider := TRUE;
        END;

        IF TextModuleCode <> '' THEN BEGIN
          AddVar(CurrRec,'type','order_mail_4');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'text_module_code',TextModuleCode);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'send_to_payment_provider',SendToPaymentProvider);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'payment_provider_address',PaymentProviderEmailAddr);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'shop_code',ShopCode);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'language_code',LanguageCode);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'invoice_no',InvoiceHeader."No.");
          InvoiceHeader.CALCFIELDS("Amount Including VAT");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'amount',InvoiceHeader."Amount Including VAT");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'customer_no',InvoiceHeader."Sell-to Customer No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'order_no',InvoiceHeader."Webshop Order No.");
          IF Language."Report PDF Invoice" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
            FileName := WebshopManagement.CreateDocumentPDF(1,ShopCode,LanguageCode,InvoiceHeader."No.");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'attachment',FileName);
          END;
          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('send_customer_email',CurrRec,FALSE,TRUE)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('send_customer_email',CurrRec);
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterSendOrderInvoiceMail(IsHandled,InvoiceHeader,ShopCode,LanguageCode,RequestMail);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetOrderShipMailSendTime@1000000226(ParaSalesShipmentHeader@1000000000 : Record 110);
    VAR
      SalesShipmentHeader@1000000001 : Record 110;
      WebshopShop@1000000002 : Record 5447301;
      IsHandled@1000000003 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeSetOrderShipMailSendTime(IsHandled,ParaSalesShipmentHeader);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        IF ParaSalesShipmentHeader."Webshop Shop Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF WebshopShop.GET(ParaSalesShipmentHeader."Webshop Shop Code") THEN BEGIN
            IF FORMAT(WebshopShop."Ship. Notice Mail Send Delay") <> '' THEN BEGIN
              SalesShipmentHeader.GET(ParaSalesShipmentHeader."No.");
              SalesShipmentHeader."Web. Ship. Mail Send Time" := CURRENTDATETIME + WebshopShop."Ship. Notice Mail Send Delay";
              SalesShipmentHeader.MODIFY(FALSE);
            END;
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterSetOrderShipMailSendTime(IsHandled,ParaSalesShipmentHeader);
    END;

    PROCEDURE ResetInvoicePayment@1140016(SalesInvoiceHeader@1140000 : Record 112);
    VAR
      Text001@1000000000 : TextConst 'DEU=Zahlungen wurden zur�ckgesetzt.;ENU=Payments have been reset.';
      IsHandled@1000000001 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeResetInvoicePayment(IsHandled,SalesInvoiceHeader);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        WITH SalesInvoiceHeader DO BEGIN
          IF GET("No.") THEN BEGIN
            "Webshop Payment Error" := '';
            "Date Webshop Payment Processed" := 0DT;
            "Process Webshop Payment" := TRUE;
            IF MODIFY(FALSE) THEN
              MESSAGE(Text001);
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterResetInvoicePayment(IsHandled,SalesInvoiceHeader);
    END;

    PROCEDURE ChangeInvoiceOnlinePayment@1140010(SalesInvoiceHeader@1000000002 : Record 112) OK : Boolean;
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000000 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      WebshopShop@1000000003 : Record 5447301;
      SalesInvoiceLine@1000000004 : Record 113;
      ItemList@1000000005 : Text[1024];
      QtyString@1000000006 : Text[30];
      AmountString@1000000007 : Text[30];
      AmountVATString@1000000008 : Text[30];
      MwSt@1000000012 : Text[30];
      OrderDesc@1000000009 : Text[1024];
      Text001@1000000010 : TextConst 'DEU="Standardversand;";ENU="Standard shipping;"';
      WebshopPaymentOption@1000000011 : Record 5447312;
      Text50700@1140000 : TextConst 'DEU=Fehler bei der Zahlung;ENU=Error during payment';
      cMwSt@1140001 : Decimal;
      NettoPrice@1140002 : Decimal;
      IsHandled@1000000013 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeChangeInvoiceOnlinePayment(IsHandled,SalesInvoiceHeader);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Online Zahlung f�r Verkaufsauftr�ge verarbeiten
        OK := FALSE;
        WebshopSetup.GET;
        WITH SalesInvoiceHeader DO BEGIN
          TESTFIELD("Webshop Payment ID");
          TESTFIELD("Webshop Payment Transaction ID");

          IF WebshopShop.GET("Webshop Shop Code") THEN BEGIN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'shop_code',"Webshop Shop Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'language_code',"Webshop Language Code");

            AddVar(CurrRec,'payment_id',"Webshop Payment ID");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'transaction_id',"Webshop Payment Transaction ID");

            CALCFIELDS("Amount Including VAT");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'amount',"Amount Including VAT");

            // Parameter f�r Billpay: InvoiceNo, Delay, ArticleList und OrderDesc
            WebshopPaymentOption.GET("Webshop Shop Code",
                                     "Webshop Language Code",
                                     "Webshop Payment Opt. Line No.");
            IF (WebshopPaymentOption.Checkout = WebshopPaymentOption.Checkout::"Rechnung Billpay") OR
               (WebshopPaymentOption.Checkout = WebshopPaymentOption.Checkout::"Lastschrift Billpay") THEN BEGIN

              // Lines holen
              SalesInvoiceLine.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
              SalesInvoiceLine.SETRANGE(Type,SalesInvoiceLine.Type::Item);
              // Versandkostenzeile ausfiltern
              SalesInvoiceLine.SETFILTER("No.",'<>%1',WebshopShop."Sales No. Shipping Cost");
              SalesInvoiceLine.SETFILTER(Quantity,'>0');
              SalesInvoiceLine.SETFILTER(Amount,'>0');
              IF SalesInvoiceLine.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
                // Artikelnr.
                ItemList += SalesInvoiceLine."No." + ';';
                // Anzahl
                QtyString := STRSUBSTNO('%1;',SalesInvoiceLine.Quantity);
                ItemList += QtyString;
                // Artikelname
                ItemList += SalesInvoiceLine.Description + ';';
                // Artikelbeschreibung
                ItemList += ';'; // Description 2 leer lassen
                // Artikelpreis NETTO

                // Artikelpreis NETTO selbst berechnen
                NettoPrice := SalesInvoiceLine."Unit Price"-(SalesInvoiceLine."Unit Price"/119*19);
                AmountString := STRSUBSTNO('%1;',ROUND(NettoPrice)*100);
                IF STRPOS(AmountString,'.') > 0 THEN
                  AmountString := DELSTR(AmountString,STRPOS(AmountString,'.'),1);
                ItemList +=  AmountString;

                // Artikelpreis NETTO aus der SalesInvoiceLine
                //AmountString := STRSUBSTNO('%1;',(SalesInvoiceLine.Amount*100));
                //IF STRPOS(AmountString,'.') > 0 THEN
                //  AmountString := DELSTR(AmountString,STRPOS(AmountString,'.'),1);
                //ItemList +=  AmountString;

                // Artikelpreis BRUTTO
                AmountVATString := STRSUBSTNO('%1;',(SalesInvoiceLine."Unit Price"*100));
                IF STRPOS(AmountVATString,'.') > 0 THEN
                  AmountVATString := DELSTR(AmountVATString,STRPOS(AmountVATString,'.'),1);
                ItemList += AmountVATString;
                // Trennzeichen
                ItemList += '+';
              UNTIL SalesInvoiceLine.NEXT = 0;

              // Letztes Zeichen (+) Abschneiden
              ItemList := DELSTR(ItemList,STRLEN(ItemList),1);
              AddVar(CurrRec,'itemlist',ItemList);

              // OrderDesc zusammenstellen
              SalesInvoiceLine.RESET;
              SalesInvoiceLine.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
              SalesInvoiceLine.SETRANGE("No.",WebshopShop."Sales No. Shipping Cost");
              SalesInvoiceLine.SETRANGE(Type,SalesInvoiceLine.Type::Resource);
              IF SalesInvoiceLine.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
                // Versandkostenname
                OrderDesc += SalesInvoiceLine.Description + ';';
                // Versandkosten NETTO
                AmountString := STRSUBSTNO('%1;',(SalesInvoiceLine.Amount*100));
                IF STRPOS(AmountString,'.') > 0 THEN
                  AmountString := DELSTR(AmountString,STRPOS(AmountString,'.'),1);
                OrderDesc +=  AmountString;
                // Versandkosten BRUTTO
                AmountVATString := STRSUBSTNO('%1;',(SalesInvoiceLine."Amount Including VAT"*100));
                IF STRPOS(AmountVATString,'.') > 0 THEN
                  AmountVATString := DELSTR(AmountVATString,STRPOS(AmountVATString,'.'),1);
                OrderDesc += AmountVATString;
              END ELSE BEGIN
                OrderDesc += Text001;
                OrderDesc += '0;0;'
              END;

              // Rabatt Netto
              SalesInvoiceLine.RESET;
              SalesInvoiceLine.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
              SalesInvoiceLine.SETRANGE(Type,SalesInvoiceLine.Type::Resource);
              SalesInvoiceLine.SETRANGE("No.",WebshopShop."Sales No. Coupon Discount");
              IF SalesInvoiceLine.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
                AmountString := STRSUBSTNO('%1;',(SalesInvoiceLine.Amount*100));
                IF STRPOS(AmountString,'.') > 0 THEN
                  AmountString := DELSTR(AmountString,STRPOS(AmountString,'.'),1);
                OrderDesc +=  AmountString;
              END ELSE BEGIN
                OrderDesc += '0;';
              END;

              // Rabatt Brutto
              SalesInvoiceLine.RESET;
              SalesInvoiceLine.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
              SalesInvoiceLine.SETRANGE(Type,SalesInvoiceLine.Type::Resource);
              SalesInvoiceLine.SETRANGE("No.",WebshopShop."Sales No. Coupon Discount");
              IF SalesInvoiceLine.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
                AmountVATString := STRSUBSTNO('%1;',(SalesInvoiceLine."Amount Including VAT"*100));
                IF STRPOS(AmountVATString,'.') > 0 THEN
                  AmountVATString := DELSTR(AmountVATString,STRPOS(AmountVATString,'.'),1);
                OrderDesc += AmountVATString;
              END ELSE BEGIN
                OrderDesc += '0;';
              END;

              // Gesamtbetrag NETTO
              CALCFIELDS(Amount);
              AmountString := STRSUBSTNO('%1;',(Amount*100));
              IF STRPOS(AmountString,'.') > 0 THEN
                AmountString := DELSTR(AmountString,STRPOS(AmountString,'.'),1);
              OrderDesc +=  AmountString;

              // Gesamtbetrag BRUTTO
              AmountVATString := STRSUBSTNO('%1;',("Amount Including VAT"*100));
              IF STRPOS(AmountVATString,'.') > 0 THEN
                AmountVATString := DELSTR(AmountVATString,STRPOS(AmountVATString,'.'),1);
              OrderDesc += AmountVATString;

              // OrderDesc anh�ngen
              AddVar(CurrRec,'orderdesc',OrderDesc);
            END;

            //AddVar(CurrRec,'amount',"Amount Including VAT");

            // Zahlung durchf�hren
            GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('change_order_online_payment',CurrRec);
            IF GetRec = '100' THEN BEGIN
              OK := TRUE;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              OK := FALSE;
            END;
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterChangeInvoiceOnlinePayment(IsHandled,SalesInvoiceHeader);
    END;

    PROCEDURE ProcessOrderOnlinePayment@1000000101(SalesHeader@1000000002 : Record 36);
    VAR

      GetRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];

      SalesLine@1000000004 : Record 37;

      QtyString@1000000006 : Text[30];
      AmountString@1000000007 : Text[30];
      AmountVATString@1000000008 : Text[30];
      OrderDesc@1000000009 : Text[1024];
      Found@1000000010 : Boolean;
      IsHandled@1000000011 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeProcessOrderOnlinePayment(IsHandled,SalesHeader);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Online Zahlung f�r Verkaufsauftr�ge verarbeiten
        WebshopSetup.GET;
        WITH SalesHeader DO BEGIN
          TESTFIELD("Webshop Payment ID");
          TESTFIELD("Webshop Payment Transaction ID");
          TESTFIELD(Status,Status::Released);

          IF WebshopShop.GET("Webshop Shop Code") THEN BEGIN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'shop_code',"Webshop Shop Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'language_code',"Webshop Language Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'payment_line_no',"Webshop Payment Opt. Line No.");

            AddVar(CurrRec,'payment_id',"Webshop Payment ID");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'transaction_id',"Webshop Payment Transaction ID");
            CALCFIELDS("Amount Including VAT");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'amount',"Amount Including VAT");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'reference_no',"No." + ' - ' + "Webshop Order No.");

            //Billpay ---
            // Parameter f�r Billpay: InvoiceNo, Delay, ArticleList und OrderDesc
            AddVar(CurrRec,'invoice_no',"No.");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'delay','0');

            // Lines holen, ohne Versandkosten
            SalesLine.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
            SalesLine.SETRANGE(Type,SalesLine.Type::Item);
            SalesLine.SETFILTER("No.",'<>%1',WebshopShop."Sales No. Shipping Cost");
            SalesLine.SETFILTER(Quantity,'<%1',0);
            IF SalesLine.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
              ItemList += SalesLine."No." + ';';
              QtyString := STRSUBSTNO('%1;',SalesLine.Quantity);
              ItemList += QtyString;
              ItemList += SalesLine."No." + ';';
              ItemList += ''''';';
              AmountString := STRSUBSTNO('%1;',(SalesLine.Amount*100));
              IF STRPOS(AmountString,'.') > 0 THEN
                AmountString := DELSTR(AmountString,STRPOS(AmountString,'.'),1);
              ItemList +=  AmountString;
              AmountVATString := STRSUBSTNO('%1;',(SalesLine."Amount Including VAT"*100));
              IF STRPOS(AmountVATString,'.') > 0 THEN
                AmountVATString := DELSTR(AmountVATString,STRPOS(AmountVATString,'.'),1);
              ItemList += AmountVATString;
              ItemList += '+';
            UNTIL SalesLine.NEXT = 0;

            // Letztes Zeichen (+) Abschneiden
            ItemList := DELSTR(ItemList,STRLEN(ItemList),1);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'itemlist',ItemList);

            // OrderDesc (Versandkosten, Gesamtsumme, etc.) zusammenstellen
            Found := FALSE;
            SalesLine.RESET;
            SalesLine.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
            SalesLine.SETRANGE("No.",WebshopShop."Sales No. Shipping Cost");
            SalesLine.SETRANGE(Type,SalesLine.Type::Item);
              IF SalesLine.FINDLAST THEN REPEAT
              Found := TRUE;
              OrderDesc += SalesLine.Description + ';';
              AmountString := STRSUBSTNO('%1;',(SalesLine.Amount*100));
              IF STRPOS(AmountString,'.') > 0 THEN
                AmountString := DELSTR(AmountString,STRPOS(AmountString,'.'),1);
              OrderDesc +=  AmountString;
              AmountVATString := STRSUBSTNO('%1;',(SalesLine."Amount Including VAT"*100));
              IF STRPOS(AmountVATString,'.') > 0 THEN
                AmountVATString := DELSTR(AmountVATString,STRPOS(AmountVATString,'.'),1);
              OrderDesc += AmountVATString;
            UNTIL SalesLine.NEXT = 0;
            IF Found = FALSE THEN BEGIN
              OrderDesc += Text001+';';
              OrderDesc += '0;0;'
            END;

            OrderDesc += '0;';    // Rabatt Netto
            OrderDesc += '0;';    // Rabatt Brutto
            CALCFIELDS(Amount);

            AmountString := STRSUBSTNO('%1;',(Amount*100));
            IF STRPOS(AmountString,'.') > 0 THEN
              AmountString := DELSTR(AmountString,STRPOS(AmountString,'.'),1);
            OrderDesc +=  AmountString;
            AmountVATString := STRSUBSTNO('%1;',("Amount Including VAT"*100));
            IF STRPOS(AmountVATString,'.') > 0 THEN
              AmountVATString := DELSTR(AmountVATString,STRPOS(AmountVATString,'.'),1);
            OrderDesc += AmountVATString;
            AddVar(CurrRec,'orderdesc',OrderDesc);
            // Billpay +++

            // Zahlung durchf�hren
            GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('process_online_payment',CurrRec);
            IF GetRec = '100' THEN BEGIN
              "Process Webshop Payment" := FALSE;
              "Date Webshop Payment Processed" := CURRENTDATETIME;
              "Webshop Payment Error" := '';
            END ELSE BEGIN
              "Process Webshop Payment" := FALSE;
              "Clear Webshop Payment" := FALSE;
              "Date Webshop Payment Processed" := 0DT;
              "Webshop Payment Error" := GetRec;
            END;
            MODIFY(FALSE);
            COMMIT;
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterProcessOrderOnlinePayment(IsHandled,SalesHeader);
    END;

    PROCEDURE ProcessAllInvoiceOnlinePayment@1000000100(CountRecords@1000000000 : Boolean) OK : Boolean;
    VAR
      SalesInvoiceHeader@1000000001 : Record 112;
      IsHandled@1000000002 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeProcessAllInvoiceOnlinePayment(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Alle Online-Zahlungen f�r Geb. Verkaufsrechnungen verarbeiten

        WITH SalesInvoiceHeader DO BEGIN
          SETCURRENTKEY("Webshop Order No.","Process Webshop Payment","Date Webshop Payment Processed","Webshop Payment Transaction ID","Webshop Payment ID");
          SETFILTER("Webshop Order No.",'<>%1','');
          SETRANGE("Process Webshop Payment",TRUE);
          SETRANGE("Date Webshop Payment Processed",0DT);
          SETFILTER("Webshop Payment Transaction ID",'<>%1','');
          SETFILTER("Webshop Payment ID",'<>%1','');
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
            IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              ProcessInvoiceOnlinePayment(SalesInvoiceHeader);
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            UNTIL NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterProcessAllInvoiceOnlinePayment(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE ProcessInvoiceOnlinePayment@1000000099(ParameterSalesInvoiceHeader@1000000002 : Record 112);
    VAR
      SalesHeader@1000000003 : Record 36;
      CurrRec@1000000000 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      SalesInvoiceHeader@1000000004 : Record 112;
      SalesInvoiceLine@1000000005 : Record 113;
      ItemList@1000000006 : Text[1024];
      QtyString@1000000007 : Text[30];
      AmountString@1000000008 : Text[30];
      AmountVATString@1000000009 : Text[30];
      WebshopShop@1000000010 : Record 5447301;
      OrderDesc@1000000011 : Text[1024];
      Found@1000000013 : Boolean;
      Pos@1000000012 : Integer;
      IsHandled@1000000014 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeProcessInvoiceOnlinePayment(IsHandled,ParameterSalesInvoiceHeader);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Online Zahlung f�r Verkaufsrechnung verarbeiten

        WebshopSetup.GET;
        WITH SalesInvoiceHeader DO BEGIN
          GET(ParameterSalesInvoiceHeader."No.");

          TESTFIELD("Webshop Payment ID");
          TESTFIELD("Webshop Payment Transaction ID");

          IF WebshopShop.GET("Webshop Shop Code") THEN BEGIN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'shop_code',"Webshop Shop Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'language_code',"Webshop Language Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'payment_line_no',"Webshop Payment Opt. Line No.");

            AddVar(CurrRec,'payment_id',"Webshop Payment ID");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'transaction_id',"Webshop Payment Transaction ID");
            CALCFIELDS("Amount Including VAT");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'amount',"Amount Including VAT");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'reference_no',"No." + ' - ' + "Webshop Order No.");

            // Billpay ---
            // Parameter f�r Billpay: InvoiceNo, Delay, ArticleList und OrderDesc
            AddVar(CurrRec,'invoice_no',"No.");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'delay','0');

            // Lines holen, ohne Versandkosten
            SalesInvoiceLine.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
            SalesInvoiceLine.SETRANGE(Type,SalesInvoiceLine.Type::Item);
            SalesInvoiceLine.SETFILTER("No.",'<>%1',WebshopShop."Sales No. Shipping Cost");
            SalesInvoiceLine.SETFILTER(Quantity,'>%1',0);
            IF SalesInvoiceLine.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
              ItemList += SalesInvoiceLine."No." + ';';
              QtyString := STRSUBSTNO('%1;',SalesInvoiceLine.Quantity);
              ItemList += QtyString;
              ItemList += SalesInvoiceLine."No." + ';';
              ItemList += ';';
              AmountString := STRSUBSTNO('%1;',((SalesInvoiceLine.Amount / SalesInvoiceLine.Quantity)*100));
              Pos := STRPOS(AmountString,',');
              IF Pos > 0 THEN
                AmountString := STRSUBSTNO('%1;',COPYSTR(AmountString,1,Pos-1));
              IF STRPOS(AmountString,'.') > 0 THEN
                AmountString := DELSTR(AmountString,STRPOS(AmountString,'.'),1);
              ItemList +=  AmountString;
              AmountVATString := STRSUBSTNO('%1;',((SalesInvoiceLine."Amount Including VAT" / SalesInvoiceLine.Quantity)*100));
              IF STRPOS(AmountVATString,'.') > 0 THEN
                AmountVATString := DELSTR(AmountVATString,STRPOS(AmountVATString,'.'),1);
              ItemList += AmountVATString;
              ItemList += '+';
            UNTIL SalesInvoiceLine.NEXT = 0;

            // Letztes Zeichen (+) Abschneiden
            IF ItemList <> '' THEN
              ItemList := DELSTR(ItemList,STRLEN(ItemList),1);

            AddVar(CurrRec,'itemlist',ItemList);

            // OrderDesc (Versandkosten, etc) zusammenstellen
            Found :=FALSE;
            SalesInvoiceLine.RESET;
            SalesInvoiceLine.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
            SalesInvoiceLine.SETRANGE("No.",WebshopShop."Sales No. Shipping Cost");
            SalesInvoiceLine.SETRANGE(Type,SalesInvoiceLine.Type::Item);
            IF SalesInvoiceLine.FINDLAST THEN REPEAT
              Found :=TRUE;
              OrderDesc += SalesInvoiceLine.Description + ';';
              AmountString := STRSUBSTNO('%1;',((SalesInvoiceLine.Amount / SalesInvoiceLine.Quantity)*100));
              IF STRPOS(AmountString,'.') > 0 THEN
                AmountString := DELSTR(AmountString,STRPOS(AmountString,'.'),1);
              OrderDesc +=  AmountString;
              AmountVATString := STRSUBSTNO('%1;',((SalesInvoiceLine."Amount Including VAT" / SalesInvoiceLine.Quantity)*100));
              IF STRPOS(AmountVATString,'.') > 0 THEN
                AmountVATString := DELSTR(AmountVATString,STRPOS(AmountVATString,'.'),1);
              OrderDesc += AmountVATString;
            UNTIL SalesInvoiceLine.NEXT = 0;
            IF NOT Found = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              OrderDesc += Text001 + ';';
              OrderDesc += '0;0;'
            END;

            OrderDesc += '0;';    // Rabatt Netto
            OrderDesc += '0;';    // Rabatt Brutto
            CALCFIELDS(Amount);
            AmountString := '0;';
            IF STRPOS(AmountString,'.') > 0 THEN
              AmountString := DELSTR(AmountString,STRPOS(AmountString,'.'),1);
            OrderDesc +=  AmountString;
            AmountVATString := STRSUBSTNO('%1;',("Amount Including VAT"*100));
            IF STRPOS(AmountVATString,'.') > 0 THEN
              AmountVATString := DELSTR(AmountVATString,STRPOS(AmountVATString,'.'),1);
            OrderDesc += AmountVATString;
            AddVar(CurrRec,'orderdesc',OrderDesc);
            // Billpay +++

            // Zahlung durchf�hren
            GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('process_online_payment',CurrRec);
            IF GetRec = '100' THEN BEGIN
              "Process Webshop Payment" := FALSE;
              "Date Webshop Payment Processed" := CURRENTDATETIME;
              "Webshop Payment Error" := '';
            END ELSE BEGIN
              "Process Webshop Payment" := FALSE;
              "Clear Webshop Payment" := FALSE;
              "Date Webshop Payment Processed" := 0DT;
              "Webshop Payment Error" := GetRec;
            END;
            MODIFY(FALSE);
            COMMIT;
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterProcessInvoiceOnlinePayment(IsHandled,ParameterSalesInvoiceHeader);
    END;

    PROCEDURE ProcessCrMemoOnlinePayment@1000000095(ParameterSalesCrMemoHeader@1000000002 : Record 114);
    VAR
      SalesHeader@1000000003 : Record 36;
      CurrRec@1000000000 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      SalesCrMemoHeader@1000000004 : Record 114;
      SalesCrMemoLine@1000000005 : Record 115;
      WebshopShop@1000000006 : Record 5447301;
      ItemList@1000000007 : Text[1024];
      QtyString@1000000008 : Text[30];
      SalesInvoiceHeader@1000000009 : Record 112;
      SalesInvoiceLine@1000000010 : Record 113;
      AmountString@1000000011 : Text[30];
      AmountVATString@1000000012 : Text[30];
      IsHandled@1000000013 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeProcessCrMemoOnlinePayment(IsHandled,ParameterSalesCrMemoHeader);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN


        // Online Zahlung f�r gebuchte Verkaufsgutschrift verarbeiten
        WebshopSetup.GET;
        WITH SalesCrMemoHeader DO BEGIN
          GET(ParameterSalesCrMemoHeader."No.");
          TESTFIELD("Webshop Payment ID");
          TESTFIELD("Webshop Payment Transaction ID");

          IF WebshopShop.GET("Webshop Shop Code") THEN BEGIN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'shop_code',"Webshop Shop Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'language_code',"Webshop Language Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'payment_line_no',"Webshop Payment Opt. Line No.");

            AddVar(CurrRec,'payment_id',"Webshop Payment ID");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'transaction_id',"Webshop Payment Transaction ID");
            CALCFIELDS("Amount Including VAT");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'amount',-"Amount Including VAT");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'reference_no',"No." + ' - ' + "Webshop Order No.");

            // Urspr�ngliche Rechnung holen
            SalesCrMemoLine.RESET;
            SalesCrMemoLine.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
            SalesCrMemoLine.SETRANGE(Type,SalesCrMemoLine.Type::Item);
            SalesCrMemoLine.SETFILTER(Quantity,'>%1',0);
            IF SalesCrMemoLine.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
              // Artikel nicht in selber Anzahl und Menge in der Gutschrift vorhanden
              // -> in die ItemList schreiben
              ItemList += SalesCrMemoLine."No." + ';';
              QtyString := STRSUBSTNO('%1;',SalesCrMemoLine.Quantity);
              ItemList += QtyString;
              ItemList += '+';
            UNTIL SalesCrMemoLine.NEXT = 0;
            IF ItemList <> '' THEN
              ItemList := DELSTR(ItemList,STRLEN(ItemList),1);

            AddVar(CurrRec,'itemlist',ItemList);

            GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('process_online_payment',CurrRec);

            IF GetRec = '100' THEN BEGIN
              "Process Webshop Payment" := FALSE;
              "Date Webshop Payment Processed" := CURRENTDATETIME;
              "Webshop Payment Error" := '';
            END ELSE BEGIN
              "Process Webshop Payment" := FALSE;
              "Clear Webshop Payment" := FALSE;
              "Date Webshop Payment Processed" := 0DT;
              "Webshop Payment Error" := GetRec;
            END;
            MODIFY(FALSE);
            COMMIT;
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterProcessCrMemoOnlinePayment(IsHandled,ParameterSalesCrMemoHeader);
    END;

    PROCEDURE ClearOrderOnlinePayment@1000000086(SalesHeader@1000000000 : Record 36);
    VAR
      JournalLine@1000000005 : Record 81;
      PaymentOption@1000000001 : Record 5447312;
      NextLineNo@1000000004 : Integer;
      Text001@1000000003 : TextConst 'DEU=Auftrag %1 - %2 - %3;ENU=Order %1 - %2 - %3';
      Text002@1000000006 : TextConst 'DEU=Zahlungsart nicht eingerichtet;ENU=Payment option has no setup';
      IsHandled@1000000002 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeClearOrderOnlinePayment(IsHandled,SalesHeader);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Zahlung f�r Auftrag automatisch in Zahlungsbuchblatt �bergeben

        WITH SalesHeader DO BEGIN
           TESTFIELD("Webshop Order No.");
           TESTFIELD("Webshop Payment Opt. Line No.");
           TESTFIELD(Status,Status::Released);
           IF PaymentOption.GET("Webshop Shop Code","Webshop Language Code","Webshop Payment Opt. Line No.") THEN BEGIN
            IF (PaymentOption."Gen. Journal Template" <> '') AND (PaymentOption."Gen. Journal Batch" <> '') THEN BEGIN

              // N�chste Zeilennr. ermitteln
              JournalLine.SETRANGE("Journal Template Name",PaymentOption."Gen. Journal Template");
              JournalLine.SETRANGE("Journal Batch Name",PaymentOption."Gen. Journal Batch");
              IF JournalLine.FINDLAST THEN
                NextLineNo := JournalLine."Line No." + 10000
              ELSE
                NextLineNo := 10000;

              // Zahlungsbuchungsblatt-Zeile schreiben
              JournalLine.INIT;
              JournalLine."Line No." := NextLineNo;
              JournalLine.VALIDATE("Journal Template Name",PaymentOption."Gen. Journal Template");
              JournalLine.VALIDATE("Journal Batch Name",PaymentOption."Gen. Journal Batch");
              JournalLine.INSERT(TRUE);
              JournalLine.VALIDATE("Account Type",JournalLine."Account Type"::Customer);
              JournalLine.VALIDATE("Account No.","Bill-to Customer No.");
              JournalLine.VALIDATE("Posting Date","Posting Date");
              JournalLine.VALIDATE("Document Type",JournalLine."Document Type"::Payment);
              JournalLine.VALIDATE("Document No.",'Z-' + "Webshop Order No.");
              JournalLine.VALIDATE(Description,COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(Text001,"No.","Webshop Order No.",
                                   "Webshop Payment Transaction ID"),1,50));
              IF PaymentOption."G/L Account Clearing" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                JournalLine.VALIDATE("Bal. Account Type",JournalLine."Bal. Account Type"::"G/L Account");
                JournalLine.VALIDATE("Bal. Account No.",PaymentOption."G/L Account Clearing");
              END;
              CALCFIELDS("Amount Including VAT");
              JournalLine.VALIDATE(Amount,-"Amount Including VAT");
              JournalLine.MODIFY(TRUE);

              // Auftrag als Zahlung ausgebucht markieren
              "Clear Webshop Payment" := FALSE;
              "Date Webshop Payment Cleared" := CURRENTDATETIME;
              MODIFY(FALSE);
            END ELSE BEGIN
              ERROR(Text002);
            END;
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterClearOrderOnlinePayment(IsHandled,SalesHeader);
    END;

    PROCEDURE ClearCrMemoOnlinePayment@1000000091(ParameterSalesCrMemoHeader@1000000006 : Record 114);
    VAR
      JournalLine@1000000005 : Record 81;
      PaymentOption@1000000000 : Record 5447312;
      NextLineNo@1000000004 : Integer;
      Text001@1000000003 : TextConst 'DEU=%1 - %2 - %3;ENU=%1 - %2 - %3';

           GET(ParameterSalesCrMemoHeader."No.");
           TESTFIELD("Webshop Order No.");
           IF PaymentOption.GET("Webshop Shop Code","Webshop Language Code","Webshop Payment Opt. Line No.") THEN BEGIN
             IF (PaymentOption."Gen. Journal Template" <> '') AND (PaymentOption."Gen. Journal Batch" <> '') THEN BEGIN

              // N�chste Zeilennr. ermitteln
              JournalLine.SETRANGE("Journal Template Name",PaymentOption."Gen. Journal Template");
              JournalLine.SETRANGE("Journal Batch Name",PaymentOption."Gen. Journal Batch");
              IF JournalLine.FINDLAST THEN
                NextLineNo := JournalLine."Line No." + 10000
              ELSE
                NextLineNo := 10000;

              // Zahlungsbuchungsblatt-Zeile schreiben
              JournalLine.INIT;
              JournalLine."Line No." := NextLineNo;
              JournalLine.VALIDATE("Journal Template Name",PaymentOption."Gen. Journal Template");
              JournalLine.VALIDATE("Journal Batch Name",PaymentOption."Gen. Journal Batch");
              JournalLine.INSERT(TRUE);
              JournalLine.VALIDATE("Account Type",JournalLine."Account Type"::Customer);
              JournalLine.VALIDATE("Account No.","Sell-to Customer No.");
              JournalLine.VALIDATE("Posting Date","Posting Date");
              JournalLine.VALIDATE("Document Type",JournalLine."Document Type"::Payment);
              JournalLine.VALIDATE("Document No.",'Z-' + "Webshop Order No.");
              JournalLine.VALIDATE("Applies-to Doc. Type",JournalLine."Applies-to Doc. Type"::"Credit Memo");
              JournalLine.VALIDATE("Applies-to Doc. No.","No.");
              JournalLine.VALIDATE(Description,COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(Text001,"Webshop Order No.",
                                   "Webshop Payment Transaction ID","Bill-to Name"),1,50));
              IF PaymentOption."G/L Account Clearing" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                JournalLine.VALIDATE("Bal. Account Type",JournalLine."Bal. Account Type"::"G/L Account");
                JournalLine.VALIDATE("Bal. Account No.",PaymentOption."G/L Account Clearing");
              END;
              CALCFIELDS("Amount Including VAT");
              JournalLine.VALIDATE(Amount,"Amount Including VAT");
              JournalLine.MODIFY(TRUE);

              // Rechnung als Zahlung ausgebucht markieren
              "Clear Webshop Payment" := FALSE;
              "Date Webshop Payment Cleared" := CURRENTDATETIME;
              MODIFY(FALSE);
            END;
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterClearCrMemoOnlinePayment(IsHandled,ParameterSalesCrMemoHeader);
    END;

    PROCEDURE "--- Gutscheine ---"@1000000084();
    BEGIN
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllCouponHeader@1118900036(CountRecords@1118900000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      CouponHeader@1118900002 : Record 5447471;
      CouponLine@1000000000 : Record 5447472;
      IsHandled@1000000001 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateAllCouponHeader(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Alle Coupon-Header �bertragen
        WITH CouponHeader DO BEGIN
          //SETRANGE(Active,TRUE);
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
            //Alle Zeilen ohne Eingabecode l�schen
            CouponLine.RESET;
            CouponLine.SETFILTER("Coupon Code",'%1','');
            IF NOT CouponLine.ISEMPTY THEN
              CouponLine.DELETEALL(TRUE);

            IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              //Alle �bertragen
              IF ("Last Date Modified" = 0DT) OR ("Last Date Updated" < "Last Date Modified") THEN
                UpdateCouponHeader(CouponHeader);
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            UNTIL NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateAllCouponHeader(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateCouponHeader@1118900037(ParameterCouponHeader@1118900000 : Record 5447471);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1118900002 : Text[1024];
      CouponLine@1118900001 : Record 5447472;
      CouponHeader@1000000000 : Record 5447471;
      IsHandled@1000000001 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateCouponHeader(IsHandled,ParameterCouponHeader);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Einzelenen Coupon-Header �bertragen
        WebshopSetup.GET;
        WITH CouponHeader DO BEGIN
          GET(ParameterCouponHeader.Code);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_coupon_header');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[description]',Description);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[value_type]',"Value Type");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[value_coupon]',"Value Coupon");

          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


          //zugeh�rige Zeilen �bertragen
          CouponLine.SETRANGE(Code,Code);
          IF CouponLine.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            IF (CouponLine."Last Date Updated" = 0DT) OR (CouponLine."Last Date Updated" < CouponLine."Last Date Modified") THEN
              UpdateCouponLine(CouponLine);
          UNTIL CouponLine.NEXT = 0;

          "Last Date Updated" := CURRENTDATETIME;
          MODIFY(FALSE);

        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateCouponHeader(IsHandled,ParameterCouponHeader);
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteAllCouponHeader@1118900038(ConfirmDelete@1118900000 : Boolean) OK : Boolean;

        ELSE
          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('delete',CurrRec);

      END;
      OnAfterDeleteAllCouponHeader(ConfirmDelete,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateCouponLine@1118900039(ParameterCouponLine@1118900000 : Record 5447472) OK : Boolean;
    VAR
      CurrRec@1118900001 : Text[1024];
      CouponLine@1000000000 : Record 5447472;
      IsHandled@1000000001 : Boolean;
      WebshopCouponLine@1000000002 : Record 5447329;
      WebshopCouponShippingLine@1000000003 : Record 5447330;
      CouponHeader@1000000004 : Record 5447471;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateCouponLine(IsHandled,ParameterCouponLine);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Einzelene Coupon-Line aus und neu �bertragen
        WebshopSetup.GET;
        WITH CouponLine DO BEGIN
          GET(ParameterCouponLine.Code,ParameterCouponLine."Coupon Code");

          CALCFIELDS(Amount,"Times Used");

          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_coupon_line');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[coupon_code]',"Coupon Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[customer_no]',"Customer No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[times_used]',"Times Used");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[max_no_of_usage]',"Max No. of Uses");
          IF "Value Coupon" THEN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[amount]',Amount)
          ELSE
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[amount]',"Amount Editable");

          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[last_date_used]',"Date Last Used");
          IF "Value Type" <> "Value Type Shop" THEN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[value_type]',"Value Type Shop")
          ELSE
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[value_type]',"Value Type");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[percentage]',Percentage);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[item_no]',"Item No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[valid_from]',"Starting Date");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[valid_to]',"Ending Date");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[amount_from]',"Amount From");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[value_coupon]',"Value Coupon");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[active]',Status);
          IF CouponHeader.GET(Code) THEN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[coupon_group]',CouponHeader."Coupon Group");

          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

          WebshopCouponLine.SETRANGE(Code,Code);
          WebshopCouponLine.SETRANGE("Coupon Code","Coupon Code");
          IF WebshopCouponLine.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            UpdateWebshopCouponLine(WebshopCouponLine);
          UNTIL WebshopCouponLine.NEXT = 0;

          WebshopCouponShippingLine.SETRANGE(Code,Code);
          WebshopCouponShippingLine.SETRANGE("Coupon Code","Coupon Code");
          IF WebshopCouponShippingLine.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            UpdateWebshopCouponShippingLine(WebshopCouponShippingLine);
          UNTIL WebshopCouponShippingLine.NEXT = 0;

          "Last Date Updated" := CURRENTDATETIME;
          MODIFY(FALSE);

        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateCouponLine(IsHandled,ParameterCouponLine);
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteAllCouponLine@1118900040(ConfirmDelete@1118900000 : Boolean;CouponCode@1000000000 : Code[20]) OK : Boolean;

    PROCEDURE GetAllCouponLine@1118900045(CountRecords@1118900000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1118900001 : Text[1024];
      NoOfRec@1118900002 : Integer;
      Text001@1118900003 : TextConst 'DEU=Gutscheine abholen;ENU=Fetch coupons';
      GetResponseText@1000 : Text;
      LogFile@1001 : File;
      IsHandled@1000000000 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeGetAllCouponLine(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        //Ge�nderte CouponLines aus Web holen

        WebshopSetup.GET;
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_coupon_line');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[update_insert]','1');
        GetResponseText := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_no_of_records',CurrRec);
        IF EVALUATE(NoOfRec,GetResponseText) THEN BEGIN
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(Text001,NoOfRec);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(Text001,NoOfRec);
            GetCouponLine;
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
            ERROR(GetResponseText)
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
              LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
              LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
              IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
               IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                ERROR(Error001);
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                  ERROR(Error002);
              END;
              LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
              LogFile.WRITE(GetResponseText);
              LogFile.CLOSE;
              IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                MESSAGE(GetResponseText);
            END ELSE
              ERROR(Error003);
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterGetAllCouponLine(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetCouponLine@1118900044() NoOfRequests : Integer;
    VAR
      SetCurrRec@1118900000 : Text[1024];
      GetCurrRec@1118900001 : Text[1024];
      CurrRec@1118900002 : Text[1024];
      CouponLine@1118900003 : Record 5447472;
      Delimiter@1118900004 : Text[10];
      NextCouponLine@1118900005 : Boolean;
      CouponLineId@1118900006 : Code[20];
      CouponLineCode@1118900007 : Code[20];
      CouponLineCouponCode@1118900008 : Code[20];
      ShopCode@1000000000 : Code[10];
      LanguageCode@1000000001 : Code[10];
      LogFile@1000 : File;
      IsHandled@1000000002 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeGetCouponLine(IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        //CouponLines aus Web aktualisieren(Restwerte,Letzte Benutzung,Anzahl Benutzungen)
        NoOfRequests := 0;
        Delimiter := '-;-';
        WebshopSetup.GET;

        CLEAR(GetCurrRec);
        AddVar(GetCurrRec,'table','shop_coupon_line');
        AddVar(GetCurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(GetCurrRec,'filter[update_insert]','1');
        AddVar(GetCurrRec,'delimiter',Delimiter);
        AddVar(GetCurrRec,'fields','id,code,coupon_code,times_used,last_date_used,amount_left');
        REPEAT
          NextCouponLine := FALSE;
          CurrRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_content',GetCurrRec);
          IF STRPOS(CurrRec,Delimiter) <> 0 THEN BEGIN
            CouponLineId := WebshopManagement.Explode(CurrRec,Delimiter);
            CouponLineCode := WebshopManagement.Explode(CurrRec,Delimiter);
            CouponLineCouponCode := WebshopManagement.Explode(CurrRec,Delimiter);
            ShopCode := WebshopManagement.Explode(CurrRec,Delimiter);
            LanguageCode := WebshopManagement.Explode(CurrRec,Delimiter);
            IF (CouponLineId <> '') AND (CouponLineCode <> '') AND (CouponLineCouponCode <> '') THEN BEGIN
              IF CouponLine.GET(CouponLineCode,CouponLineCouponCode,ShopCode,LanguageCode) THEN BEGIN
                EVALUATE(CouponLine."Times Used",WebshopManagement.Explode(CurrRec,Delimiter));
                EVALUATE(CouponLine."Date Last Used",WebshopManagement.Explode(CurrRec,Delimiter));
                EVALUATE(CouponLine.Percentage,WebshopManagement.Explode(CurrRec,Delimiter));
                CouponLine.MODIFY(TRUE);
              END ELSE BEGIN
                CouponLine.INIT();
                EVALUATE(CouponLine.Code,CouponLineCode);
                EVALUATE(CouponLine."Coupon Code",CouponLineCouponCode);
                EVALUATE(CouponLine."Times Used",WebshopManagement.Explode(CurrRec,Delimiter));
                EVALUATE(CouponLine."Date Last Used",WebshopManagement.Explode(CurrRec,Delimiter));
                EVALUATE(CouponLine.Amount,WebshopManagement.Explode(CurrRec,Delimiter));
                CouponLine.Percentage := CouponLine.Amount;
                CouponLine.INSERT(TRUE);
                COMMIT;
              END;
              CLEAR(SetCurrRec);
              AddVar(SetCurrRec,'table','shop_coupon_line');
              AddVar(SetCurrRec,'primary[id]',CouponLineId);
              AddVar(SetCurrRec,'data[update_insert]',FALSE);
              IF WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',SetCurrRec) THEN
                NextCouponLine := TRUE;
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF CurrRec<>'0' THEN BEGIN
              IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
                ERROR(CurrRec)
              ELSE BEGIN
                IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
                  LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                  LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                  IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
                   IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                    ERROR(Error001);
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                      ERROR(Error002);
                  END;
                  LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                  LogFile.WRITE(CurrRec);
                  LogFile.CLOSE;
                  IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                    MESSAGE(CurrRec);
                END ELSE
                  ERROR(Error003);
              END;
            END;
          END;
        UNTIL NOT NextCouponLine;

      END;
      OnAfterGetCouponLine(IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE PostCouponLines@1118900046(CouponHeader@1118900000 : Record 5447471);
    VAR
      CouponLine@1118900001 : Record 5447472;
      JournalLine@1118900002 : Record 81;
      LineNo@1118900003 : Integer;
      CuBatch@1118900004 : Codeunit 13;
      Text001@1000000001 : TextConst 'DEU=Gutschein;ENU=Coupon';
      IsHandled@1000000002 : Boolean;
      CouponSetup@1000000000 : Record 5447470;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforePostCouponLines(IsHandled,CouponHeader);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        //Wertgutscheine verbuchen
        IF CouponHeader."Value Coupon" = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          CouponSetup.GET;
          CouponLine.SETRANGE(Code,CouponHeader.Code);
          CouponLine.SETRANGE("Is Posted",FALSE);
          CouponLine.SETFILTER(Amount,'<>0');

          IF CouponLine.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            JournalLine.SETRANGE("Journal Template Name",CouponSetup."Coupon Journal Template");
            JournalLine.SETRANGE("Journal Batch Name",CouponSetup."Coupon Journal Name");
            IF JournalLine.FINDLAST THEN
              LineNo := JournalLine."Line No." +10000
            ELSE
              LineNo := 10000;
            JournalLine.RESET;
            JournalLine.INIT;
            JournalLine.VALIDATE("Journal Template Name",CouponSetup."Coupon Journal Template");
            JournalLine.VALIDATE("Journal Batch Name",CouponSetup."Coupon Journal Name");
            JournalLine.VALIDATE("Line No.",LineNo);
            JournalLine.SetUpNewLine(JournalLine,0,FALSE);
            JournalLine.INSERT(TRUE);
            JournalLine.VALIDATE("Document No.",CouponLine."Coupon Code");
            JournalLine.VALIDATE("Account Type",JournalLine."Account Type"::"G/L Account");
            JournalLine.VALIDATE("Account No.",CouponSetup."Coupon Posting Acc. No.");
            JournalLine.VALIDATE("Posting Date",TODAY);
            JournalLine.Description := Text001 + ' ' + CouponLine."Coupon Code";
            JournalLine.VALIDATE(Amount,CouponLine.Amount);
            JournalLine.MODIFY(TRUE);

            CouponLine."Is Posted" := TRUE;
            CouponLine.MODIFY(TRUE);
          UNTIL CouponLine.NEXT = 0;

          JournalLine.SETRANGE("Journal Template Name",CouponSetup."Coupon Journal Template");
          JournalLine.SETRANGE("Journal Batch Name",CouponSetup."Coupon Journal Name");
          CuBatch.RUN(JournalLine);
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterPostCouponLines(IsHandled,CouponHeader);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckDCFiles@1000000135(WebshopLanguage@1000000000 : Record 5447302;"No."@1000000002 : Integer) Ok : Boolean;
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000004 : Text[1024];
      Field@1000000003 : Text[30];
      IsHandled@1000000005 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeCheckDCFiles(IsHandled,WebshopLanguage,"No.");
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Dateien an Webshop-Kategorie pr�fen

        WITH WebshopLanguage DO BEGIN
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_language');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'field',STRSUBSTNO('digital_coupon_background_%1',"No."));
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
          GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('check_field_content',CurrRec);
          IF GetRec = '' THEN
            Ok := FALSE
          ELSE IF GetRec = '100' THEN
            Ok := TRUE
          ELSE
            ERROR(GetRec);
          CASE "No." OF
            1: "Digital Coupon Background 1" := Ok;
            2: "Digital Coupon Background 2" := Ok;
            3: "Digital Coupon Background 3" := Ok;
            4: "Digital Coupon Background 4" := Ok;
            5: "Digital Coupon Background 5" := Ok;
          END;
          MODIFY;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterCheckDCFiles(IsHandled,WebshopLanguage,"No.");
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckCouponOrders@1000000125(CouponCode@1000000004 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      NoOfOrder@1000000003 : Integer;
      CurrRec@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      GetResponseText@1000000001 : Text;
      LogFile@1000000000 : File;
      Text001@1000000005 : TextConst 'DEU=Bestellungen abrufen;ENU=Fetch Orders';
      Text002@1000000006 : TextConst 'DEU=Pr�fe Coupon;ENU=Check Coupon';
      IsHandled@1000000007 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeCheckCouponOrders(IsHandled,CouponCode);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        IF CouponCode <> '' THEN BEGIN
          WebshopSetup.GET;
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_sales_header');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[update_insert]',TRUE);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[coupon_code]',CouponCode);
          GetResponseText := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_no_of_records',CurrRec);
          IF EVALUATE(NoOfOrder,GetResponseText) THEN BEGIN
            IF NoOfOrder > 0 THEN BEGIN
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorOpen(Text002);
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(Text001,NoOfOrder);
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(Text001,NoOfOrder);
              GetSalesHeader(CouponCode,'');
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorClose;
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
              ERROR(GetResponseText)
            ELSE BEGIN
              IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
                LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
                 IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                  ERROR(Error001);
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                    ERROR(Error002);
                END;
                LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                LogFile.WRITE(GetResponseText);
                LogFile.CLOSE;
                IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                  MESSAGE(GetResponseText);
              END ELSE
                ERROR(Error003);
            END;
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterCheckCouponOrders(IsHandled,CouponCode);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllPermissionGroup@1000000059(CountRecords@1118900000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      PermissionGroup@1118900002 : Record 5447336;
      IsHandled@1000000000 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateAllPermissionGroup(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Alle Berechtigungsgruppen �bertragen
        WITH PermissionGroup DO BEGIN
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
            DeleteAllPermissionGroup(FALSE);
            IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              UpdatePermissionGroup(PermissionGroup);
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            UNTIL NEXT = 0;
            DeleteAllPermissionGroup(TRUE);
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateAllPermissionGroup(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SwapCategoryPermissions@1000000134(CategoryShop@1000000000 : Code[20];CategoryLanguage@1000000001 : Code[10];FromCategoryLineNo@1000000002 : Integer;ToCategoryLineNo@1000000005 : Integer);
    VAR
      tempPermLink@1000000003 : TEMPORARY Record 5447337;
      physPermLink@1000000004 : Record 5447337;
      CurrRec@1000000006 : Text[1024];
      IsHandled@1000000007 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeSwapCategoryPermissions(IsHandled,CategoryShop,CategoryLanguage,FromCategoryLineNo,ToCategoryLineNo);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        //Schreibt Berechtigungsverkn�pfungen bei Kategorieverschiebung um
        physPermLink.SETFILTER(Type,'%1|%2',physPermLink.Type::CustomerCategory,physPermLink.Type::CategoryGroup);
        physPermLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code",CategoryShop);
        physPermLink.SETRANGE("Language Code",CategoryLanguage);
        physPermLink.SETRANGE("Line No.",FromCategoryLineNo);
        IF physPermLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          tempPermLink.INIT;
          tempPermLink.TRANSFERFIELDS(physPermLink);
          tempPermLink."Line No." := ToCategoryLineNo;
          tempPermLink.INSERT(FALSE);
        UNTIL physPermLink.NEXT = 0;

        physPermLink.RESET;
        physPermLink.SETFILTER(Type,'%1|%2',physPermLink.Type::CustomerCategory,physPermLink.Type::CategoryGroup);
        physPermLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code",CategoryShop);
        physPermLink.SETRANGE("Language Code",CategoryLanguage);
        physPermLink.SETRANGE("Line No.",ToCategoryLineNo);
        IF physPermLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          tempPermLink.INIT;
          tempPermLink.TRANSFERFIELDS(physPermLink);
          tempPermLink."Line No." := FromCategoryLineNo;
          tempPermLink.INSERT(FALSE);
        UNTIL physPermLink.NEXT = 0;

        IF NOT tempPermLink.ISEMPTY THEN BEGIN
          physPermLink.RESET;
          physPermLink.SETFILTER(Type,'%1|%2',physPermLink.Type::CustomerCategory,physPermLink.Type::CategoryGroup);
          physPermLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code",CategoryShop);
          physPermLink.SETRANGE("Language Code",CategoryLanguage);
          physPermLink.SETFILTER("Line No.",'%1|%2',FromCategoryLineNo,ToCategoryLineNo);
          physPermLink.DELETEALL(FALSE);

          IF tempPermLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            physPermLink.INIT;
            physPermLink.TRANSFERFIELDS(tempPermLink);
            physPermLink.INSERT(FALSE);
          UNTIL tempPermLink.NEXT = 0;

          CLEAR(CurrRec);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_permissions_group_link');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[shop_code]',CategoryShop);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[language_code]',CategoryLanguage);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[line_no]',FromCategoryLineNo);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[type]',physPermLink.Type::CustomerCategory);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'confirm_delete',FALSE);
          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('delete',CurrRec,FALSE,TRUE)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('delete',CurrRec);

          CLEAR(CurrRec);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_permissions_group_link');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[shop_code]',CategoryShop);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[language_code]',CategoryLanguage);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[line_no]',FromCategoryLineNo);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[type]',physPermLink.Type::CategoryGroup);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'confirm_delete',FALSE);
          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('delete',CurrRec,FALSE,TRUE)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('delete',CurrRec);

          CLEAR(CurrRec);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_permissions_group_link');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[shop_code]',CategoryShop);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[language_code]',CategoryLanguage);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[line_no]',ToCategoryLineNo);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[type]',physPermLink.Type::CustomerCategory);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'confirm_delete',FALSE);
          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('delete',CurrRec,FALSE,TRUE)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('delete',CurrRec);

          CLEAR(CurrRec);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_permissions_group_link');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[shop_code]',CategoryShop);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[language_code]',CategoryLanguage);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[line_no]',ToCategoryLineNo);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[type]',physPermLink.Type::CategoryGroup);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'confirm_delete',FALSE);
          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('delete',CurrRec,FALSE,TRUE)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('delete',CurrRec);

          IF tempPermLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            UpdatePermGroupLink(tempPermLink);
          UNTIL tempPermLink.NEXT = 0;

        END;

      END;
      OnAfterSwapCategoryPermissions(IsHandled,CategoryShop,CategoryLanguage,FromCategoryLineNo,ToCategoryLineNo);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateGoogleMerchant@1000000123(ShopCode@1000000000 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      WebshopShop@1000000001 : Record 5447301;
      CurrRec@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      IsHandled@1000000003 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateGoogleMerchant(IsHandled,ShopCode);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // SH: XML im Web erstellen und an Google senden
        WebshopSetup.GET;
        WebshopShop.GET(ShopCode);
        WITH WebshopShop DO BEGIN
          AddVar(CurrRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'shop_code',Code);

          IF ("Use Items from Shop Code" <> Code) AND ("Use Items from Shop Code" <> '') THEN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'item_source',"Use Items from Shop Code")
          ELSE
            AddVar(CurrRec,'item_source',Code);

          IF ("Use Categories from Shop Code" <> Code) AND ("Use Categories from Shop Code" <> '') THEN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'category_source',"Use Categories from Shop Code")
          ELSE
            AddVar(CurrRec,'category_source',Code);

          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('google_merchant_center',CurrRec,FALSE,FALSE)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('google_merchant_center',CurrRec);
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateGoogleMerchant(IsHandled,ShopCode);
    END;

    PROCEDURE NewsletterSegmentLineSync@1000000233(SegLine@1000000000 : Record 5077;Shop@1000000010 : Record 5447301);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text;
      Contact@1000000002 : Record 5050;
      Text001@1000000003 : TextConst 'DEU=Herr;ENU=Mr.';
      Text002@1000000004 : TextConst 'DEU=Frau;ENU=Ms.';
      Contact2@1000000005 : Record 5050;
      Setup@1000000006 : Record 5447300;
      SalutationFormula@1000000007 : Record 5069;
      Text003@1000000008 : TextConst 'DEU=Dynamics NAV Segment;ENU=Dynamics NAV Segment';
      AnredeText@1000000009 : Text[20];
      IsHandled@1000000011 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeNewsletterSegmentLineSync(IsHandled,SegLine,Shop);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        //Segmentzeile an Copernica �bertragen
        Setup.GET;
        IF (Contact.GET(SegLine."Contact No.")) AND (Contact."E-Mail" <> '') THEN BEGIN

          AddVar(CurrRec,'Segment',SegLine."Segment No.");
          IF Contact."Salutation Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
            SalutationFormula.SETRANGE("Salutation Code",Contact."Salutation Code");
            SalutationFormula.SETRANGE("Language Code",Contact."Language Code");
            SalutationFormula.SETRANGE("Salutation Type",SalutationFormula."Salutation Type"::Formal);
            IF NOT SalutationFormula.ISEMPTY THEN
              AddVar(CurrRec,'Anrede_formell',Contact.GetSalutation(SalutationFormula."Salutation Type"::Formal,Contact."Language Code"));
            SalutationFormula.SETRANGE("Salutation Type",SalutationFormula."Salutation Type"::Informal);
            IF NOT SalutationFormula.ISEMPTY THEN
              AddVar(CurrRec,'Anrede_informell',Contact.GetSalutation(SalutationFormula."Salutation Type"::Informal,Contact."Language Code"));

            Contact2.RESET;
            Contact2.SETRANGE("No.",Contact."No.");
            Contact2.SETFILTER("Salutation Code",Shop."Salutation Code Filter Male");
            AnredeText := '';
            IF NOT Contact2.ISEMPTY THEN
              AnredeText := Text001
            ELSE BEGIN
              Contact2.SETFILTER("Salutation Code",Shop."Salutation Code Filter Female");
              IF NOT Contact2.ISEMPTY THEN
                AnredeText := Text002
            END;
          END;

          AddVar(CurrRec,'Anrede',AnredeText);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'Vorname',Contact."First Name");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'Nachname',Contact.Surname);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'Name',Contact.Name);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'EMail',Contact."E-Mail");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'Sprachcode',Contact."Language Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'PLZ',Contact."Post Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'Ort',Contact.City);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'Unternehmen',Contact."Company Name");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'Quelle',Text003);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'newsletter_account',Shop."Newsletter Account");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'newsletter_login',Shop."Newsletter Login");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'newsletter_password',Shop."Newsletter Password");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'newsletter_group',Shop."Newsletter Group");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'newsletter_segment',Shop."Newsletter Segment");
          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('upsert_newsletter_profile',CurrRec,FALSE,TRUE)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('upsert_newsletter_profile',CurrRec);
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterNewsletterSegmentLineSync(IsHandled,SegLine,Shop);
    END;
