    PROCEDURE UpdateAllReturnReason@1000000146(CountRecords@1000000001 : Boolean);
    VAR
      ReturnReason@1000000000 : Record 6635;
      Text001@1000000002 : TextConst 'DEU=Reklamationsgr�nde �bertragen;ENU=Synchronizing return reasons';
      IsHandled@1000000003 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateAllReturnReason(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        //Alle Reklamationsgr�nde an Funktion zum �bertragen einzelner Datens�tze weiterleiten
        ReturnReason.SETRANGE("Is Webshop Active",TRUE);
        IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(Text001,ReturnReason.COUNT);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          DeleteAllReturnReason(FALSE);
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(Text001,ReturnReason.COUNT);
          IF ReturnReason.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            UpdateReturnReason(ReturnReason);
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
          UNTIL ReturnReason.NEXT = 0;
          DeleteAllReturnReason(TRUE);
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateAllReturnReason(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateReturnReason@1000000147(ParamReturnReason@1000000002 : Record 6635);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000000 : Text;
      ReturnReason@1000000001 : Record 6635;
      IsHandled@1000000003 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateReturnReason(IsHandled,ParamReturnReason);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        //Einzelnen Reklamationsgrund �bertragen
        WITH ReturnReason DO BEGIN
          IF (ReturnReason.GET(ParamReturnReason.Code)) THEN BEGIN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_return_reason');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[description]',Description);
            IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
              WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
            ELSE
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateReturnReason(IsHandled,ParamReturnReason);
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteAllReturnReason@1000000150(ConfirmDelete@1000000000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text;
      IsHandled@1000000002 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeDeleteAllReturnReason(ConfirmDelete,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        //Alle Reklamationsgr�nde l�schen
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_return_reason');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'confirm_delete',ConfirmDelete);
        WebshopManagement.SendRequest('delete',CurrRec);
        IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
          WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('delete',CurrRec,FALSE,TRUE)
        ELSE
          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('delete',CurrRec);

      END;
      OnAfterDeleteAllReturnReason(ConfirmDelete,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllReturnOrders@1000000156(CountRecords@1000000000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      SalesHeader@1000000001 : Record 36;
      Customer@1000000002 : Record 18;
      IsHandled@1000000003 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateAllReturnOrders(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Alle Verkaufsreklamationen �bertragen

        WITH SalesHeader DO BEGIN
          SETRANGE("Document Type","Document Type"::"Return Order");
          SETRANGE(SalesHeader.Status,SalesHeader.Status::Released);
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
            IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              IF Customer.GET("Sell-to Customer No.") AND Customer."Has Webshop Login" THEN BEGIN
                IF (("Date Last Updated (Webshop)" < "Date Last Modified (Webshop)") OR ("Date Last Modified (Webshop)" = 0DT) OR ("Date Last Updated (Webshop)" = 0DT)) THEN
                  UpdateSalesOrder(SalesHeader);
              END;
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            UNTIL NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateAllReturnOrders(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetAllReturnOrder@1000000157(CountRecords@1000000000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      Text001@1000000001 : TextConst 'DEU=Reklamationen abrufen;ENU=Fetch return orders';
      NoOfOrder@1000000002 : Integer;
      CurrRec@1000000003 : Text[1024];
      GetResponseText@1000 : Text;
      LogFile@1001 : File;
      IsHandled@1000000004 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeGetAllReturnOrder(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Alle neuen Reklamationen abrufen

        WebshopSetup.GET;
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_sales_shipment_header');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[return_order]',TRUE);
        GetResponseText := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_no_of_records',CurrRec);
        IF EVALUATE(NoOfOrder,GetResponseText) THEN BEGIN
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(Text001,NoOfOrder);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(Text001,NoOfOrder);
            GetReturnOrder;
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
            ERROR(GetResponseText)
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
              LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
              LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
              IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
               IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                ERROR(Error001);
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                  ERROR(Error002);
              END;
              LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
              LogFile.WRITE(GetResponseText);
              LogFile.CLOSE;
              IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                MESSAGE(GetResponseText);
            END ELSE
              ERROR(Error003);
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterGetAllReturnOrder(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetReturnOrder@1000000155();
    VAR
      Shop@1000000012 : Record 5447301;
      Language@1000000011 : Record 5447302;
      SalesHeader@1000000002 : Record 36;
      SalesLine@1000000000 : Record 37;
      ShipmentHeader@1000000004 : Record 110;
      ShipmentLine@1000000007 : Record 111;
      SellToCust@1000000019 : Record 18;
      BillToCust@1000000008 : Record 18;
      DefaultDim@1000000023 : Record 352;
      DefaultDim2@1000000025 : Record 352;
      StandardText@1000000018 : Record 7;
      CurrRec@1000000003 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000020 : Text[1024];
      SetRec@1000000030 : Text[1024];
      Delimiter@1000000006 : Text[30];
      SellToCustBlockedState@1000000017 : Integer;
      BillToCustBlockedState@1000000010 : Integer;
      CommentLineNo@1000000016 : Integer;
      i@1000000027 : Integer;
      SalesLineTyp@1000000028 : Integer;
      Pos@1000000005 : Integer;
      NextSalesHeader@1000000001 : Boolean;
      ShipmentHeaderId@1000000009 : Code[11];
      ShopCode@1000000032 : Code[20];
      LanguageCode@1000000033 : Code[20];
      DocumentNo@1000000034 : Code[20];
      CustomerNo@1000000035 : Code[20];
      Text001@1000000076 : TextConst 'DEU=Lastschriftkonto: %1;ENU=Direct debit account: %1';
      Text002@1000000080 : TextConst 'DEU=Gutscheinrabatt Code %1;ENU=Coupon Discount Code %1';
      TempSalesLine@1000000095 : Record 37;
      ResetRec@1000000096 : Text[1024];
      SalespersonCode@1000000098 : Code[10];
      DimSetID@1000000099 : Integer;
      DimMgt@1000000100 : Codeunit 408;
      TMPDimensionSetEntry@1000000101 : TEMPORARY Record 480;
      DimValue@1000000103 : Record 349;
      DimValueID@1000000102 : Integer;
      LogFile@1000000013 : File;
      WebReturnOrderNo@1000000014 : Code[30];
      ReturnOrderReference@1000000015 : Text[80];
      IsHandled@1000000021 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeGetReturnOrder(IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Alle neuen Bestellungen abrufen
        Delimiter := '-;-';

        WebshopSetup.GET;
        SalesHeader.RESET;

        WITH SalesHeader DO REPEAT
          SalesHeader.RESET;

          NextSalesHeader := FALSE;

          // Webshop-Reklamation anfordern
          CLEAR(CurrRec);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_sales_shipment_header');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'delimiter',Delimiter);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'fields','id,no,return_shop_code,return_language_code,return_order_shop_no,return_order_reference');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[return_order]',TRUE);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[return_order_insert]',TRUE);
          GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_content',CurrRec);
          IF STRPOS(GetRec,Delimiter) <> 0 THEN BEGIN

            // Webshop-Bestellung Teil 1 in Variablen speichern
            CLEAR(ShipmentHeaderId);
            CLEAR(DocumentNo);
            CLEAR(ShopCode);
            CLEAR(LanguageCode);
            CLEAR(WebReturnOrderNo);
            CLEAR(ReturnOrderReference);
            EVALUATE(ShipmentHeaderId,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(DocumentNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(ShopCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(LanguageCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(WebReturnOrderNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(ReturnOrderReference,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            IF (ShipmentHeaderId <> '') AND (DocumentNo <> '') AND (ShopCode <> '') AND (WebReturnOrderNo <> '') AND (LanguageCode <> '')
              AND (ShipmentHeader.GET(DocumentNo)) AND (Shop.GET(ShopCode)) AND (Language.GET(ShopCode,LanguageCode)) THEN BEGIN

              // Gesperrten Kunden entsperren
              CLEAR(SellToCust);
              SellToCustBlockedState := 0;

              SellToCust.GET(ShipmentHeader."Sell-to Customer No.");
              IF SellToCust.Blocked <> SellToCust.Blocked::" " THEN BEGIN
                SellToCustBlockedState := SellToCust.Blocked;
                SellToCust.Blocked := SellToCust.Blocked::" ";
                SellToCust.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;

              CLEAR(BillToCust);
              BillToCustBlockedState := 0;

              BillToCust.GET(ShipmentHeader."Bill-to Customer No.");
              IF BillToCust.Blocked <> BillToCust.Blocked::" " THEN BEGIN
                BillToCustBlockedState := BillToCust.Blocked;
                BillToCust.Blocked := BillToCust.Blocked::" ";
                BillToCust.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;


              // R�cksendungsauftrag oder Gutschrift in NAV erstellen
              INIT;

              IF Shop."Return Order Type" = Shop."Return Order Type"::"Return Order" THEN
                VALIDATE("Document Type","Document Type"::"Return Order")
              ELSE
                VALIDATE("Document Type","Document Type"::"Credit Memo");

              IF Shop."Return Order No. Series" <> '' THEN
                "No. Series" := Shop."Return Order No. Series";
              "No." := '';
              Status := Status::Open;
              INSERT(TRUE);

              "No. Series" := '';
              VALIDATE("Sell-to Customer No.",SellToCust."No.");
              VALIDATE("Sell-to Contact",ShipmentHeader."Sell-to Contact");
              VALIDATE("Sell-to Country/Region Code",ShipmentHeader."Sell-to Country/Region Code");
              VALIDATE("Bill-to Customer No.",BillToCust."No.");
              VALIDATE("Bill-to Contact",ShipmentHeader."Sell-to Contact");
              VALIDATE("Bill-to Country/Region Code",ShipmentHeader."Bill-to Country/Region Code");
              VALIDATE("Ship-to Name",ShipmentHeader."Ship-to Name");
              VALIDATE("Ship-to Name 2",ShipmentHeader."Ship-to Name 2");
              VALIDATE("Ship-to Address",ShipmentHeader."Ship-to Address");
              VALIDATE("Ship-to Address 2",ShipmentHeader."Ship-to Address 2");
              "Ship-to Post Code" := ShipmentHeader."Ship-to Post Code";
              "Ship-to City" := ShipmentHeader."Ship-to City";
              VALIDATE("Ship-to Country/Region Code",ShipmentHeader."Ship-to Country/Region Code");
              VALIDATE("Ship-to Contact",ShipmentHeader."Ship-to Contact");
              "Webshop Order No." := ShipmentHeader."Webshop Order No.";
              "Date Webshop Synchronization" := CURRENTDATETIME;
              "Webshop Shop Code" := ShopCode;
              "Webshop Language Code" := LanguageCode;
              "Webshop Return Order Shop Code" := ShopCode;
              "Webshop Return Order Shop Code" := LanguageCode;
              "Date Webshop Ret. Order Sync." := CURRENTDATETIME;
              "Clear Webshop Payment" := ShipmentHeader."Clear Webshop Payment";


              IF (ShipmentHeader."Currency Code" <> '') THEN
                VALIDATE("Currency Code",ShipmentHeader."Currency Code");
              IF (ShipmentHeader."Campaign No." <> '') THEN
                VALIDATE("Campaign No.",ShipmentHeader."Campaign No.");

              "Salesperson Code" := ShipmentHeader."Salesperson Code";

              IF (ShipmentHeader."Customer Price Group" <> '') THEN
                VALIDATE("Customer Price Group",ShipmentHeader."Customer Price Group");
              IF (ShipmentHeader."Customer Disc. Group" <> '') THEN
                VALIDATE("Customer Disc. Group",ShipmentHeader."Customer Disc. Group");

              //Online-Reklamationsnummer einf�gen
              "Webshop Return Order No." := WebReturnOrderNo;

              MODIFY(TRUE);


              // Dimension setzen
              IF (Language."Report Dimension" <> '') AND (Language."Report Dimension Value" <> '')
                 AND (DimValue.GET(Language."Report Dimension",Language."Report Dimension Value")) THEN BEGIN

                DimValueID := DimValue."Dimension Value ID";

                DimSetID := "Dimension Set ID";
                DimMgt.GetDimensionSet(TMPDimensionSetEntry,DimSetID);

                TMPDimensionSetEntry.RESET;
                TMPDimensionSetEntry.INIT;
                TMPDimensionSetEntry."Dimension Code" := Language."Report Dimension";
                TMPDimensionSetEntry."Dimension Value Code" := Language."Report Dimension Value";
                TMPDimensionSetEntry."Dimension Value ID" := DimValueID;
                TMPDimensionSetEntry.INSERT;

                DimSetID := DimMgt.GetDimensionSetID(TMPDimensionSetEntry);

                VALIDATE("Dimension Set ID",DimSetID);
              END;

              MODIFY(TRUE);

              // Bestellnummer einf�gen
              IF Language."Return Order No Text" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                StandardText.GET(Language."Return Order No Text");
                WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,Language."Online Order No Text",'',0,0,
                                STRSUBSTNO(StandardText.Description,WebReturnOrderNo),FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE,'');
              END;

              // Referenztext einf�gen
              IF ReturnOrderReference <> '' THEN BEGIN
                IF Language."Return Order Reference Text" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                  StandardText.GET(Language."Return Order Reference Text");
                  WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,Language."Your Reference Text",'',0,0,
                                    STRSUBSTNO(StandardText.Description,ReturnOrderReference),FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE,'');
                END;
              END;

              // Leeerzeile einf�gen
              WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,'','',0,0,'',FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE,'');

              // Artikelzeilen einf�gen
              GetReturnLines(SalesHeader,SalesLine,ShipmentHeader."No.");

              // Gesperrten Kunden zur�cksetzten
              IF SellToCustBlockedState <> 0 THEN BEGIN
                SellToCust.Blocked := SellToCustBlockedState;
                SellToCust.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;

              IF BillToCustBlockedState <> 0 THEN BEGIN
                BillToCust.Blocked := BillToCustBlockedState;
                BillToCust.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;

              // Bestellung als �bertragen markieren
              CLEAR(SetRec);
              AddVar(SetRec,'table','shop_sales_shipment_header');
              AddVar(SetRec,'primary[id]',ShipmentHeaderId);
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[return_order_insert]',FALSE);
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[return_order]',FALSE);
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[return_shop_code]','');
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[return_language_code]','');
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[return_order_reference]','');
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[return_order_shop_no]','');
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[return_order_token]','');
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',SetRec);

              CLEAR(SetRec);
              AddVar(SetRec,'table','shop_ret_shipment_nos');
              AddVar(SetRec,'primary[ret_order_no]',WebReturnOrderNo);
              WebshopManagement.AddDTVar(SetRec,'data[datetime_used]',CURRENTDATETIME);
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',SetRec);
              NextSalesHeader := TRUE;
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;

              COMMIT;
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF GetRec<>'0' THEN BEGIN
              IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
                ERROR(GetRec)
              ELSE BEGIN
                IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
                  LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                  LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                  IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
                   IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                    ERROR(Error001);
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                      ERROR(Error002);
                  END;
                  LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                  LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
                  LogFile.CLOSE;
                  IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                    MESSAGE(GetRec);
                END ELSE
                  ERROR(Error003);
              END;
            END;
          END;
        UNTIL NOT NextSalesHeader;

      END;
      OnAfterGetReturnOrder(IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetReturnLines@1000000149(VAR SalesHeader@1000000006 : Record 36;VAR SalesLine@1000000007 : Record 37;ShipmentHeaderNo@1000000009 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000000 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      SetRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      NextSalesLine@1000000003 : Boolean;
      ItemNo@1000000004 : Code[20];
      ItemQuantity@1000000005 : Decimal;
      Delimiter@1000000008 : Text[10];
      SalesLineId@1000000010 : Integer;
      ShipmentLine@1000000023 : Record 111;
      Item@1000000011 : Record 27;
      Text001@1000000012 : TextConst 'DEU=!!! Artikel %1, Menge %2 fehlt !!!;ENU=!!! Item %1, Qty %2 does not exist !!!';
      VariantCode@1000000013 : Code[10];
      ErrorText@1000000014 : Text[100];
      Text002@1000000015 : TextConst 'DEU=!!! Artikel %1, Menge %2 gesperrt !!!;ENU=!!! Item %1, Qty %2 blocked !!!';
      IsCouponItem@1000000016 : Boolean;
      SalespersonDiscount@1000000017 : Decimal;
      ShopCode@1000000018 : Code[10];
      LanguageCode@1000000019 : Code[10];
      Shop@1000000020 : Record 5447301;
      WebshopItem@1000000021 : Record 5447304;
      LogFile@1000 : File;
      ShipmentHeader@1000000022 : Record 110;
      ShipmentLineNo@1000000024 : Integer;
      WebShipmentLineID@1000000025 : Integer;
      QuantityReturned@1000000038 : Decimal;
      ReturnQuantity@1000000026 : Decimal;
      ReturnReasonCode@1000000027 : Code[20];
      Text003@1000000028 : TextConst 'DEU=Es konnte keine Lieferzeile vom Typ ''Artikel'' anhand der mitgegebenen Daten ermittelt werden.;ENU=No Shipment Line of type ''Item'' could be found for the given values.';
      Text004@1000000029 : TextConst 'DEU=Es konnten nicht alle n�tigen Werte aus dem erhaltenen Web-Datensatz bezogen werden.;ENU=Not all required values could be drawn from the Web-dataset.';
      ReturnReason@1000000030 : Record 6635;
      CopyDocMgt@1000000031 : Codeunit 6620;
      LinesNotCopied@1000000032 : Integer;
      QtyNotReturned@1000000033 : Decimal;
      MissingExCostRevLink@1000000034 : Boolean;
      Text005@1000000035 : TextConst 'DEU=Die Lieferzeile konnte nicht in den Reklamationsauftrag �bernommen werden;ENU=The shipment line could not be copied to the return order';
      Text006@1000000036 : TextConst 'DEU=R�cksendungsgrund: %1;ENU=Return reason: %1';
      ModifySalesLine@1000000037 : Boolean;
      IsHandled@1000000039 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeGetReturnLines(IsHandled,SalesHeader,SalesLine,ShipmentHeaderNo);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Reklamationszeile abholen

        Delimiter := '-;-';

        WITH SalesLine DO REPEAT
          NextSalesLine := FALSE;

          IF(ShipmentHeaderNo <> '') AND (ShipmentHeader.GET(ShipmentHeaderNo)) THEN BEGIN
            // Daten von Webshop-Reklamationsszeilen anfordern
            CLEAR(CurrRec);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_sales_shipment_line');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'delimiter',Delimiter);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'fields','id,line_no,quantity_returned,return_quantity,return_reason_code');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[document_no]',ShipmentHeaderNo);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[return_order]',TRUE);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[return_order_insert]',TRUE);

            GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_content',CurrRec);

            IF STRPOS(GetRec,Delimiter) <> 0 THEN BEGIN

              // Verkaufszeile einf�gen
              CLEAR(WebShipmentLineID);
              CLEAR(ShipmentLineNo);
              CLEAR(QuantityReturned);
              CLEAR(ReturnQuantity);
              CLEAR(ReturnReasonCode);
              EVALUATE(WebShipmentLineID,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(ShipmentLineNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(QuantityReturned,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(ReturnQuantity,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(ReturnReasonCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              IF (WebShipmentLineID > 0) AND (ShipmentLineNo > 0) AND (ReturnQuantity > 0) AND (ReturnReasonCode <> '')
                AND (ReturnReason.GET(ReturnReasonCode)) THEN BEGIN

                //Lieferzeile holen
                ShipmentLine.RESET;
                ShipmentLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",ShipmentHeaderNo);
                ShipmentLine.SETRANGE("Line No.",ShipmentLineNo);
                IF (ShipmentLine.FINDFIRST) AND (ShipmentLine.Type = ShipmentLine.Type::Item) THEN BEGIN

                  //Maximal reklamierte Menge nicht gr��er gelieferte, noch nicht reklamierte Menge
                  QtyNotReturned := 0;
                  ShipmentLine.CalcShippedSaleNotReturned(QtyNotReturned,ShipmentLine."Unit Cost (LCY)",TRUE);


                  IF (QtyNotReturned < ReturnQuantity) THEN
                    ReturnQuantity := QtyNotReturned;

        //          ErrorText := '';
        //          IF NOT Item.GET(ItemNo) THEN
        //            ErrorText := STRSUBSTNO(Text001,ItemNo,ItemQuantity);
        //          IF Item.Blocked THEN
        //            ErrorText := STRSUBSTNO(Text002,ItemNo,ItemQuantity);
        //          IF ErrorText = '' THEN BEGIN
        //            WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,1,ItemNo,VariantCode,ItemQuantity,0,'',IsCouponItem,
        //                                              SalespersonDiscount,WebshopItem."Unit of Measure Code");
        //          END ELSE BEGIN
        //            WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,'','',0,0,ErrorText,FALSE,0,'');
        //          END;

                  //Zeilen in Sales Header kopieren
                  LinesNotCopied := 0;

                  CopyDocMgt.SetProperties(FALSE,FALSE,FALSE,FALSE,TRUE,TRUE,TRUE);
                  MissingExCostRevLink := TRUE;
                  CopyDocMgt.CopySalesShptLinesToDoc(SalesHeader,ShipmentLine,LinesNotCopied,MissingExCostRevLink);

                  IF LinesNotCopied = 0 THEN BEGIN

                    ModifySalesLine := FALSE;

                    SalesLine.RESET;
                    SalesLine.SETRANGE("Document Type",SalesHeader."Document Type");
                    SalesLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",SalesHeader."No.");
                    SalesLine.SETRANGE(Type,SalesLine.Type::Item);
                    SalesLine.FINDLAST;

                    //Menge korrigieren wenn n�tig
                    IF SalesLine.Quantity > ReturnQuantity THEN BEGIN
                      VALIDATE(SalesLine.Quantity,ReturnQuantity);
                      ModifySalesLine := TRUE;
                    END;

                    //R�cksendungsgrund bei Return Order angeben
                    IF (SalesHeader."Document Type" = SalesHeader."Document Type"::"Return Order") THEN BEGIN
                      VALIDATE(SalesLine."Return Reason Code",ReturnReason.Code);
                      ModifySalesLine := TRUE;
                    END;

                    IF ModifySalesLine THEN
                      SalesLine.MODIFY;

                    SalesLine.RESET;

                    //R�cksendungsgrund (bei Return Order zus�tzlich) als Textzeile angeben
                    WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,'','',0,0,STRSUBSTNO(Text006,ReturnReason.Description),FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE,'');

                    // Leeerzeile einf�gen
                    WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,'','',0,0,'',FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE,'');

                    // Webshop-Reklamationsszeile als �bertragen kennzeichnen
                    CLEAR(SetRec);
                    AddVar(SetRec,'table','shop_sales_shipment_line');
                    AddVar(SetRec,'primary[id]',WebShipmentLineID);
                    AddVar(SetRec,'data[quantity_returned]',(QuantityReturned + ReturnQuantity));
                    AddVar(SetRec,'data[return_order_insert]',FALSE);
                    AddVar(SetRec,'data[return_order]',FALSE);
                    AddVar(SetRec,'data[return_quantity]',0);
                    AddVar(SetRec,'data[return_reason_code]','');
                    WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',SetRec);
                    NextSalesLine := TRUE

                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
                      ERROR(Text005)
                    ELSE BEGIN
                      IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
                        LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                        LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                        IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
                         IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                          ERROR(Error001);
                        END ELSE BEGIN
                          IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                            ERROR(Error002);
                        END;
                        LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                        LogFile.WRITE(Text005);
                        LogFile.CLOSE;
                        IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                          MESSAGE(Text005);
                      END ELSE
                        ERROR(Error003);
                    END;
                  END;
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
                    ERROR(Text003)
                  ELSE BEGIN
                    IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
                      LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                      LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                      IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
                       IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                        ERROR(Error001);
                      END ELSE BEGIN
                        IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                          ERROR(Error002);
                      END;
                      LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                      LogFile.WRITE(Text003);
                      LogFile.CLOSE;
                      IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                        MESSAGE(Text003);
                    END ELSE
                      ERROR(Error003);
                  END;
                END;
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
                  ERROR(Text004)
                ELSE BEGIN
                  IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
                    LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                    LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                    IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
                     IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                      ERROR(Error001);
                    END ELSE BEGIN
                      IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                        ERROR(Error002);
                    END;
                    LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                    LogFile.WRITE(Text004);
                    LogFile.CLOSE;
                    IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                      MESSAGE(Text004);
                  END ELSE
                    ERROR(Error003);
                END;
              END;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              IF GetRec<>'0' THEN BEGIN
                IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
                  ERROR(GetRec)
                ELSE BEGIN
                  IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
                    LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                    LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                    IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
                     IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                      ERROR(Error001);
                    END ELSE BEGIN
                      IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                        ERROR(Error002);
                    END;
                    LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                    LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
                    LogFile.CLOSE;
                    IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                      MESSAGE(GetRec);
                  END ELSE
                    ERROR(Error003);
                END;
              END;
            END;
          END;
          CLEAR(CopyDocMgt);
        UNTIL NOT NextSalesLine;

      END;
      OnAfterGetReturnLines(IsHandled,SalesHeader,SalesLine,ShipmentHeaderNo);
    END;

    PROCEDURE "--- Abo ---"@1000000164();
    BEGIN
    END;

    PROCEDURE ManageSubscriptions@1000000165(CountRecords@1000000001 : Boolean);
    VAR
      WebshopSubscriptionHeader@1000000000 : Record 5447340;
      IsHandled@1000000002 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeManageSubscriptions(IsHandled,CountRecords);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        //Verwaltet alle wiederkehrenden Abomodul Prozesse

        WebshopSubscriptionHeader.SETRANGE("Is Active",TRUE);
        IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(WebshopSubscriptionHeader.TABLECAPTION,WebshopSubscriptionHeader.COUNT)
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(WebshopSubscriptionHeader.TABLECAPTION,WebshopSubscriptionHeader.COUNT);
            CreateSalOrdFromSubscriptions(WebshopSubscriptionHeader);
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
          UNTIL WebshopSubscriptionHeader.NEXT = 0;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterManageSubscriptions(IsHandled,CountRecords);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CreateSalOrdFromSubscriptions@1000000166(WebshopSubscriptionHeader@1000000000 : Record 5447340);
    VAR
      WebshopSubscrCustomerLink@1000000001 : Record 5447343;
      PayAndOrShip@1000000002 : 'PaymentOnly,ShipmentOnly,Both';
      MaxTurns@1000000003 : Integer;
      IsHandled@1000000004 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeCreateSalOrdFromSubscriptions(IsHandled,WebshopSubscriptionHeader);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        //St��t die Erzeugung von Auftr�gen auf Basis von Abos und Abo-Kunden-Zuordnungen an

        IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Max. No. of Turns Per Customer" > 0 THEN
          MaxTurns := WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Max. No. of Turns Per Customer";

        WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.RESET;
        WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.SETRANGE("Subscription Code",WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code);
        WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.SETFILTER("Next Order Creation Date",'>%1&<=%2',0D,WORKDATE);
        WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.SETFILTER("Next Order w. Payment Date",'=%1',0D);
        IF WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          IF (MaxTurns = 0) OR (WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."No. of turns processed" < MaxTurns) THEN
            CreateSalOrdForSubscription(WebshopSubscriptionHeader,WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,PayAndOrShip::ShipmentOnly)
        UNTIL WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.NEXT = 0;

        WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.RESET;
        WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.SETRANGE("Subscription Code",WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code);
        WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.SETFILTER("Next Order Creation Date",'=%1',0D);
        WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.SETFILTER("Next Order w. Payment Date",'>%1&<=%2',0D,WORKDATE);
        IF WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          IF (MaxTurns = 0) OR (WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."No. of turns processed" < MaxTurns) THEN
            CreateSalOrdForSubscription(WebshopSubscriptionHeader,WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,PayAndOrShip::PaymentOnly);
        UNTIL WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.NEXT = 0;

        WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.RESET;
        WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.SETRANGE("Subscription Code",WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code);
        WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.SETFILTER("Next Order Creation Date",'>%1&<=%2',0D,WORKDATE);
        WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.SETFILTER("Next Order w. Payment Date",'>%1&<=%2',0D,WORKDATE);
        IF WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          IF (MaxTurns = 0) OR (WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."No. of turns processed" < MaxTurns) THEN
            CreateSalOrdForSubscription(WebshopSubscriptionHeader,WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,PayAndOrShip::Both);
        UNTIL WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.NEXT = 0;

      END;
      OnAfterCreateSalOrdFromSubscriptions(IsHandled,WebshopSubscriptionHeader);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CreateSalOrdForSubscription@1000000167(WebshopSubscriptionHeader@1000000000 : Record 5447340;WebshopSubscrCustomerLink@1000000001 : Record 5447343;PayAndOrShip@1000000008 : 'PaymentOnly,ShipmentOnly,Both');
    VAR
      Customer@1000000002 : Record 18;
      SalesHeader@1000000003 : Record 36;
      Shop@1000000004 : Record 5447301;
      ShopLanguage@1000000005 : Record 5447302;
      WebshopPaymentOption@1000000006 : Record 5447312;
      WebshopCustPseudoPayData@1000000007 : Record 5447315;
      Error001@1000000009 : TextConst 'DEU=Die Abonnement-Kundenzuordnung sieht f�r das aktuelle Arbeitsdatum keine Erzeugung einer Verkaufsbestellung vor.;ENU=The Subscription-Customer-link is not set for the creation of a Sales Order on the current workdate.';
      Error002@1000000010 : TextConst 'DEU=Die maximale Anzahl an Verkaufsbestellungen f�r diese Abonnement-Kundenzuordnung wurde bereits erstellt.;ENU=The maximum number of Sales Orders for this Customer-Subscription-link has already been created.';
      SalesShipmentHeader@1000000011 : Record 110;
      ShipmentHeaderCount@1000000012 : Integer;
      CustomerBlockedState@1000000013 : Integer;
      NoSeriesCode@1000000014 : Code[10];
      PaymentOption@1000000015 : Record 5447312;
      ShippingOption@1000000016 : Record 5447453;
      WebshopShippingOptionLink@1000000053 : Record 5447311;
      DimValueID@1000000017 : Integer;
      DimValue@1000000018 : Record 349;
      DimSetID@1000000019 : Integer;
      DimMgt@1000000020 : Codeunit 408;
      TMPDimensionSetEntry@1000000021 : TEMPORARY Record 480;
      WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep@1000000022 : Record 5447341;
      ItemQtyToShip@1000000023 : Decimal;
      ItemQtyToInvoice@1000000028 : Decimal;
      NextPayOrderCreateDate@1000000024 : Date;
      NextShipOrderCreateDate@1000000025 : Date;
      BaseDateHandling@1000000026 : 'BaseDateIncluded,BaseDateExcluded';
      NextWorkdayDirection@1000000027 : '>,<';
      Error003@1000000029 : TextConst 'DEU=Es kann aktuell kein Auftrag mit zu berechnenden Positionen f�r die Abonnement-Kundenzuweisung erstellt werden;ENU=A Sales Order with positions to invoice cannot currently be created for the Subscription-Customer-link';
      Error004@1000000030 : TextConst 'DEU=Es kann aktuell kein Auftrag mit zu berechnenden Positionen f�r die Abonnement-Kundenzuweisung erstellt werden;ENU=A Sales Order with positions to invoice cannot currently be created for the Subscription-Customer-link';
      SalesLine@1000000031 : Record 37;
      SalesLineType@1000000032 : Integer;
      CurrItemNo@1000000033 : Code[30];
      CurrItemVarCode@1000000040 : Code[10];
      CurrItemQty@1000000039 : Decimal;
      CurrItemUnitPrice@1000000041 : Decimal;
      CurrSalesLineUnitPrice@1000000034 : Decimal;
      CurrSalesLineQty@1000000035 : Decimal;
      CurrTotalPayAmount@1000000044 : Decimal;
      WebshopSubscriptionItemLink@1000000036 : Record 5447342;
      TMPSalesLine@1000000037 : TEMPORARY Record 37;
      WebshopItem@1000000038 : Record 5447304;
      Text001@1000000042 : TextConst 'DEU=Zahlung aus Abonnement;ENU=Payed for from subscription';
      Text002@1000000043 : TextConst 'DEU=Abonnement Abrechnung f�r Zeitraum:;ENU=Subscription payment for period:';
      PayAmntPerTurn@1000000045 : Decimal;
      NoOfTurnsInCurrBillingInterval@1000000046 : Integer;
      DateRangeText@1000000047 : Text[50];
      NextPlannedDeliveryDate@1000000048 : Date;
      CurrReferenceDeliveryDate@1000000049 : Date;
      Item@1000000050 : Record 27;
      QtyMultiplier@1000000051 : Decimal;
      IsHandled@1000000052 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeCreateSalOrdForSubscription(IsHandled,WebshopSubscriptionHeader,WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,PayAndOrShip);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        //Erzeugt Verkaufskopf f�r Abo und Abo-Kunden-Zuordnungen

        IF (WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Next Order Creation Date" > WORKDATE) AND
           (WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Next Order w. Payment Date" > WORKDATE) THEN
          ERROR(Error001);

        Customer.GET(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Customer No.");
        Shop.GET(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Webshop Shop Code");
        ShopLanguage.GET(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Webshop Shop Code",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Webshop Language Code");
        PaymentOption.GET(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Webshop Shop Code",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Webshop Language Code",
                          WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Webshop Pay. Opt. Line No.");
        ShippingOption.GET(Shop."Shipping Group Code",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Webshop Ship. Opt. Line No.");

        IF (WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Next Planned Delivery Date" > 0D) AND (WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Next Planned Delivery Date" >
        WORKDATE) THEN
          CurrReferenceDeliveryDate := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Next Planned Delivery Date"
        ELSE
          CurrReferenceDeliveryDate := WORKDATE;

        NextPlannedDeliveryDate := GetCustSubscNextDeliveryDate(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,CurrReferenceDeliveryDate,
                                                                BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded);
        NextShipOrderCreateDate := NextPlannedDeliveryDate;
        IF NextShipOrderCreateDate > 0D THEN BEGIN
          NextShipOrderCreateDate := CALCDATE(Shop."Forerun Subscr. Order Create",NextShipOrderCreateDate);
          NextShipOrderCreateDate := WebshopManagement.GetNextWorkday(NextShipOrderCreateDate,NextWorkdayDirection::">");
        END;

        NextPayOrderCreateDate := GetCustSubscrNextPayOrderDate(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,CurrReferenceDeliveryDate,BaseDateHandling
        ::BaseDateExcluded);

        IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionSequenceItem THEN
          WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.GET(WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code,WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Next Sequence Step Line No.");

        IF (WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionOrderOption) AND (PayAndOrShip = PayAndOrShip::
        Both) THEN BEGIN
          ItemQtyToShip := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Subscription Qty. Per Turn";
          ItemQtyToInvoice := GetCustSubscrItQtyInDateRange(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,WORKDATE,NextPayOrderCreateDate);
        END ELSE IF (WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionOrderOption) AND (PayAndOrShip =
        PayAndOrShip::PaymentOnly) THEN BEGIN
          ItemQtyToInvoice := GetCustSubscrItQtyInDateRange(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,WORKDATE,NextPayOrderCreateDate);
        END ELSE IF (WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionOrderOption) AND (PayAndOrShip =
        PayAndOrShip::ShipmentOnly) THEN BEGIN
          ItemQtyToShip := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Subscription Qty. Per Turn";
        END;


        IF ((PayAndOrShip = PayAndOrShip::PaymentOnly) OR (PayAndOrShip = PayAndOrShip::Both)) AND
           NOT (WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Next Order w. Payment Date" <= WORKDATE) THEN
           ERROR(Error003);

        IF ((PayAndOrShip = PayAndOrShip::ShipmentOnly) OR (PayAndOrShip = PayAndOrShip::Both)) AND
           NOT (WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Next Order Creation Date" <= WORKDATE) THEN
           ERROR(Error004);


        // Gesperrten Kunden entsperren
        CLEAR(CustomerBlockedState);
        IF Customer.Blocked <> Customer.Blocked::" " THEN BEGIN
          CustomerBlockedState := Customer.Blocked;
          Customer.Blocked := Customer.Blocked::" ";
          Customer.MODIFY(FALSE);
        END;

        // Verkaufsangebot oder Verkaufsauftrag in NAV erstellen
        WITH SalesHeader DO BEGIN
          RESET;
          INIT;
          IF Shop."Subscr. Order No. Series Code" <> '' THEN
            NoSeriesCode := Shop."Subscr. Order No. Series Code"
          ELSE
            NoSeriesCode := Shop."Code No. Series Orders";

          VALIDATE("Document Type","Document Type"::Order);
          "No. Series" := NoSeriesCode;
          "No." := '';
          INSERT(TRUE);
          "No. Series" := '';
          VALIDATE("Sell-to Customer No.",Customer."No.");
          VALIDATE("Sell-to Contact",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."User Name");
          IF Shop."Use Pool Customer" THEN BEGIN
            VALIDATE("Sell-to Customer Name",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Bill-to Name");
            VALIDATE("Sell-to Customer Name 2",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Bill-to Name 2");
            VALIDATE("Sell-to Address",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Bill-to Address");
            VALIDATE("Sell-to Address 2",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Bill-to Address 2");
            "Sell-to Post Code" := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Bill-to Post Code";
            "Sell-to City" := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Bill-to City";
            VALIDATE("Sell-to Country/Region Code",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Bill-to Country/Region Code");
            VALIDATE("Bill-to Name",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Bill-to Name");
            VALIDATE("Bill-to Name 2",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Bill-to Name 2");
            VALIDATE("Bill-to Address",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Bill-to Address");
            VALIDATE("Bill-to Address 2",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Bill-to Post Code");
            "Bill-to Post Code" := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Bill-to Post Code";
            "Bill-to City" := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Bill-to City";
            VALIDATE("Bill-to Country/Region Code",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Bill-to Country/Region Code");
          END;
          VALIDATE("Ship-to Name",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Ship-to Name");
          VALIDATE("Ship-to Name 2",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Ship-to Name 2");
          VALIDATE("Ship-to Address",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Ship-to Address");
          VALIDATE("Ship-to Address 2",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Ship-to Address 2");
          "Ship-to Post Code" := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Ship-to Post Code";
          "Ship-to City" := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Ship-to City";
          VALIDATE("Ship-to Country/Region Code",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Ship-to Country/Region Code");
          VALIDATE("Ship-to Contact",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."User Name");
          VALIDATE("Sell-to Country/Region Code",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Ship-to Country/Region Code");
          VALIDATE("Requested Delivery Date",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Next Planned Delivery Date");
        //  VALIDATE("Your Reference",YourReference);
        //  "Webshop Order No." := OrderNo;
        //  "Webshop Sync Date" := CURRENTDATETIME;
          "Webshop Shop Code" := Shop.Code;
          "Webshop Language Code" := ShopLanguage.Code;
          "Webshop Payment Opt. Line No." := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Webshop Pay. Opt. Line No.";
          "Webshop Shipping Opt. Line No." := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Webshop Ship. Opt. Line No.";
          "Shipping Group Code" := ShippingOption."Shipping Group Code";
          "Process Webshop Payment" := ((PayAndOrShip = PayAndOrShip::PaymentOnly) OR (PayAndOrShip = PayAndOrShip::Both));
          "Webshop Subscription Code" := WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code;
          "Webshop Subscr. Cust. Line No." := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Line No.";
          "Webshop Order No." := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Webshop Initial Order No.";
          IF ((PayAndOrShip = PayAndOrShip::PaymentOnly) OR (PayAndOrShip = PayAndOrShip::Both)) THEN
            "Web. Cust. Ps.-Pay. Line No." := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Customer Pseudo-Pay. Line No.";
          "Webshop Sub. Seq. St. Line No." := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Next Sequence Step Line No.";

          IF ((PayAndOrShip = PayAndOrShip::PaymentOnly) OR (PayAndOrShip = PayAndOrShip::Both)) THEN
            "Is Webshop Subscr. Inv. Order" := TRUE;

          IF ((PayAndOrShip = PayAndOrShip::ShipmentOnly) OR (PayAndOrShip = PayAndOrShip::Both)) THEN
            "Is Webshop Subscr. Ship. Order" := TRUE;

          "Clear Webshop Payment" := PaymentOption."Auto Clearing";
          IF (ShopLanguage."E-Mail Order 2" <> '') AND
             ((PayAndOrShip = PayAndOrShip::ShipmentOnly) OR (PayAndOrShip = PayAndOrShip::Both)) THEN
            "Send Webshop Order Confirm." := TRUE;
          IF (ShopLanguage."E-Mail Order 3" <> '') AND
             ((PayAndOrShip = PayAndOrShip::ShipmentOnly) OR (PayAndOrShip = PayAndOrShip::Both)) THEN
            "Send Webshop Shipping Notice" := TRUE;
          IF (ShopLanguage."E-Mail Order 4" <> '') AND
             ((PayAndOrShip = PayAndOrShip::PaymentOnly) OR (PayAndOrShip = PayAndOrShip::Both)) THEN
            "Send Webshop Invoice Mail" := TRUE;

          MODIFY(TRUE);

          IF WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Currency Code" <> '' THEN
            VALIDATE("Currency Code",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Currency Code");
          IF (Shop."Default Salesperson Code" <> '') AND (NOT (Shop."Shop Type" = Shop."Shop Type"::salesperson)) THEN
            "Salesperson Code" := Shop."Default Salesperson Code";
          IF PaymentOption."Payment Terms Code" <> '' THEN
            "Payment Terms Code" := PaymentOption."Payment Terms Code";
          IF PaymentOption."Payment Method Code" <> '' THEN
            "Payment Method Code" := PaymentOption."Payment Method Code";
          IF ShippingOption."Shipping Agent Code" <> '' THEN
            "Shipping Agent Code" := ShippingOption."Shipping Agent Code";
          IF ShippingOption."Shipping Agent Service Code" <> '' THEN
            "Shipping Agent Service Code" := ShippingOption."Shipping Agent Service Code";
          IF ShippingOption."Shipment Method Code" <> '' THEN
             "Shipment Method Code" := ShippingOption."Shipment Method Code";
          IF Shop."Cust. Price Group Base Price" <> '' THEN
            VALIDATE("Customer Price Group",Shop."Cust. Price Group Base Price");
          IF Shop."Cust. Disc. Group Base Price" <> '' THEN
            VALIDATE("Customer Disc. Group",Shop."Cust. Disc. Group Base Price");

          MODIFY(TRUE);

          // Dimension f�r Angebot/Auftrag setzten
          IF (ShopLanguage."Report Dimension" <> '') AND (ShopLanguage."Report Dimension Value" <> '')
             AND (DimValue.GET(ShopLanguage."Report Dimension",ShopLanguage."Report Dimension Value")) THEN BEGIN

            DimValueID := DimValue."Dimension Value ID";

            DimSetID := SalesHeader."Dimension Set ID";
            DimMgt.GetDimensionSet(TMPDimensionSetEntry,DimSetID);

            //Variable hat Eigenschaft Temporary=Yes!
            TMPDimensionSetEntry.RESET;
            TMPDimensionSetEntry.INIT;
            TMPDimensionSetEntry."Dimension Code" := ShopLanguage."Report Dimension";
            TMPDimensionSetEntry."Dimension Value Code" := ShopLanguage."Report Dimension Value";
            TMPDimensionSetEntry."Dimension Value ID" := DimValueID;
            TMPDimensionSetEntry.INSERT;

            DimSetID := DimMgt.GetDimensionSetID(TMPDimensionSetEntry);

            VALIDATE("Dimension Set ID",DimSetID);
          END;


          //Artikelzeilen f�r Lieferung anlegen
          IF ((PayAndOrShip = PayAndOrShip::ShipmentOnly) OR (PayAndOrShip = PayAndOrShip::Both)) THEN BEGIN
            SalesLineType := 1;
            "Is Webshop Subscr. Ship. Order" := TRUE;
            IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionOrderOption THEN BEGIN
              CurrItemNo := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Subscription Item No.";
              CurrItemQty := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Subscription Qty. Per Turn";
              WebshopSubscriptionItemLink.RESET;
              WebshopSubscriptionItemLink.SETRANGE("Subscription Code",WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code);
              WebshopSubscriptionItemLink.SETRANGE(WebshopSubscriptionItemLink."Item No.",CurrItemNo);
              WebshopSubscriptionItemLink.FINDFIRST;
              WebshopItem.RESET;
              Item.GET(CurrItemNo);

              WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLineType,CurrItemNo,CurrItemVarCode,CurrItemQty,
        WebshopSubscriptionItemLink."Fix Price",'',FALSE,
                                                WebshopSubscriptionItemLink."Discount %",Item."Sales Unit of Measure",FALSE,FALSE,'');

            END ELSE IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionSequenceItem THEN BEGIN
              WebshopSubscriptionItemLink.RESET;
              WebshopSubscriptionItemLink.SETRANGE("Subscription Code",WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code);
              WebshopSubscriptionItemLink.SETRANGE("Subscr. Seq. Step Line No.",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Next Sequence Step Line No.");
              WebshopSubscriptionItemLink.FINDSET;
              REPEAT
                QtyMultiplier := 1;
                IF WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Subscription Qty. Per Turn" > 1 THEN
                  QtyMultiplier := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Subscription Qty. Per Turn";

                CurrItemNo := WebshopSubscriptionItemLink."Item No.";

                CurrItemQty := WebshopSubscriptionItemLink.Quantity * QtyMultiplier;
                CurrItemVarCode := WebshopSubscriptionItemLink."Variant Code";
                Item.GET(CurrItemNo);

                //Tats�chliche SalesLine schreiben - 100% Zeilenrabatt
                SalesLineType := 1;
                WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLineType,CurrItemNo,CurrItemVarCode,CurrItemQty,
                                                  CurrItemUnitPrice,'',FALSE,100,Item."Sales Unit of Measure",FALSE,FALSE,'');
                //Textzeile Zahlung ?ber Abonnement
                SalesLineType := 0;
                WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLineType,'','',0,0,Text001,FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE,'');
              UNTIL WebshopSubscriptionItemLink.NEXT = 0;
            END;

            IF PayAndOrShip = PayAndOrShip::Both THEN BEGIN
              //Leerzeile
              SalesLineType := 0;
              WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLineType,'','',0,0,'',FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE,'');
            END;

            //N�chstes Datum Lieferauftrags-Erstellung setzen
            WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Next Order Creation Date" := NextShipOrderCreateDate;
            //Anzahl erledigte Turni heraufsetzen
            WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."No. of turns processed" += 1;

            WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.RESET;
            WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.SETRANGE("Subscription Code",WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code);
            WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.SETFILTER("Line No.",'>%1',WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Next Sequence Step Line No.");
            IF WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.FINDFIRST THEN
              WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Next Sequence Step Line No." := WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Line No.";
          END;

          IF ((PayAndOrShip = PayAndOrShip::PaymentOnly) OR (PayAndOrShip = PayAndOrShip::Both)) THEN BEGIN
            "Is Webshop Subscr. Inv. Order" := TRUE;
            IF (WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionSequenceItem) THEN
              CurrTotalPayAmount := WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Price per Billing Interval"
            ELSE IF (WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Type" = WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Type"::PerTurn) AND
                    (WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionOrderOption) THEN
              CurrTotalPayAmount := PayAmntPerTurn;

            IF NOT((WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionOrderOption) AND
                   ((WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Type" = WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Type"::Once) OR
                    (WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Type" = WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Type"::PerTurn))) THEN BEGIN

              //Tats�chliche SalesLine schreiben - G/L Account
              SalesLineType := SalesLine.Type::"G/L Account";
              WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLineType,Shop."G/L Account Subscriptions",'',1,
                                                CurrTotalPayAmount,Text002,FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE,'');
            END;

            //Textzeilen Zahlungs-Zeitraum
            SalesLineType := SalesLine.Type::" ";
            DateRangeText := FORMAT(WORKDATE) + ' - ' + FORMAT(NextPayOrderCreateDate);
            WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLineType,'','',0,0,Text002,FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE,'');
            WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLineType,'','',0,0,DateRangeText,FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE,'');

            //Zahlung registrieren
            IF (WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Next Order w. Payment Date" <= WORKDATE) THEN BEGIN
              SalesHeader.MODIFY(TRUE);
              GetSubscriptionTransactionIdForSalesHeaderAndSubscriptionCustomerLink(SalesHeader,WebshopSubscrCustomerLink);
            END;

            //N�chstes Datum Zahlungsauftrags-Erstellung setzen
            WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Next Order w. Payment Date" := NextPayOrderCreateDate;
          END;
        END;
        SalesHeader.MODIFY(TRUE);
        WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Next Planned Delivery Date" := NextPlannedDeliveryDate;
        WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.MODIFY(TRUE);

      END;
      OnAfterCreateSalOrdForSubscription(IsHandled,WebshopSubscriptionHeader,WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,PayAndOrShip);
    END;

    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateSubscriptionHeader@1000000170(WebshopSubscriptionHeader@1000000000 : Record 5447340);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      IsHandled@1000000002 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateSubscriptionHeader(IsHandled,WebshopSubscriptionHeader);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        WITH WebshopSubscriptionHeader DO BEGIN
          IF (GET(WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code)) THEN BEGIN
            CALCFIELDS("No. of Sequence Steps");
            CALCFIELDS("No. of Items");
            CALCFIELDS("No. of Customers");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_subscription_header');

            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[type]',Type);

            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[item_no_subscription_item]',"Item No. Subscription Item");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[payment_type]',"Payment Type");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[payment_type_description]',"Payment Type Description");

            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[turn_interval_description]',"Interval Description");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[subscription_duration]',"Subscription Duration");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[no_of_sequence_steps]',"No. of Sequence Steps");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[no_of_items]',"No. of Items");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[no_of_customers]',"No. of Customers");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[active]',"Is Active");
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

            //Auf 2 Teile aufteilen wegen L�nge
            CLEAR(CurrRec);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_subscription_header');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[valid_from]',"Starting Date Processing");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[valid_to]',"Ending Date Processing");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[cancel_period_date_formula]',"Cancel. Period Date Formula");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[cancel_period_description]',"Cancel. Period Description");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[price_per_billing_interval]',"Price per Billing Interval");

            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[orderable_to]',"Ending Date Orderability");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[language_code]',"Language Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[all_shops]',"All Shops");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[all_languages]',"All Languages");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[first_order_email_text]',"First Order Email Text");

            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[last_ship_email_text]',"Last Shipm. Email Text");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[shipping_option_line_no]',"Shipping Option Line No.");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ref_pay_date_from_start_date]',FORMAT("Ref. Pay. Date f. Start Date"));
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[allow_late_entry]',"Allow Late Entry");

            IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
              WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
            ELSE
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);
          END;

        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateSubscriptionHeader(IsHandled,WebshopSubscriptionHeader);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetAllSubscriptionCustomerLink@1000000175(CountRecords@1000000003 : Boolean);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000000 : Text[1024];
      ResponseText@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      NoOfSignups@1000000002 : Integer;
      Text001@1000000004 : TextConst 'DEU=Abonnenten;ENU=Subscribers';
      LogFile@1000000005 : File;
      IsHandled@1000000006 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeGetAllSubscriptionCustomerLink(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Alle neuen Abonnement-Beitritte abrufen

        WebshopSetup.GET;
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_subscr_customer_link');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[update_insert]',TRUE);
        //AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[subscription_order]',FALSE);
        ResponseText := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_no_of_records',CurrRec);
        IF EVALUATE(NoOfSignups,ResponseText) THEN BEGIN
          IF NoOfSignups > 0 THEN BEGIN
            IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(Text001,NoOfSignups);
            END ELSE BEGIN
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(Text001,NoOfSignups);
              GetSubscCustLink;
            END;
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
            ERROR(ResponseText)
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
              LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
              LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
              IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
               IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                ERROR(Error001);
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                  ERROR(Error002);
              END;
              LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
              LogFile.WRITE(ResponseText);
              LogFile.CLOSE;
              IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                MESSAGE(ResponseText);
            END ELSE
              ERROR(Error003);
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterGetAllSubscriptionCustomerLink(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetSubscCustLink@1000000184();
    VAR
      SubscrCustLink@1000000000 : Record 5447343;
      NextCustLink@1000000001 : Boolean;
      CurrRec@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      CustLinkID@1000000003 : Integer;
      SubscriptionCode@1000000004 : Code[30];
      CustomerNo@1000000005 : Code[30];
      OrderEmail@1000000006 : Text[256];
      LineNo@1000000007 : Integer;
      BillToName@1000000008 : Text[50];
      BillToName2@1000000009 : Text[50];
      BillToAddress@1000000010 : Text[80];
      BillToAddress2@1000000011 : Text[80];
      BillToCity@1000000012 : Text[50];
      ShipToName@1000000013 : Text[50];
      ShipToName2@1000000014 : Text[50];
      ShipToAddress@1000000015 : Text[80];
      ShipToAddress2@1000000016 : Text[80];
      ShipToCity@1000000017 : Text[50];
      CurrencyCode@1000000018 : Code[10];
      BillToPostCode@1000000019 : Text[10];
      BillToCounty@1000000020 : Code[30];
      BillToCountryRegion@1000000021 : Code[30];
      ShipToPostCode@1000000022 : Text[10];
      ShipToCounty@1000000023 : Code[30];
      ShipToCountryRegion@1000000024 : Code[30];
      WebshopShopCode@1000000025 : Code[30];
      WebshopLanguageCode@1000000026 : Code[10];
      WebshopPayOptLineNo@1000000027 : Integer;
      WebshopShipOptLineNo@1000000028 : Integer;
      CustPseudoPayLineNo@1000000030 : Integer;
      SignupDate@1000000031 : Date;
      CancellationDate@1000000032 : Date;
      NextOrderCreationDate@1000000033 : Date;
      NextOrderWPaymentDate@1000000034 : Date;
      NoOfTurnsProcessed@1000000035 : Integer;
      UserName@1000000037 : Text[125];
      SubscriptionItemNo@1000000036 : Code[30];
      NextSeqStepLineNo@1000000038 : Integer;
      SubscriptionQtyPerTurn@1000000039 : Decimal;
      Delimiter@1000000029 : Text[3];
      GetRec@1000000040 : Text[1024];
      ErrState@1000000041 : Boolean;
      ErrMsg@1000000042 : Text[1024];
      Error001@1000000043 : TextConst 'DEU=Es konnten keine wohlformatierten Daten aus dem Web f�r die Kunden-Abonnement-Verkn�pfung bezogen werden. Antwort: %1;ENU=No data for the customer-subscription-link could be retrieved from the web.';
      Error002@1000000044 : TextConst 'DEU=Die Daten aus dem Web f�r die Kunden-Abonnement-Verkn�pfung sind nicht valide. Bitte Verkn�pfung mit ID %1 pr�fen. Antwort: %2;ENU=The data retrieved from the web for the customer-subscription-link isn''t valid. Please check the link with ID %1.';
      Error003@1000000045 : TextConst 'DEU=Verbindungsfehler. Antwort: %3;ENU=Connection-Error.';
      TMPDateString@72000 : Text[20];
      SetRec@72001 : Text[1024];
      IsHandled@1000000046 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeGetSubscCustLink(IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Alle neuen Bestellungen abrufen
        Delimiter := '-;-';

        WITH SubscrCustLink DO REPEAT
          NextCustLink := FALSE;

          //Teil 1 anfordern
          CLEAR(CurrRec);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_subscr_customer_link');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'delimiter',Delimiter);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'fields','id,subscription_code,customer_no,webshop_order_email,line_no,bill_to_name,bill_to_name_2,bill_to_address,' +
                                 'bill_to_address_2,bill_to_city,ship_to_name,ship_to_name_2,ship_to_address,ship_to_address_2,ship_to_city,'+
                                 'currency_code,bill_to_post_code,bill_to_county,bill_to_country_region_code,ship_to_post_code,ship_to_county,' +
                                 'ship_to_country_region_code,webshop_shop_code,webshop_language_code,webshop_pay_opt_line_no,webshop_ship_opt_line_no');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[update_insert]',TRUE);
          GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_content',CurrRec);
          IF STRPOS(GetRec,Delimiter) <> 0 THEN BEGIN

            // Webshop-Bestellung Teil 1 in Variablen speichern
            EVALUATE(CustLinkID,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(SubscriptionCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(CustomerNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(OrderEmail,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(LineNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(BillToName,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(BillToName2,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(BillToAddress,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(BillToAddress2,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(BillToCity,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(ShipToName,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(ShipToName2,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(ShipToAddress,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(ShipToAddress2,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(ShipToCity,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(CurrencyCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(BillToPostCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(BillToCounty,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(BillToCountryRegion,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(ShipToPostCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(ShipToCounty,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(ShipToCountryRegion,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(WebshopShopCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(WebshopLanguageCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(WebshopPayOptLineNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
            EVALUATE(WebshopShipOptLineNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));


            IF
                (WebshopShopCode <> '') AND
                (WebshopLanguageCode <> '') AND
                (CustomerNo <>'') AND
                (OrderEmail <> '') AND
                (SubscriptionCode <> '') THEN BEGIN
              //Teil 2 anfordern
              CLEAR(CurrRec);
              AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_subscr_customer_link');
              AddVar(CurrRec,'delimiter',Delimiter);
              AddVar(CurrRec,'fields','customer_pseudo_pay_line_no,signup_date,cancellation_date,next_order_creation_date,next_order_w_payment_date,' +
                                     'no_of_turns_processed,user_name,subscription_item_no,next_sequence_step_line_no,subscription_qty_per_turn');
              AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
              AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[update_insert]',TRUE);
              GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_content',CurrRec);

              IF STRPOS(GetRec,Delimiter) <> 0 THEN BEGIN
                // Webshop-Bestellung Teil 2 in Variablen speichern
                EVALUATE(CustPseudoPayLineNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
                EVALUATE(TMPDateString,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
                IF (TMPDateString <> '0000-00-00') AND (TMPDateString <> '00-00-0000') AND (TMPDateString <> '1970-01-01') AND (TMPDateString <> '01-01-1970') THEN BEGIN
                  //MESSAGE('DATE-STR:' + TMPDateString);
                  EVALUATE(SignupDate,TMPDateString);
                  CLEAR(TMPDateString);
                END;
                EVALUATE(TMPDateString,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
                IF (TMPDateString <> '0000-00-00') AND (TMPDateString <> '00-00-0000') AND (TMPDateString <> '1970-01-01') AND (TMPDateString <> '01-01-1970') THEN BEGIN
                    //      MESSAGE('DATE-STR:' + TMPDateString);
                    EVALUATE(CancellationDate,TMPDateString);
                    CLEAR(TMPDateString);
                END;
                EVALUATE(TMPDateString,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
                IF (TMPDateString <> '0000-00-00') AND (TMPDateString <> '00-00-0000') AND (TMPDateString <> '1970-01-01') AND (TMPDateString <> '01-01-1970') THEN BEGIN
                      //    MESSAGE('DATE-STR:' + TMPDateString);
                    EVALUATE(NextOrderCreationDate,TMPDateString);
                    CLEAR(TMPDateString);
                END;
                EVALUATE(TMPDateString,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
                IF (TMPDateString <> '0000-00-00') AND (TMPDateString <> '00-00-0000') AND (TMPDateString <> '1970-01-01') AND (TMPDateString <> '01-01-1970') THEN BEGIN
                        //  MESSAGE('DATE-STR:' + TMPDateString);
                    EVALUATE(NextOrderWPaymentDate,TMPDateString);
                    CLEAR(TMPDateString);
                END;
                EVALUATE(NoOfTurnsProcessed,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
                EVALUATE(UserName,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
                EVALUATE(SubscriptionItemNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
                EVALUATE(NextSeqStepLineNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
                EVALUATE(SubscriptionQtyPerTurn,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              END ELSE BEGIN
                ErrState := TRUE;
                ErrMsg := STRSUBSTNO(Error001,GetRec);
              END;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              ErrState := TRUE;
              ErrMsg := STRSUBSTNO(Error002,CustLinkID,GetRec);
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            ErrState := TRUE;
            ErrMsg := STRSUBSTNO(Error001 + '-2',GetRec);
          END;

          IF ErrState THEN
            ERROR(ErrMsg);

          CLEAR(SetRec);
          AddVar(SetRec,'table','shop_subscr_customer_link');
          AddVar(SetRec,'primary[id]',CustLinkID);
          AddVar(SetRec,'data[update_insert]',FALSE);
          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',SetRec);
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;

        UNTIL NOT NextCustLink;

      END;
      OnAfterGetSubscCustLink(IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdAllSubscCustLink@1000000176(CountRecords@1000000000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      WebshopSubscrCustomerLink@1000000001 : Record 5447343;
      IsHandled@1000000002 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdAllSubscCustLink(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        WITH WebshopSubscrCustomerLink DO BEGIN
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
            IF FINDSET(TRUE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              UpdateSubscriptionCustLink(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink);
              "Last Date Updated" := CURRENTDATETIME;
              MODIFY(FALSE);
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            UNTIL NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdAllSubscCustLink(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdAllSubscCustLinkForHeader@1000000187(WebshopSubscriptionHeader@1000000000 : Record 5447340);
    VAR
      WebshopSubscrCustomerLink@1000000001 : Record 5447343;
      IsHandled@1000000002 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdAllSubscCustLinkForHeader(WebshopSubscriptionHeader,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        WITH WebshopSubscrCustomerLink DO BEGIN
          SETFILTER("Subscription Code",WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code);
          IF FINDSET(TRUE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            UpdateSubscriptionCustLink(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink);
            "Last Date Updated" := CURRENTDATETIME;
            MODIFY(FALSE);
          UNTIL NEXT = 0;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdAllSubscCustLinkForHeader(WebshopSubscriptionHeader,IsHandled);
    END;
