    PROCEDURE GetCustSubscNextDeliveryDate@1000000168(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink@1000000000 : Record 5447343;AlternativeBaseDate@1000000011 : Date;BaseDateHandling@1000000017 : 'BaseDateIncluded,BaseDateExcluded') NextPlannedShippingDate : Date;
    VAR
      WebshopSubscriptionHeader@1000000001 : Record 5447340;
      SalesHeader@1000000002 : Record 36;
      ShipmentHeader@1000000003 : Record 110;
      NextTurnDateFormula@1000000004 : DateFormula;
      Shop@1000000005 : Record 5447301;
      ReferenceDate@1000000006 : Date;
      Error001@1000000007 : TextConst 'DEU=Es kann kein n�chstes geplantes Lieferdatum f�r eine Abonnement-Kundenzuweisung erzeugt werden, die bereits einen oder mehrere Turni durchlaufen hat, zu der jedoch keine Verkaufsk�pfe mehr bestehen.;ENU=It is not possible to determine a next planned shipping date for a Subscription-Customer-link which has already completed one or more turns, but for which no Sales Headers exist.';
      WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep@1000000008 : Record 5447341;
      MinSeqStepLineNo@1000000009 : Integer;
      CancellationCutoffDate@1000000010 : Date;
      IntermediateCalculationDate@1000000012 : Date;
      CurrReferenceTurn@1000000013 : Integer;
      BaseDate@1000000014 : Date;
      CurrReferenceStepLineNo@1000000015 : Integer;
      NextWorkdayDirection@1000000016 : '>,<';
      Error002@1000000018 : TextConst 'DEU=Das Abonnement enth�lt keine Sequenzschritte.;ENU=The Subscription contains no sequence steps.';
      IsHandled@1000000019 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeGetCustSubscNextDeliveryDate(IsHandled,WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,AlternativeBaseDate,BaseDateHandling);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        //Ermittelt das N�chste Lieferdatum f�r eine Abonnement-Kundenzuweisung

        BaseDate := WORKDATE;
        IF (AlternativeBaseDate > 0D) AND (AlternativeBaseDate <> WORKDATE) THEN
          BaseDate := AlternativeBaseDate;

        WebshopSubscriptionHeader.GET(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Subscription Code");

        IF (NOT WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Is Active") OR
           ((BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded) AND (WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Starting Date Processing" <> 0D) AND (BaseDate <
        WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Starting Date Processing")) OR
           ((BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded) AND (WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Ending Date Processing" <> 0D) AND (BaseDate >
        WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Ending Date Processing")) OR
           ((BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded) AND (WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Starting Date Processing" <> 0D) AND (BaseDate <=
        WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Starting Date Processing")) OR
           ((BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded) AND (WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Ending Date Processing" <> 0D) AND (BaseDate >=
        WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Ending Date Processing")) THEN BEGIN
          NextPlannedShippingDate := 0D;
          EXIT(NextPlannedShippingDate);
        END;

        IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionOrderOption THEN BEGIN

          CurrReferenceTurn := 1;
          ReferenceDate := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Signup Date";
          NextTurnDateFormula := WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Turn Interval Formula";
          IntermediateCalculationDate := CALCDATE(WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Start Date Formula",ReferenceDate);

          IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopManagement.GetNextWorkday(IntermediateCalculationDate,NextWorkdayDirection::">");
          IF (((IntermediateCalculationDate < BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded)) OR
             ((IntermediateCalculationDate <= BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded))) THEN REPEAT
            ReferenceDate := IntermediateCalculationDate;
            IntermediateCalculationDate := CALCDATE(NextTurnDateFormula,ReferenceDate);
            IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopManagement.GetNextWorkday(IntermediateCalculationDate,NextWorkdayDirection::">");
            CurrReferenceTurn += 1;
          UNTIL ((IntermediateCalculationDate >= BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded)) OR
                ((IntermediateCalculationDate > BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded));

          IF ((IntermediateCalculationDate < BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded)) OR
             ((IntermediateCalculationDate <= BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded)) THEN BEGIN
            NextPlannedShippingDate := 0D;
            EXIT(NextPlannedShippingDate);
          END;

        END ELSE IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionSequenceItem THEN BEGIN

          WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.RESET;
          WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.SETRANGE("Subscription Code",WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code);
          IF NOT WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.FINDFIRST THEN
            ERROR(Error002);
          MinSeqStepLineNo := WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Line No.";
          IF WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Fix Date" > 0D THEN
            IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Fix Date"
          ELSE
            IntermediateCalculationDate := CALCDATE(WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.Interval,WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Signup Date");

          IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Start Type" = WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Start Type"::Anew THEN
            IntermediateCalculationDate := CALCDATE(WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.Interval,IntermediateCalculationDate)
          ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.RESET;
            WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.SETRANGE("Subscription Code",WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code);
            WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.SETFILTER("Line No.",'>%1',MinSeqStepLineNo);

            IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopManagement.GetNextWorkday(IntermediateCalculationDate,NextWorkdayDirection::">");
            IF WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.FINDSET AND
               ((IntermediateCalculationDate < BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded)) OR
               ((IntermediateCalculationDate <= BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded)) THEN REPEAT
              IF WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Fix Date" > 0D THEN
                IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Fix Date"
              ELSE
                IntermediateCalculationDate := CALCDATE(WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.Interval,IntermediateCalculationDate);
              IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopManagement.GetNextWorkday(IntermediateCalculationDate,NextWorkdayDirection::">");
            UNTIL (WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.NEXT = 0) OR ((IntermediateCalculationDate >= BaseDate)
                  AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded)) OR
                  ((IntermediateCalculationDate > BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded));

            IF ((IntermediateCalculationDate < BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded)) OR
               ((IntermediateCalculationDate <= BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded)) THEN BEGIN
              NextPlannedShippingDate := 0D;
              EXIT(NextPlannedShippingDate);
            END;
          END;

        END;

        NextPlannedShippingDate := IntermediateCalculationDate;
        IF WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Cancellation Date" > 0D THEN BEGIN
          CancellationCutoffDate := CALCDATE(WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Cancel. Period Date Formula",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.
                                             "Cancellation Date");
          IF NextPlannedShippingDate >= CancellationCutoffDate THEN
            NextPlannedShippingDate := 0D;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterGetCustSubscNextDeliveryDate(IsHandled,WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,AlternativeBaseDate,BaseDateHandling);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetCustSubscrNextShipOrderDate@1000000178(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink@1000000000 : Record 5447343;Shop@1000000001 : Record 5447301;AlternativeBaseDate@1000000003 : Date;BaseDateHandling@1000000006 : 'BaseDateIncluded,BaseDateExcluded') NextOrderCreationDate : Date;
    VAR
      NextPlannedShippingDate@1000000002 : Date;
      BaseDate@1000000004 : Date;
      NextWorkdayDirection@1000000005 : '>,<';
      IsHandled@1000000007 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeGetCustSubscrNextShipOrderDate(IsHandled,WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,Shop,AlternativeBaseDate,BaseDateHandling);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        //Ermittelt das n�chste Datum f�r die Erzeugung eines Verkaufsauftrags f�r Abonnement-Lieferabwicklung von einem gegebenen Referenzdatum aus

        BaseDate := WORKDATE;
        IF (AlternativeBaseDate <> WORKDATE) AND (AlternativeBaseDate > 0D) THEN
          BaseDate := AlternativeBaseDate;

        NextPlannedShippingDate := GetCustSubscNextDeliveryDate(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,AlternativeBaseDate,BaseDateHandling);

        IF NextPlannedShippingDate = 0D THEN
          EXIT(0D);

        Shop.GET(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Webshop Shop Code");
        NextOrderCreationDate := CALCDATE(Shop."Forerun Subscr. Order Create",NextPlannedShippingDate);
        IF ((NextOrderCreationDate < BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded)) OR
           ((NextOrderCreationDate <= BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded)) THEN BEGIN
          NextPlannedShippingDate := GetCustSubscNextDeliveryDate(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,NextOrderCreationDate,BaseDateHandling
        ::BaseDateExcluded);
          NextOrderCreationDate := CALCDATE(Shop."Forerun Subscr. Order Create",NextPlannedShippingDate);
        END;
        NextOrderCreationDate := WebshopManagement.GetNextWorkday(NextOrderCreationDate,NextWorkdayDirection::">");

      END;
      OnAfterGetCustSubscrNextShipOrderDate(IsHandled,WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,Shop,AlternativeBaseDate,BaseDateHandling);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetCustSubscrNextSeqStepLineNo@1000000181(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink@1000000000 : Record 5447343;AlternativeBaseDate@1000000007 : Date;BaseDateHandling@1000000006 : 'BaseDateIncluded,BaseDateExcluded') NextLineNo : Integer;
    VAR
      SalesHeader@1000000001 : Record 36;
      WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep@1000000002 : Record 5447341;
      WebshopSubscriptionHeader@1000000003 : Record 5447340;
      Error001@1000000004 : TextConst 'DEU=Es kann keine n�chste Sequenz-Schritt-Zeilennummer f�r ein Abonnement vom Typ Bestelloption ermittelt werden.;ENU=It is not possible to generate a next Sequence Step Line No. for a subscription of type Order Option.';
      BaseDate@1000000005 : Date;
      IntermediateCalculationDate@1000000008 : Date;
      IntermediateSeqStepLineNo@1000000009 : Integer;
      NextWorkdayDirection@1000000010 : '>,<';
      IsHandled@1000000011 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeGetCustSubscrNextSeqStepLineNo(IsHandled,WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,AlternativeBaseDate,BaseDateHandling);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        //Ermittelt die n�chste Abonnement-Sequenz-Schritt Zeilennummer f�r eine Abonnement-Kundenzuweisung von einem gegebenen Referenzdatum aus

        BaseDate := WORKDATE;
        IF (AlternativeBaseDate <> WORKDATE) AND (AlternativeBaseDate > 0D) THEN
          BaseDate := AlternativeBaseDate;

        WebshopSubscriptionHeader.GET(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Subscription Code");
        IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type <> WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionSequenceItem THEN
          ERROR(Error001);

        WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.SETRANGE("Subscription Code",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Subscription Code");
        IF NOT WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.FINDFIRST THEN
          EXIT(0);

        IntermediateSeqStepLineNo := WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Line No.";
        IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Signup Date";
        IF WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Fix Date" = 0D THEN
          IntermediateCalculationDate := CALCDATE(WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.Interval,WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Signup Date");

        IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopManagement.GetNextWorkday(IntermediateCalculationDate,NextWorkdayDirection::">");
        IF ((IntermediateCalculationDate < BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded)) OR
           ((IntermediateCalculationDate <= BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded)) THEN REPEAT
          IntermediateSeqStepLineNo := WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Line No.";
          IF WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Fix Date" > 0D THEN BEGIN
            IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Fix Date";
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IntermediateCalculationDate := CALCDATE(WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.Interval,IntermediateCalculationDate);
          END;
          IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopManagement.GetNextWorkday(IntermediateCalculationDate,NextWorkdayDirection::">");
        UNTIL (WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.NEXT = 0) OR ((IntermediateCalculationDate >= BaseDate) AND
              (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded)) OR ((IntermediateCalculationDate > BaseDate) AND
              (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded));

        IF ((IntermediateCalculationDate < BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded)) OR
           ((IntermediateCalculationDate <= BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded)) THEN
           EXIT(0);

        NextLineNo := IntermediateSeqStepLineNo;

      END;
      OnAfterGetCustSubscrNextSeqStepLineNo(IsHandled,WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,AlternativeBaseDate,BaseDateHandling);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetCustSubscrNextPayOrderDate@1000000188(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink@1000000000 : Record 5447343;AlternativeBaseDate@1000000009 : Date;BaseDateHandling@1000000008 : 'BaseDateIncluded,BaseDateExcluded') PayOrderCreateDate : Date;
    VAR
      WebshopSubscriptionHeader@1000000001 : Record 5447340;
      WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep@1000000002 : Record 5447341;
      Shop@1000000003 : Record 5447301;
      PayIntervalFormula@1000000004 : DateFormula;
      StartDate@1000000005 : Date;
      IntermediateCalculationDate@1000000006 : Date;
      BaseDate@1000000010 : Date;
      NextWorkdayDirection@1000000007 : '>,<';
      IsHandled@1000000011 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeGetCustSubscrNextPayOrderDate(IsHandled,WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,AlternativeBaseDate,BaseDateHandling);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        //Ermittelt das n�chste Datum f�r die Erzeugung eines Verkaufsauftrags f�r Abonnement-Lieferabwicklung von einem gegebenen Referenzdatum aus

        BaseDate := WORKDATE;
        IF (AlternativeBaseDate <> WORKDATE) AND (AlternativeBaseDate > 0D) THEN
          BaseDate := AlternativeBaseDate;

        WebshopSubscriptionHeader.GET(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Subscription Code");
        Shop.GET(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Webshop Shop Code");

        CASE WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Type" OF
          WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Type"::Once:
            BEGIN
              PayOrderCreateDate := 0D;
              EXIT(PayOrderCreateDate);
            END;
          WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Type"::PerTurn:
            BEGIN
              PayOrderCreateDate := GetCustSubscrNextShipOrderDate(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,Shop,AlternativeBaseDate,
        BaseDateHandling);
              EXIT(PayOrderCreateDate);
            END;
          WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Type"::Monthly:
            EVALUATE(PayIntervalFormula,'<CD+1M>');
          WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Type"::Quarterly:
            EVALUATE(PayIntervalFormula,'<CD+3M>');
          WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Type"::SemiAnnually:
            EVALUATE(PayIntervalFormula,'<CD+6M>');
          WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Type"::Annually:
            EVALUATE(PayIntervalFormula,'<CD+12M>');
        END;

        IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionOrderOption THEN BEGIN
          StartDate := CALCDATE(WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Start Date Formula",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Signup Date");
        END ELSE IF (WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionSequenceItem) AND
                (WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Start Type" = WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Start Type"::Anew) THEN BEGIN
          WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.RESET;
          WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.SETRANGE("Subscription Code",WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code);
          IF NOT WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
            PayOrderCreateDate := 0D;
            EXIT(PayOrderCreateDate);
          END;
          StartDate := CALCDATE(WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.Interval,WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Signup Date");
        END ELSE IF (WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionSequenceItem) AND
                (WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Start Type" = WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Start Type"::Join) THEN BEGIN
          WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.RESET;
          WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.SETRANGE("Subscription Code",WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code);
          IF NOT WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
            PayOrderCreateDate := 0D;
            EXIT(PayOrderCreateDate);
          END;
          StartDate := WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Fix Date";
        END;

        IntermediateCalculationDate := CALCDATE(Shop."Forerun Payment Capture",StartDate);
        IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopManagement.GetNextWorkday(IntermediateCalculationDate,NextWorkdayDirection::">");

        IF ((IntermediateCalculationDate < BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded)) OR
           ((IntermediateCalculationDate <= BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded)) THEN REPEAT
          IntermediateCalculationDate := CALCDATE(PayIntervalFormula,IntermediateCalculationDate);
          IntermediateCalculationDate := CALCDATE(Shop."Forerun Payment Capture",IntermediateCalculationDate);
          IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopManagement.GetNextWorkday(IntermediateCalculationDate,NextWorkdayDirection::">");
        UNTIL ((IntermediateCalculationDate >= BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded)) OR
              ((IntermediateCalculationDate > BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded));

        PayOrderCreateDate := IntermediateCalculationDate;

      END;
      OnAfterGetCustSubscrNextPayOrderDate(IsHandled,WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,AlternativeBaseDate,BaseDateHandling);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetCustSubscrItQtyInDateRange@1000000179(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink@1000000002 : Record 5447343;StartDate@1000000000 : Date;EndDate@1000000001 : Date) TotalItemQuantity : Decimal;
    VAR
      NoOfTurns@1000000003 : Integer;
      IntermediateCalculationDate@1000000004 : Date;
      BaseDateHandling@1000000005 : 'BaseDateIncluded,BaseDateExcluded';
      IsHandled@1000000006 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeGetCustSubscrItQtyInDateRange(IsHandled,WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,StartDate,EndDate);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        //Ermittelt die insgesamt zu liefernde Menge des abonnierten Artikels innerhalb eines Zeitrahmens f�r eine Abonnement-Kundenzuweisung


        NoOfTurns := GetCustSubNoOfTurnsInDateRange(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,StartDate,EndDate);
        TotalItemQuantity := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Subscription Qty. Per Turn" * NoOfTurns;

      END;
      OnAfterGetCustSubscrItQtyInDateRange(IsHandled,WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,StartDate,EndDate);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetCustSubNoOfTurnsInDateRange@1000000182(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink@1000000002 : Record 5447343;StartDate@1000000000 : Date;EndDate@1000000001 : Date) NoOfTurns : Integer;
    VAR
      IntermediateCalculationDate@1000000004 : Date;
      BaseDateHandling@1000000005 : 'BaseDateIncluded,BaseDateExcluded';
      WebshopSubscriptionHeader@1000000006 : Record 5447340;
      WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep@1000000003 : Record 5447341;
      EmptyDateFormula@1000000007 : DateFormula;
      NextWorkdayDirection@1000000008 : '>,<';
      IsHandled@1000000009 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeGetCustSubNoOfTurnsInDateRange(IsHandled,WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,StartDate,EndDate);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        //Ermittelt Die Anzahl Turni f�r eine Abonnement-Kundenzuweisung innerhalb eines gewissen Zeitrahmens

        WebshopSubscriptionHeader.GET(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Subscription Code");
        NoOfTurns := 0;

        IntermediateCalculationDate := StartDate;

        IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionOrderOption THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            IntermediateCalculationDate := GetCustSubscNextDeliveryDate(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,IntermediateCalculationDate,
        BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded);
            IF (IntermediateCalculationDate > 0D) AND (IntermediateCalculationDate > StartDate) AND (IntermediateCalculationDate <=
        EndDate) THEN
              NoOfTurns += 1;
          UNTIL (IntermediateCalculationDate = 0D) OR (IntermediateCalculationDate > EndDate);
        END ELSE IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionSequenceItem THEN BEGIN
          WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.SETRANGE("Subscription Code",WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code);
          IF NOT WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.FINDFIRST THEN
            EXIT(0);
          IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Signup Date";
          REPEAT
            IF WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Fix Date" <> 0D THEN
              IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Fix Date"
            ELSE IF WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.Interval <> EmptyDateFormula THEN
              IntermediateCalculationDate := CALCDATE(WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.Interval,IntermediateCalculationDate);
            IF (IntermediateCalculationDate > StartDate) AND (IntermediateCalculationDate <= EndDate) THEN
              NoOfTurns += 1;
          UNTIL (WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.NEXT = 0) OR (IntermediateCalculationDate > EndDate);
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterGetCustSubNoOfTurnsInDateRange(IsHandled,WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,StartDate,EndDate);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SendAllSubscInvoiceMail@1000000195(CountRecords@1000000009 : Boolean);
    VAR
      SalesInvoiceHeader@1000000000 : Record 112;
      WebshopSubscriptionHeader@1000000001 : Record 5447340;
      WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep@1000000002 : Record 5447341;
      WebshopSubscrCustomerLink@1000000003 : Record 5447343;
      PostFieldsString@1000000004 : Text[1024];
      TextModule@1000000005 : Record 5447309;
      WebshopLanguage@1000000006 : Record 5447302;
      AttachmentFileName@1000000007 : Text[255];
      WebshopShop@1000000008 : Record 5447301;
      CheckAttachmentPostString@1000000013 : Text[1024];
      CheckAttachmentResponse@1000000012 : Text[1024];
      AttachmentPresent@1000000011 : Boolean;
      IsHandled@1000000010 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeSendAllSubscInvoiceMail(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        WebshopSubscriptionHeader.SETRANGE("Is Active",TRUE);
        WebshopSubscriptionHeader.SETFILTER("Payment Email Text",'<>%1','');
        IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(WebshopSubscriptionHeader.TABLECAPTION,WebshopSubscriptionHeader.COUNT)
        END ELSE BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(WebshopSubscriptionHeader.TABLECAPTION,WebshopSubscriptionHeader.COUNT);
          IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            IF ((WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Starting Date Processing" = 0D) OR (WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Starting Date Processing" <= WORKDATE)) AND
                ((WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Ending Date Processing" = 0D) OR (WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Ending Date Processing" >= WORKDATE)) THEN BEGIN
                TextModule.GET(WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Email Text");
                SalesInvoiceHeader.SETRANGE("Webshop Subscription Code",WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code);
                SalesInvoiceHeader.SETRANGE("Send Webshop Invoice Mail",TRUE);
                SalesInvoiceHeader.SETRANGE("Date Webshop Invoice Mail Sent",0DT);
                SalesInvoiceHeader.SETFILTER("Posting Date",'>%1',0D);
                IF SalesInvoiceHeader.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
                  WebshopShop.GET(SalesInvoiceHeader."Webshop Shop Code");
                  WebshopLanguage.GET(SalesInvoiceHeader."Webshop Language Code");
                  WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.SETRANGE("Subscription Code",WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code);
                  WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.SETRANGE("Customer No.",SalesInvoiceHeader."Sell-to Customer No.");
                  WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.SETRANGE("Line No.",SalesInvoiceHeader."Webshop Subscr. Cust. Line No.");
                  WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.FINDFIRST;
                  AddVar(PostFieldsString,'customer_no',SalesInvoiceHeader."Sell-to Customer No.");
                  AddVar(PostFieldsString,'customer_name',SalesInvoiceHeader."Bill-to Name");
                  AddVar(PostFieldsString,'invoice_no',SalesInvoiceHeader."No.");
                  IF WebshopLanguage."Report PDF Invoice" <> 0 THEN
                    AttachmentFileName := WebshopManagement.CreateDocumentPDF(1,WebshopLanguage."Shop Code",WebshopLanguage.Code,SalesInvoiceHeader."No.");
                  SendTextModuleMail(WebshopLanguage,TextModule,WebshopShop."E-Mail Sender Addr.",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Webshop Order Email",PostFieldsString,AttachmentFileName);
                  SalesInvoiceHeader."Send Webshop Invoice Mail" := FALSE;
                  SalesInvoiceHeader."Date Webshop Invoice Mail Sent" := CURRENTDATETIME;
                  SalesInvoiceHeader.MODIFY(FALSE);
                  WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
                UNTIL SalesInvoiceHeader.NEXT = 0;
            END;
          UNTIL WebshopSubscriptionHeader.NEXT = 0;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterSendAllSubscInvoiceMail(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

                  WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
                UNTIL SalesShipmentHeader.NEXT = 0;
            END;
          UNTIL WebshopSubscriptionHeader.NEXT = 0;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterSendAllSubscShipmentMail(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SendAllSubscCombinedMail@1000000202(CountRecords@1000000009 : Boolean);
    VAR
      SalesShipmentHeader@1000000000 : Record 110;
      WebshopSubscriptionHeader@1000000001 : Record 5447340;
      WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep@1000000002 : Record 5447341;
      WebshopSubscrCustomerLink@1000000003 : Record 5447343;
      PostFieldsString@1000000004 : Text[1024];
      TextModule@1000000005 : Record 5447309;
      WebshopLanguage@1000000006 : Record 5447302;
      AttachmentFileName@1000000007 : Text[255];
      WebshopShop@1000000008 : Record 5447301;
      IsHandled@1000000010 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeSendAllSubscCombinedMail(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        WebshopSubscriptionHeader.SETRANGE("Is Active",TRUE);
        WebshopSubscriptionHeader.SETFILTER(WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Pay. And Ship. Email Text",'<>%1','');
        IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(WebshopSubscriptionHeader.TABLECAPTION,WebshopSubscriptionHeader.COUNT)
        END ELSE BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(WebshopSubscriptionHeader.TABLECAPTION,WebshopSubscriptionHeader.COUNT);
          IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            IF ((WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Starting Date Processing" = 0D) OR (WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Starting Date Processing" <= WORKDATE)) AND
                ((WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Ending Date Processing" = 0D) OR (WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Ending Date Processing" >= WORKDATE)) THEN BEGIN
                TextModule.GET(WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Pay. And Ship. Email Text");
                SalesShipmentHeader.SETRANGE("Webshop Subscription Code",WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code);
                SalesShipmentHeader.SETRANGE("Send Webshop Shipping Notice",TRUE);
                SalesShipmentHeader.SETRANGE("Date Web. Ship. Notice Sent",0DT);
                SalesShipmentHeader.SETFILTER("Posting Date",'>%1',0D);
                IF SalesShipmentHeader.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
                  WebshopShop.GET(SalesShipmentHeader."Webshop Shop Code");
                  WebshopLanguage.GET(SalesShipmentHeader."Webshop Language Code");
                  WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.SETRANGE("Subscription Code",WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code);
                  WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.SETRANGE("Customer No.",SalesShipmentHeader."Sell-to Customer No.");
                  WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.SETRANGE("Line No.",SalesShipmentHeader."Webshop Subscr. Cust. Line No.");
                  WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.FINDFIRST;
                  AddVar(PostFieldsString,'customer_no',SalesShipmentHeader."Sell-to Customer No.");
                  AddVar(PostFieldsString,'customer_name',SalesShipmentHeader."Bill-to Name");
                  AddVar(PostFieldsString,'shipment_no',SalesShipmentHeader."No.");
                  IF WebshopLanguage."Report PDF Invoice" <> 0 THEN
                    AttachmentFileName := WebshopManagement.CreateDocumentPDF(2,WebshopLanguage."Shop Code",WebshopLanguage.Code,SalesShipmentHeader."No.");
                  SendTextModuleMail(WebshopLanguage,TextModule,WebshopShop."E-Mail Sender Addr.",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Webshop Order Email",PostFieldsString,AttachmentFileName);
                  SalesShipmentHeader."Send Webshop Shipping Notice" := FALSE;
                  SalesShipmentHeader."Date Web. Ship. Notice Sent" := CURRENTDATETIME;
                  SalesShipmentHeader.MODIFY(FALSE);
                  WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
                UNTIL SalesShipmentHeader.NEXT = 0;
            END;
          UNTIL WebshopSubscriptionHeader.NEXT = 0;
        END;
      END;
      OnAfterSendAllSubscCombinedMail(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE GetSubscriptionTransactionIdForSalesHeaderAndSubscriptionCustomerLink@1000000260(VAR SalesHeader@1000000000 : Record 36;ParamSubscriptionCustomerLink@1000000001 : Record 5447343);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000002 : Text;
      GetRec@1000000003 : Text;
      Delimiter@1000000006 : Text;
      PaymentTransactionId@1000000004 : Text[30];
      PaymentId@1000000005 : Text[32];
    BEGIN
      AddVar(CurrRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
      AddVar(CurrRec,'creditCardNumber',ParamSubscriptionCustomerLink."Credit Card Pseudo No.");
      AddVar(CurrRec,'creditCardType',ParamSubscriptionCustomerLink."Credit Card Type");
      AddVar(CurrRec,'creditCardExpirationDate',ParamSubscriptionCustomerLink."Credit Card Expire Date");
      AddVar(CurrRec,'shopCode',SalesHeader."Webshop Shop Code");
      AddVar(CurrRec,'languageCode',SalesHeader."Webshop Language Code");
      AddVar(CurrRec,'orderNo',SalesHeader."Webshop Language Code");
      AddVar(CurrRec,'paymentOptionLineNo',SalesHeader."Webshop Payment Opt. Line No.");
      SalesHeader.CALCFIELDS("Amount Including VAT");
      AddVar(CurrRec,'amount',SalesHeader."Amount Including VAT");
      AddVar(CurrRec,'currencyCode',SalesHeader."Currency Code");
      GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('PaySubscription',CurrRec);
      Delimiter := '-|-';
      IF STRPOS(GetRec,Delimiter) <> 0 THEN BEGIN
        EVALUATE(PaymentTransactionId,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
        EVALUATE(PaymentId,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
        SalesHeader."Webshop Payment ID" := PaymentId;
        SalesHeader."Webshop Payment Transaction ID" := PaymentTransactionId;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        SalesHeader."Webshop Payment Error" := GetRec;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllCampaignHeader@1000000190(CountRecords@1000000000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      WebshopCampaignHeader@1000000001 : Record 5447345;
      IsHandled@1000000002 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateAllCampaignHeader(CountRecords,IsHandled);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        WITH WebshopCampaignHeader DO BEGIN
          IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
          END ELSE BEGIN
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
            IF FINDSET THEN REPEAT
              IF ("Date Last Updated" = 0DT) OR ("Date Last Modified" > "Date Last Updated") THEN
                UpdateCampaignHeader(WebshopCampaignHeader);
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            UNTIL NEXT=0;
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateAllCampaignHeader(CountRecords,IsHandled);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateCampaignByCode@1000000197(WebshopCampaignCode@1000000000 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      WebshopCampaignHeader@1000000001 : Record 5447345;
      WebshopCampaignElement@1000000002 : Record 5447346;
      IsHandled@1000000003 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeUpdateCampaignByCode(IsHandled,WebshopCampaignCode);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        IF WebshopCampaignHeader.GET(WebshopCampaignCode) THEN BEGIN
          UpdateCampaignHeader(WebshopCampaignHeader);
          WebshopCampaignElement.SETRANGE("Link-to Header Code",WebshopCampaignHeader.Code);
          IF WebshopCampaignElement.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            UpdateCampaignElement(WebshopCampaignElement);
          UNTIL WebshopCampaignElement.NEXT = 0;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterUpdateCampaignByCode(IsHandled,WebshopCampaignCode);
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteCampaignByCode@1000000198(WebshopCampaignCode@1000000000 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      WebshopCampaignHeader@1000000001 : Record 5447345;
      WebshopCampaignElement@1000000002 : Record 5447346;
      IsHandled@1000000003 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeDeleteCampaignByCode(IsHandled,WebshopCampaignCode);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        WebshopCampaignElement.SETRANGE("Link-to Header Code",WebshopCampaignCode);
        IF WebshopCampaignElement.FINDSET THEN
          WebshopCampaignElement.DELETEALL(TRUE);

        IF WebshopCampaignHeader.GET(WebshopCampaignCode) THEN
          DeleteCampaignHeader(WebshopCampaignHeader);

      END;
      OnAfterDeleteCampaignByCode(IsHandled,WebshopCampaignCode);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckCampaignImage@1000000199(CampaignHeader@1000000000 : Record 5447345;Field@1000000001 : FieldRef) Ok : Boolean;
    VAR
      ImageFieldName@1000000002 : Text[30];
      InitFieldValue@1000000003 : Boolean;
      CurrRec@1000000004 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000005 : Text[1024];
      LogFile@1000000006 : File;
      RecRef@1000000007 : RecordRef;
      IsHandled@1000000008 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeCheckCampaignImage(IsHandled,CampaignHeader,Field);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Einzelne Webshop Kampagnen-Datei pr?fen

        InitFieldValue := Field.VALUE;
        RecRef.GETTABLE(CampaignHeader);
        Field := RecRef.FIELD(Field.NUMBER);

        CASE Field.NAME OF
          'Icon Condition Items':   ImageFieldName := 'icon_condition_items';
          'Banner Condition Items': ImageFieldName := 'banner_condition_items';
          'Icon Action Items':      ImageFieldName := 'icon_action_items';
          'Banner Action Items':    ImageFieldName := 'banner_action_items';
          'Banner Basket':          ImageFieldName := 'banner_basket';
        END;

        WITH CampaignHeader DO BEGIN
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_campaign_header');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'field',ImageFieldName);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
          GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('check_field_content',CurrRec);
          IF GetRec = '' THEN
            Ok := FALSE
          ELSE IF GetRec = '100' THEN
            Ok := TRUE
          ELSE
            IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
              ERROR(GetRec)
            ELSE BEGIN
              IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path")>0 THEN BEGIN
                LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN BEGIN
                 IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                  ERROR(Error001);
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Connection Log File Path") THEN
                    ERROR(Error002);
                END;
                LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
                LogFile.CLOSE;
                IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                  MESSAGE(GetRec);
              END ELSE
                ERROR(Error003);
            END;
          IF Ok <> InitFieldValue THEN BEGIN
            Field.VALUE(Ok);
            RecRef.MODIFY(TRUE);
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterCheckCampaignImage(IsHandled,CampaignHeader,Field);
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteCampaignImage@1000000200(CampaignHeader@1000000001 : Record 5447345;Field@1000000000 : FieldRef);
    VAR
      ImageFieldName@1000000007 : Text[30];
      InitFieldValue@1000000006 : Boolean;
      CurrRec@1000000005 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000004 : Text[1024];
      LogFile@1000000003 : File;
      RecRef@1000000002 : RecordRef;
      Success@1000000008 : Boolean;
      IsHandled@1000000009 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      OnBeforeDeleteCampaignImage(IsHandled,CampaignHeader,Field);
      IF NOT IsHandled THEN BEGIN

        // Einzelne Webshop Kampagnen-Datei l�schen

        InitFieldValue := Field.VALUE;
        RecRef.GETTABLE(CampaignHeader);
        Field := RecRef.FIELD(Field.NUMBER);

        CASE Field.NAME OF
          'Icon Condition Items':   ImageFieldName := 'icon_condition_items';
          'Banner Condition Items': ImageFieldName := 'banner_condition_items';
          'Icon Action Items':      ImageFieldName := 'icon_action_items';
          'Banner Action Items':    ImageFieldName := 'banner_action_items';
          'Banner Basket':          ImageFieldName := 'banner_basket';
        END;


        WITH CampaignHeader DO BEGIN
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_campaign_header');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[' + ImageFieldName + ']','');

          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);
          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            Success := WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,TRUE)
          ELSE
            Success := WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

          IF Success AND InitFieldValue THEN BEGIN
            Field.VALUE(FALSE);
            RecRef.MODIFY(TRUE);
          END;
        END;

      END;
      OnAfterDeleteCampaignImage(IsHandled,CampaignHeader,Field);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SendReqToMySQL@1000000124(Action@1000000000 : Text;CurrRec@1000000001 : Text) Query : Text;
    VAR
      RegexMatch@1000000002 : DotNet "'System, Version=4.0.0.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b77a5c561934e089'.System.Text.RegularExpressions.Match";
      RegexInput@1000000003 : DotNet "'System, Version=4.0.0.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b77a5c561934e089'.System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex";
      RegexPattern@1000000004 : Text;
      TableName@1000000005 : Text;
      DataName@1000000006 : Text;
      DataValue@1000000007 : Text;
      Counter@1000000008 : Integer;
      Error001@1000000009 : TextConst 'DEU=Kein Tabellenname angegeben.;ENU=No table name specified.';
      Error002@1000000010 : TextConst 'DEU=Keine Daten-Felder angegeben.;ENU=No data-fields specified.';
    BEGIN
      IF Action='update' THEN BEGIN
        RegexPattern := 'table\=[^\&]+';
        RegexMatch := RegexInput.Match(CurrRec,RegexPattern);
        IF RegexMatch.Success THEN BEGIN
          TableName := COPYSTR(RegexMatch.Value(),7);
          TableName := DELSTR(TableName,STRPOS(TableName,']'));
          Query := 'UPDATE `'+TableName+'`';
        END ELSE
          ERROR(Error001);

        RegexPattern := 'data\[[^\]]+\]\=[^\&]+';
        RegexMatch := RegexInput.Match(CurrRec,RegexPattern);
        IF RegexMatch.Success THEN BEGIN
          Counter := 0;
          WHILE (RegexMatch.Success) DO BEGIN
            CLEAR(DataName);
            CLEAR(DataValue);
            DataName := COPYSTR(RegexMatch.Value(),6);
            DataName := DELSTR(DataName,STRPOS(DataName,']'));
            DataValue := COPYSTR(RegexMatch.Value(),(6+STRLEN(DataName)+2));
            IF Counter=0 THEN
              Query += WebshopManagement.URLEncode(' SET `'+DataName+'`=''')+DataValue+WebshopManagement.URLEncode('''')
            ELSE
              Query += WebshopManagement.URLEncode(',`'+DataName+'`=''')+DataValue+WebshopManagement.URLEncode('''');
            RegexMatch := RegexMatch.NextMatch();
          END;
        END ELSE
          ERROR(Error002);

        RegexPattern := 'primary\[[^\]]+\]\=[^\&]+';
        RegexMatch := RegexInput.Match(CurrRec,RegexPattern);
        WHILE (RegexMatch.Success) DO BEGIN

        END;
      END ELSE IF Action='delete' THEN BEGIN

      END ELSE IF Action='instant_delete' THEN BEGIN

      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE RegexMatches@1000000126(RegexInput@1000000001 : DotNet "'System, Version=4.0.0.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b77a5c561934e089'.System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex");
    VAR
      RegexMatch@1000000000 : DotNet "'System, Version=4.0.0.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b77a5c561934e089'.System.Text.RegularExpressions.Match";
    BEGIN
    END;
